definition{
  name: i0041,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The schema is generic. It contains a child-before-parent DAG of closed kernel" (cons "expressions (universe levels, de Bruijn variables, sorts, constants," (cons "applications, lambdas, dependent function types, lets, literals, and" (cons "structure projections), plus declarations for definitions, theorems," (cons "inductives, structures, classes, instances, constructors, and recursors." (cons "Every declaration carries its universe parameters, exact type, applicable" (cons "value or proof, kernel reducibility hints, effective elaborator transparency," (cons "complete structure field and parent registration, class status, exact" (cons "instance priority, attribute kind and synthesis order, and an explicit none," (cons "allow-subset, or exact axiom policy. There is no Atlas-specific node and no" (cons "backend-source field. A declaration's global name is also its component" (cons "identity for a document denotation owned by that native module." (nil String)))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0042,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "Linking rejects a noncanonical schema discriminator, a forward or out-of-range" (cons "DAG edge, a malformed or repeated global name, inconsistent declaration" (cons "metadata, an absent constructor, a missing explicit axiom policy, and any" (cons "resource-limit overrun. A native module contributes all expression and" (cons "declaration nodes to `max_ir_nodes` and contributes its normalized source," (cons "decoded semantic value, and policy data to the project identities." (nil String)))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

      (cons "Both backends traverse this same linked value. The LaTeX backend renders the" (cons "native-environment declaration names, exact types, and definition or proof" (cons "terms as a canonical human-readable core document. The Lean backend" (cons "reconstructs `Lean.Expr` and `Lean.Declaration` values and submits every native" (cons "environment declaration through Lean's checked declaration API; generated" (cons "inductive auxiliaries may be submitted only once but remain named rows in the" (cons "IR and in the verification audit." (nil String))))))))
    ],

}

definition{
  name: i0044,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A lossless environment import may retain a source compiler's private" (cons "self-recursive implementation record with `generated = true`. Such a record" (cons "is not a safe kernel declaration that the native backend can recreate. It is" (cons "accepted only when no native-environment declaration refers to it; it remains" (cons "hashed import provenance and appears in neither generated backend nor the" (cons "axiom audit. Any other generated row without a native owner is rejected. This" (cons "classification is generic and structural: it is not selected by an Atlas" (cons "name, module, or feature. Generated Lean may import only the foundational" (cons "modules listed in the core data and other modules in the generated graph. The" (cons "generic environment-reconstruction runtime may privately import `Lean`, and" (cons "the runtime of a version 2 module also `Lean.Meta.Constructions.SparseCasesOn`" (cons "in full (below); no" (cons "generated module may import an independently authored implementation of the" (cons "declarations being generated." (nil String)))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

  name: i0045,

    arguments: [
      (cons "This form exists for lossless migration of already elaborated formal" (cons "libraries. A one-time conversion implementation can establish byte-exact" (cons "migration evidence, but it is not an input to either backend and is absent from" (cons "the release tree. Once committed, the native `.lex.tex` module is the source of" (cons "coverage and both generated artifacts. Under language 1.2 a module of schema" (cons "`lexlean/core-module/2` is written in the authoring notation (§17.21.8) instead" (cons "of one `\\coredata{…}` argument, and the two read to one module." (nil String))))))))
    ],

  name: i0046,

    arguments: [
      (cons "Language 1.2 additionally accepts the schema `lexlean/core-module/2`" (cons "(`schemas/core-module-v2.schema.json`); languages 1.0 and 1.1 accept only" (cons "`lexlean/core-module/1`, and a module of the later schema in an earlier" (cons "language, or an earlier-schema module carrying any construct below, is" (cons "rejected. The extension exists so that a formal library that elaborates" (cons "against more than the kernel can be migrated without loss: what Lean's checked" (cons "declaration API creates is the declaration and the recursor of an inductive" (cons "type, and everything else the elaborator derives, and the environment" (cons "extensions that make a later `import` of the library behave as the original," (cons "are data of the module, not conventions of a backend." (nil String)))))))))))
    ],

definition{
  name: i0047,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "- An annotation node `{\"k\":\"d\"}` wraps a node and carries an ordered list of" (cons "  named entries whose values are a Boolean, a natural number, an integer, a" (cons "  string, or a name." (cons "- A global name may carry numeric components, written `#` followed by a" (cons "  canonical decimal numeral. Every other component is a nonempty string of" (cons "  non-control characters without a dot, in which a backslash introduces" (cons "  exactly `\\\\` (a backslash), `\\d` (a dot), or `\\h` (a leading `#`), so that" (cons "  the components of a name round-trip exactly and a name the pinned Lean" (cons "  prints with guillemets is not a different name." (cons "- A definition may state a safety, `partial` or `unsafe`, and the group of" (cons "  definitions it was submitted with; a group is submitted to the kernel in one" (cons "  call, in the order of the data. A definition, theorem, or inductive may state" (cons "  that it is compiled, so that the code generator's state for it exists; and" (cons "  the reducibility tags, `protected`, `aux_recursor`, `elab_as_elim`, `defeq`," (cons "  `backward_defeq`, `recursive`, and `sparse_cases_on`, in that order and" (cons "  without repetition." (cons "- A definition may state the extension state of the structure it is a" (cons "  projection of, of a `match` it is the matcher of, of a no-confusion" (cons "  construction, and of its equational-lemma record; a theorem may state its" (cons "  simplification-set registration. A definition may state a compiler inlining" (cons "  attribute (`inline`, `noinline`, `macro_inline`, `inline_if_reduce`," (cons "  `always_inline`), and a theorem a `grind` attribute of one of the closed" (cons "  kinds `eq_lhs`, `eq_rhs`, `eq_both`, `eq_bwd`, `fwd`, `bwd`, `left_right`," (cons "  `right_left`, and `default`, which the decoder registers with the pinned" (cons "  Lean's own attribute function and no priorities." (cons "- A definition may state that it is a sparse `casesOn`, a `casesOn` with arms" (cons "  for only some constructors of a type, by the type, the constructors that" (cons "  have an arm, the position of the major premise, and the arity. The pinned" (cons "  Lean keeps that record in an extension no code outside its own construction" (cons "  can write, and equation-lemma generation for a `match` that uses the" (cons "  constant reads it, so the decoder does not submit such a row: it makes the" (cons "  constant with the elaborator's construction from the type and the" (cons "  constructors, in a realization of the type as the elaborator makes each one" (cons "  (two constants of one module may be the same construction, which a shared" (cons "  cache would otherwise return once), numbered and visible as the row's name" (cons "  shows, and refuses the module unless the constant, its name, and its record" (cons "  are those the row states. It then enters the constant in the elaborator's" (cons "  cache of sparse `casesOn`, under the exported and the private key unless" (cons "  the key is taken, so that a later construction that needs the same one" (cons "  finds it, as it did when the elaborator made the module; each chunk first" (cons "  drops from the cache the runtime's own constants, which are not the" (cons "  module's. The cache is private to its Lean module, which the runtime" (cons "  therefore imports in full. A certificate rendered outside the module" (cons "  system (§17.17) cannot import a module in full; it states theorems only," (cons "  no sparse `casesOn` and no derived row, and its runtime leaves the cache" (cons "  alone." (cons "- A row whose name the pinned Lean reserves (`Lean.isReservedName`: the" (cons "  equations and splitter of a matcher, the equations and unfolding theorem" (cons "  of a definition, the `else_eq` of a sparse `casesOn`, and the other names" (cons "  a registered reserved-name action makes) is not submitted: the decoder runs" (cons "  the action the pinned Lean registers for the name and refuses the module" (cons "  unless the action makes the constant and the constant has the row's kind," (cons "  levels, type, and value. A row whose constant an earlier action of the" (cons "  module already made is checked the same way. So that each action makes what" (cons "  the elaborator made, the state it reads is installed as each row is added," (cons "  not after the module: the `recursive` tag, the transparency, the matcher" (cons "  record, the equational-lemma record, and the row's realization context," (cons "  which for a definition with an equational-lemma record is opened once its" (cons "  smart-unfolding row (`_sunfold`) is in. definition{

(cons "- A row may be *derived* (`\"derived\": true`): it states its name, its kind" (cons "  (`theorem`, `definition`, or `abbrev`), its transparency and axiom policy," (cons "  and the extension state it installs (the tags, the compiler inlining" (cons "  attribute, the compiled flag, and the other members of version 2 that are" (cons "  not a term), and for a sparse `casesOn` its record (the type, the" (cons "  constructors that have an arm, the position of the major premise, and the" (cons "  arity), and it states no universe parameters, type, value, reducibility" (cons "  metadata, group, or safety, which the construction decides. The decoder" (cons "  makes the constant at the row's place in the order with the construction" (cons "  the row names, the action the pinned Lean registers for a reserved name or" (cons "  the sparse `casesOn` construction above, installs the state the row" (cons "  states, and refuses the module unless the name is one the pinned Lean" (cons "  reserves or the row states a sparse record, the construction makes the" (cons "  name, and the constant is a theorem when the row is a theorem, an" (cons "  abbreviation (its hint is `abbrev`) when the row is an `abbrev`, and" (cons "  another definition when it is a `definition`. What the construction makes" (cons "  is therefore never stored, so it cannot go stale when a type of the module" (cons "  changes, and an authored proof that needs another equation fails to check" (cons "  and is named. A sparse record's constructors are checked" (cons "  against the type: each is a constructor of it, and none is named twice (the" (cons "  record lists them in the order of the arms, not of the type). Every chunk" (cons "  carries the names of the module's sparse `casesOn` rows, and the decoder refuses" (cons "  a chunk whose construction made a sparse `casesOn` that the module does not" (cons "  state, so a row cannot be left out for the construction to supply. The last" (cons "  chunk of a module also carries every row's name and the derived sparse rows," (cons "  and the decoder refuses the module unless each derived sparse `casesOn` is" (cons "  among the constants that the type or value of some row refers to, those of a" (cons "  derived row (a splitter) being the term the construction made. That is the" (cons "  whole of what is checked of a derived row's content: the kernel has checked" (cons "  every term that uses it, and a recipe that selects other valid constructors" (cons "  of the type, where the use accepts the constant, is not distinguished. A derived" (cons "  row names no term, so the order of emission is the" (cons "  data's: a derived row follows the derived row before it in the module," (cons "  the rows its name extends (not a sparse `casesOn`, which is named under" (cons "  the splitter whose construction needs it and so comes first), and the" (cons "  type and the constructors its record names. A certificate cites no" (cons "  derived row, since no type of it is stated. The stored form remains valid:" (cons "  a row that states its type and value is checked as above (`SM-33`), so a" (cons "  library migrated before the derived form decodes unchanged." definition{
  name: i0053,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The closed declaration variants are structure, class, explicit instance," (cons "finite nonrecursive inductive, definition, and theorem. Structures, classes," (cons "and inductives carry ordered explicit type parameters, fields, and positional" (cons "constructors; their schema-level `parameters` array is required to be empty so" (cons "no value index can be accepted without a corresponding closed type/application" (cons "IR. Definitions and theorems carry ordered explicit value parameters." (cons "Instances have exactly priority 1000," (cons "target a prior document class, carry the exact ordered field set, and are" (cons "unique for one class/type argument tuple. Definitions are nonrecursive unless" (cons "they name exactly one `Nat`, `List`, or document-inductive decreasing argument" (cons "and their body is a top-level exhaustive match on that argument. A recursive" (cons "self-call is legal only inside a match branch and passes a pattern-bound" (cons "structural subvalue in that argument position. Mutual recursion, forward references, missing or mixed" (cons "cases, recursive inductive payloads, and ambiguous instances are rejected in" (cons "linking. An `instance_value` term names its required class/type tuple and the" (cons "resolved prior instance. Linking recomputes that unique resolution and rejects" (cons "a missing, different, forward, self-referential, or cyclic instance instead of" (cons "delegating synthesis to Lean." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0054,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The closed type variants include `Type`, type parameters, `Nat`, `Bool`," (cons "`Prop`, `Unit`, mathematical unbounded `Int`, the distinct fixed-width types" (cons "`Int8`, `Int16`, `Int32`, `Int64`, `UInt8`, `UInt16`, `UInt32`, and `UInt64`," (cons "UTF-8 `String`, immutable `Bytes`, three-way `Ordering`, `Option`, `Result`," (cons "`List`, and document-named types. `Option` and `Result` carry their complete" (cons "closed value/error types. They are not aliases for a sentinel, exception, or" (cons "host-width integer. JSON decoding guarantees that a `String` literal is valid" (cons "UTF-8. Generated Lean strings escape quotes, backslashes, newline, carriage" (cons "return and tab; other control scalars use Lean's four-digit Unicode escapes." (cons "Other Unicode scalars remain literal UTF-8, without normalization or changes" (cons "to literal escape-shaped data. Source-document restrictions remain unchanged." (cons "Digit runs within `semanticdata` JSON string values are preserved as" (cons "string bytes and are never reclassified as source numerals; this permits exact" (cons "content identities whose first hexadecimal digit is zero. JSON number tokens" (cons "remain subject to the JSON decoder's grammar. A byte literal is an even-length" (cons "lower-case hexadecimal string." (cons "Generated Lean forbids line and block comment tokens in syntax, while the same" (cons "byte sequences inside a generated Lean string literal remain exact string" (cons "data." (cons "Integer literals use one canonical base-ten spelling: zero is `0`; any other" (cons "value has no leading zero; only signed representations admit a leading minus;" (cons "and every fixed-width literal is rejected unless it is within the exact range" (cons "of its named type. `Int` is not range-limited or lowered through a host-sized" (cons "integer." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

They additionally contain one typed `primitive` node whose" (cons "`operation`, ordered `arguments`, and explicit `result` select exactly one of:" (cons "`subtract`, `multiply`, `quotient`, `remainder`, `negate`, `checked_convert`," (cons "`checked_add`, `checked_subtract`, `checked_multiply`, `checked_negate`," (cons "`checked_quotient`, `bit_and`, `bit_or`, `bit_xor`, `bit_not`, `shift_left`," (cons "`shift_right`, `append`, `length`, `index`, `slice`, `utf8_encode`," (cons "`utf8_decode`, `compare_bytes`, `equal`, `split_exact`, `join`," (cons "`parse_decimal`, or `format_decimal`. definition{
  name: i0056,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "Unbounded subtraction and multiplication accept only equal `Nat` or `Int`" (cons "operands; unbounded negation accepts `Int`. Quotient and remainder accept two" (cons "equal `Nat` or `Int` operands and an explicit same-typed zero-divisor result." (cons "Fixed-width conversion produces `Option` of the explicit target type. Fixed" (cons "addition, subtraction, multiplication, signed negation, and quotient produce" (cons "`Option` and return none for overflow or, for quotient, a zero divisor." (cons "Bitwise operations preserve one fixed-width type. A shift amount is `UInt32`," (cons "and the result is none unless it is less than the width. Append preserves an" (cons "equal list or byte type. Length returns `Nat`; index and slice return `Option`." (cons "UTF-8 decode returns `Option String`; byte comparison is unsigned" (cons "lexicographic `Ordering`. `equal` accepts two operands of exactly the same" (cons "`Nat`, `Bool`, fixed-width integer, `String`, `Bytes`, or `Ordering` type and" (cons "uses that type's fixed decidable equality without coercion; mathematical" (cons "`Int`, containers, and document types are rejected. Exact split rejects an empty delimiter or more than" (cons "the explicit `UInt32` maximum number of fields. Join is its deterministic" (cons "delimiter intercalation. Decimal parsing accepts a value exactly when parsing," (cons "range checking, and formatting reproduce the input byte-for-byte; formatting" (cons "is canonical base ten. No operation wraps, saturates, defaults a numeric type," (cons "uses a host exception, or delegates its meaning to a backend-specific payload." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },

}

definition{

      (cons "The closed proof variants are reflexivity, decidable Boolean bridging," (cons "`simp only` over a nonempty sorted unique set of document definitions," (cons "induction hypotheses, and the fixed `Bool.and_eq_true`, `Nat.beq_eq`, or" (cons "`Nat.blt_eq` Boolean-to-proposition bridge lemmas; constructor" (cons "congruence, cases, and structural induction. Each has one fixed proof-term or" (cons "tactic lowering and one canonical LaTeX description; users cannot supply a" (cons "tactic name or argument string. Cases and induction require a `Nat`, `List`," (cons "or document-inductive local; their constructor set and pattern-binder arities" (cons "are checked exactly before rendering. Induction may carry a strictly sorted," (cons "unique list of other in-scope theorem parameters to generalize; the scrutinee" (cons "cannot be generalized. Every simplification name resolves" (cons "to one of those closed choices. An axiom-free `boolean_reflection` proof is" (cons "available only in two validated" (cons "generic shapes: structural `List Nat` recursion that reflects `Nat.beq` or" (cons "`Nat.blt` into an independently recursive proposition, and a finite structure" (cons "whose complete ordered `Nat` field set is reflected from right-associated" (cons "Boolean conjunction into propositional conjunction. The proof payload names" (cons "the prior Boolean and proposition definitions and the in-scope locals; record" (cons "expected values are canonical Nat literals. The backend emits fixed natural" (cons "deduction using primitive Nat direction lemmas and locally proved Boolean" (cons "conjunction/reflexivity bridges. It does not invoke `propext` or a general" (cons "tactic." (cons "An `apply` proof names one prior theorem and supplies exactly its ordered," (cons "type-checked semantic arguments; it lowers to one `exact` application and" (cons "cannot carry a proof-term or tactic string." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))

(cons "Structures, classes, instances, and inductives always use the exact empty set." (cons "Computational definitions may declare a nonempty exact policy only to account" (cons "for trusted dependencies observed in their fixed Lean implementation; this" (cons "does not authorize source axioms, proof holes, raw Lean, or an unchecked" (cons "backend. Verification rejects a missing, additional, or unlisted observed" (cons "axiom for every declaration and records the declared and observed sets in the" (cons "attestation. A theorem that unfolds or otherwise depends on a computational" (cons "definition inherits every dependency Lean observes; separating declaration" (cons "policies never erases or masks that dependency." The constructs that exist only in language 1.2 are listed in the" (cons "closed table below; each is rejected under language 1.1 before either" (cons "backend runs." (nil String))))))))

definition{
  name: i0066,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "| 1.2-only construct | Schema | Meaning |" (cons "|---|---|---|" (cons "| `let` term | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | A typed, nonrecursive local definition `{\"binder\":{\"name\":...,\"type\":...},\"kind\":\"let\",\"value\":...,\"body\":...}`. The value is checked in the enclosing scope and must have exactly the binder type; the binder is in scope only in `body`, may not shadow any local, and is never treated as a structurally smaller recursive argument. It lowers to the Lean term `(let x : T := v; b)` and contributes its binder type, value, and body to `max_ir_nodes`. |" (cons "| `product` type | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"product\",\"left\":T,\"right\":U}`, the binary product of two closed types, lowered to `(Prod (T) (U))`. |" (cons "| `pair`, `first`, `second` terms | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"pair\",\"left\":a,\"right\":b}` has the product of its component types and lowers to `(a, b)`; `first` and `second` require a product operand and lower to `(v).1` and `(v).2`. A `match` on a product has exactly one `Prod.mk` branch with two binders. |" (cons "| recursive inductive | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | A constructor field may mention the inductive being declared, under the recursive-data rules below. |" (cons "| `mutual` inductive label | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `\"mutual\":\"Label\"` on an inductive places it in a mutual group under the recursive-data rules below. |" (cons "| `function` type | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"function\",\"parameters\":[T...],\"result\":U}` with at least one parameter, lowered to `((T1) -> ... -> (U))`. |" (cons "| `lambda`, `apply`, `function_ref` terms | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | Anonymous functions with explicit captures, full application of a function value, and a document definition as a function value, under the higher-order rules below. |" (cons "| generic definitions and theorems | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `type_parameters` on a definition or theorem and `type_arguments` on a `call`, a `function_ref`, or an `apply` proof, under the higher-order rules below. |" (cons "| `executable` definitions | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `\"executable\":true` asserts production eligibility under the higher-order rules below. |" (cons "| `mutual` definition label | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `\"mutual\":\"Label\"` on a structurally recursive definition places it in a mutual definition group under the recursion rules below. |" (cons "| `termination` evidence | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"measure\":m,\"evidence\":[theorem...]}` on a definition declares well-founded recursion under the recursion rules below. |" (cons "| `linear_arithmetic` proof | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"linear_arithmetic\"}`, optionally with `definitions`, a strictly sorted list of prior document definitions and no other member. It lowers to one fixed script (*Fold laws* below): `intros`; with definitions, `subst_vars`; one `simp only [...] at *` over, in order, the named definitions, the Boolean-comparison bridges `← Bool.not_eq_true, Bool.and_eq_true, Bool.or_eq_true, Bool.not_eq_true', and_true, true_and, Option.some.injEq, Nat.beq_eq, Nat.blt_eq, Nat.ble_eq`, the runtime definitions `LexLeanRuntime.subtract, LexLeanRuntime.multiply, LexLeanRuntime.negate, LexLeanRuntime.equal`, the Lean lemmas `beq_iff_eq, decide_eq_true_eq, decide_eq_false_iff_not, Nat.add_assoc, Int.add_assoc, Int.neg_neg, Int.emod_neg, Int.neg_zero, ↓reduceIte`, the registered rewrite set of the fold laws, and for each fixed-width type `T` (`UInt8`, `UInt16`, `UInt32`, `UInt64` read by `toNat`; `Int8`, `Int16`, `Int32`, `Int64` read by `toInt`) first `T.le_iff_v_le, T.lt_iff_v_lt` and then `T.reduceToNat` or `T.reduceToInt` and `← T.v_inj`; a second `simp only [...] at *` over `UInt8.toNat` … `Int64.toInt` and `BitVec.toInt_eq_toNat_cond`, which reads every remaining fixed-width value as the bounded natural number of its bits; `all_goals (repeat' split)`, which splits every `if` and `match` left in the goal; and `all_goals omega`. Each `simp only` is under `try` with the unused-argument linter off, so a goal the simplifier already closed leaves nothing for `omega`. With definitions, hypotheses equating a local with a constructor value are substituted and a measure over them unfolds to linear arithmetic. A module with this proof imports `LexLeanRuntime.Laws`. |" (cons "| `law` proof | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"law\",\"name\":N,\"type_arguments\":[T...],\"arguments\":[...],\"premises\":[theorem...]}` (`type_arguments` and `premises` omitted when empty): exact application of the registered fold law `N` (*Fold laws* below) to explicit type arguments, arguments, and prior theorems discharging its premises in order. Linking checks the counts, the type arguments, each argument against the registered parameter type, and that each premise names a prior theorem; Lean checks the statement. It lowers to `exact @L (T1) … (a1) … p1 …`, `L` the law's Lean name, and a module with it imports `LexLeanRuntime.Laws`. |" (cons "| integer order | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | The order propositions `le` and `lt`, which language 1.1 types over `Nat` only, take two operands of one integer type: `Nat`, `Int`, or one fixed-width type. They lower to Lean's `<=` and `<` on that type, which on a fixed-width type is the order of its mathematical value. |" (cons "| `map`, `set` types | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"map\",\"key\":K,\"value\":V}` and `{\"kind\":\"set\",\"element\":K}` over an ordered key type, under the collection rules below. |" (cons "| `map_literal`, `set_literal`, `graph_literal` terms | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | Literal collections of literal keys, canonicalized in linking under the collection rules below. |" (cons "| collection primitives | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | The `primitive` operations `map_insert`, `map_remove`, `map_lookup`, `map_contains`, `map_size`, `map_keys`, `map_values`, `map_entries`, `map_fold`, `set_insert`, `set_remove`, `set_contains`, `set_size`, `set_elements`, `set_union`, `set_intersection`, `set_difference`, `set_fold`, `list_fold`, `iterate`, `iterate_until`, `graph_successors`, `graph_reachable`, and `graph_topological`, under the collection rules below. |" (cons "| `less_than` primitive | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"primitive\",\"operation\":\"less_than\",\"arguments\":[a,b],\"result\":{\"kind\":\"bool\"}}` over two values of one ordered key type (rule 1 under *Collections and state threading*): `true` exactly when `a` precedes `b` in that type's fixed order. It lowers to `LexLeanCollections.lessThan`. |" (cons "| `to_int`, `to_nat` primitives | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"primitive\",\"operation\":\"to_int\",\"arguments\":[v],\"result\":{\"kind\":\"int\"}}` over any fixed-width integer `v` (`int8`..`int64`, `uint8`..`uint64`), and `{\"kind\":\"primitive\",\"operation\":\"to_nat\",\"arguments\":[v],\"result\":{\"kind\":\"nat\"}}` over any unsigned fixed-width integer `v` (`uint8`..`uint64`), under the widening-conversion rules below. |" (cons "| `artifact` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | A typed constant decoded from content-addressed project bytes, under the model rules below. |" (cons "| `contract` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | Typed input, output, and optional state interfaces, proposition-valued predicates, and their runtime validators, under the model rules below. |" (cons "| `realization` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | A deterministic, rule, statistical, neural, or composite denotation of an interface, under the model rules below. |" (cons "| `evidence` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | Closed claims about a realization against a contract, each discharged by a theorem, under the model rules below. |" (cons "| `model` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | A contract bound to a realization of its interface, with evidence and entry theorems, under the model rules below. |" (cons "| `checked_apply` term | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"checked_apply\",\"model\":M,\"type_arguments\":[T...],\"arguments\":[...],\"checks\":[...]}`, a model application that runs the named validators, under the model rules below. |" (cons "| `contract_violation` type | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"contract_violation\"}`, the closed type of a validator refusal, with the four nullary constructors `ContractViolation.precondition`, `.input_invariant`, `.postcondition`, and `.output_invariant`, under the model rules below. |" (cons "| `logic` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | A state type with a relation, an optional invariant, and an optional ranking, under the reasoning rules below. |" (cons "| `inference_rule` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | A guarded transition of a logic's state with exact soundness and progress theorems, under the reasoning rules below. |" (cons "| `verifier` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | An executable check of a candidate answer, sound (and optionally complete) for a specification, under the reasoning rules below. |" (cons "| `reasoner` declaration | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | A bounded forward, search, or generate-and-verify engine with a verified or proved-correct answer, under the reasoning rules below. |" (cons "| `reasoning_failure` type | `lexlean/semantic-module/2` | `{\"kind\":\"reasoning_failure\"}`, the closed type of a reasoner's missing answer, with the four nullary constructors `ReasoningFailure.exhausted`, `.unsolved`, `.rejected`, and `.invalid_step`, under the reasoning rules below. |" (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0069,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **Groups.** A standalone inductive is a group of one. Inductives carrying" (cons "   the same `mutual` label form one group: the members are contiguous in" (cons "   declaration order, there are at least two, they declare identical ordered" (cons "   type parameters, and each has at least one constructor and no value" (cons "   parameters. A member may mention any member of its group; every other" (cons "   reference is to an earlier declaration. No language-1.2 declaration is named `Bool`, `List`, `Nat`, `Option`," (cons "   `Prod`, or `Result`: a local reference to a built-in constructor carries no" (cons "   module, so such a declaration would make it ambiguous." (cons "2. **Uniformity.** Every mention of a group member inside a constructor field" (cons "   applies it to exactly the declared type parameters, in order." (cons "3. **Strict positivity.** A group member occurs in a field only as the whole" (cons "   field type (a *direct* occurrence) or nested under `List`, `Option`," (cons "   `Result`, or a product. A group member inside the type arguments of" (cons "   another document type is rejected, because the positivity of that type's" (cons "   parameters is not part of its closed contract." (cons "4. **Inhabitation.** The least set of inhabited members is computed by" (cons "   fixed point: a member is inhabited when one of its constructors has only" (cons "   constructible fields. `List` and `Option` are always constructible, a" (cons "   `Result` when either side is, a product when both are, a group member" (cons "   when it is already inhabited, and every other accepted type always. A" (cons "   member outside the fixed point is an uninhabited recursive cycle." (cons "5. **Structures** and classes remain nonrecursive: a field that mentions its" (cons "   own structure or class is rejected; recursive data is an inductive." (cons "6. **Structural recursion.** When a recursive definition matches its" (cons "   decreasing argument, the structurally smaller pattern binders are the" (cons "   `List.cons` tail, the `Nat.succ` predecessor, and every field of a" (cons "   document constructor that is a direct occurrence of the matched" (cons "   inductive itself. A nested occurrence, or a member of another type in a" (cons "   mutual group, is not smaller under this rule. Imported recursive" (cons "   inductives keep the same smaller positions. A standalone recursive" (cons "   definition decreases only on `Nat`, `List`, or a self-recursive inductive" (cons "   with no nested occurrence and no mutual group, exactly the types" (cons "   `induction` accepts: a nested or mutual type has no single structural" (cons "   eliminator for one function." (cons "7. **Induction.** `induction` applies to a self-recursive inductive with no" (cons "   nested occurrence and no mutual group, and binds the constructor fields" (cons "   followed by one hypothesis per direct recursive field, in field order." (cons "   `cases` applies to every inductive." (cons "8. **Lowering.** Each inductive lowers to one `public inductive` with" (cons "   positional `(_ : T)` fields; each mutual group lowers to one" (cons "   `mutual ... end` block. A constructor's identity is its owner and name," (cons "   `Owner.ctor`, qualified by its module when imported; constructor names are" (cons "   unique within their owner and lowered in declaration order. The canonical" (cons "   LaTeX of a `lexlean/semantic-module/2` module additionally lists the type" (cons "   parameters of every parameterized inductive, structure, or class, every" (cons "   constructor with its field types, the mutual group label, and every" (cons "   structure or class field; language-1.1 documents keep their bytes." (cons "9. **Accounting.** Every constructor field type is charged recursively to" (cons "   `max_ir_nodes`, and a `lexlean/semantic-module/2` module is also charged" (cons "   one node per type parameter, per constructor, and per mutual group label," (cons "   so no part of a data declaration is free; language-1.1 modules keep their" (cons "   historical count." (cons "10. **Binder hygiene.** The backend refers to the module's own declarations," (cons "    to the built-in names it emits (`And`, `BitVec`, `Bool`, `ByteArray`," (cons "    `Except`, `Iff`, `Int`, `Int8`..`Int64`, `LexLeanCollections`," (cons "    `LexLeanLaws`, `LexLeanModels`, `LexLeanReasoning`, `LexLeanRuntime`," (cons "    `List`, `Nat`, `Option`, `Ordering`, `Prod`, `Prop`, `Result`, `String`," (cons "    `Type`, `UInt8`..`UInt64`, `Unit`, `and_congr`, `and_true`, `beq_iff_eq`," (cons "    `congr`, `decide`, `decide_eq_false_iff_not`, `decide_eq_true_eq`," (cons "    `false`, `id`, `reduceIte`, `rfl`, `true`, `true_and`), and to every imported declaration through" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0070,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "    the project's module prefix, all without qualification (a byte literal" (cons "    instead qualifies `_root_.ByteArray.mk` and `_root_.UInt8.ofNat (nat_lit n)`" (cons "    from the root and binds literal-local `let __byteN` variables whose `__`" (cons "    prefix no source binder can spell). In a" (cons "    `lexlean/semantic-module/2` module no binder (a type parameter, a value" (cons "    parameter, or a pattern, `let`, quantifier, lambda, or proof binder) is" (cons "    spelled like a declaration of the module, one of those built-in names, or" (cons "    the first segment of the module prefix, because Lean would resolve the" (cons "    reference to the binder after linking has accepted the module; and no" (cons "    declaration (a definition, theorem, structure, class, inductive, or" (cons "    instance) is spelled like one of those built-in names, because a" (cons "    declaration `Int` is the `Int` of every later signature of its module" (cons "    (`error: type expected, got (Int : Nat -> Nat)`), and one named `true` is" (cons "    the `true` of every Boolean literal. Quoting cannot prevent either, since" (cons "    Lean resolves the name and not its spelling, so linking refuses them:" (cons "    `LLT4001`, fixtures `declaration-lean-name` (a definition `Int`) and" (cons "    `binder-bool-literal-name` (a parameter `true`), and `conformance_df_12`" (cons "    for each name and each kind of declaration. The other names the backend" (cons "    writes, such as the owners of the built-in constructors (`Bool`, `List`," (cons "    `Nat`, `Option`, `Prod`, `Result`, `ContractViolation`), keep their own" (cons "    refusal. A name spelled like a word the audit of generated Lean forbids" (cons "    (`native_decide`, `IO`) is not captured by anything and is valid for a" (cons "    declaration, a field, a constructor, and a parameter: the backend quotes it" (cons "    (`«native_decide»`), as do the certificates and the audit module that" (cons "    prints its axioms (`#print axioms Production.Main.«native_decide»`)." (cons "    A module is a namespace of the project that Lean searches before the root" (cons "    from every module of it, and so is every prefix of a dotted module name" (cons "    (`Sub.Nat` declares in `Prefix.Sub.Nat`, which Lean searches from" (cons "    `Prefix.Sub.Other`), so a module any segment of whose name is spelled like a" (cons "    namespace the generated code writes qualified (`Nat`, `List`, `Option`," (cons "    `Bool`, `String`, ..., the runtime classes `Appendable`, `Key`, ..., and the" (cons "    roots this compiler ships) would capture it (`module Nat` with a function" (cons "    `blt`: `Function expected at Nat.blt`, also in the modules that import it;" (cons "    `Sub.Nat` the same in `Sub.Other`): `LLT4001`, fixtures" (cons "    `module-name-lean-namespace`, `module-name-dotted-last`, and" (cons "    `module-name-dotted-first`; `conformance_sp_09` reads the" (cons "    namespaces the corpora's generated modules write qualified off them and" (cons "    requires that each is refused. A constructor or a field is spelled like no" (cons "    member Lean declares for the type (`rec`, `recOn`, `casesOn`, `noConfusion`," (cons "    `noConfusionType`, `ctorElim`, `ctorElimType`, `ctorIdx`, `toCtorIdx`," (cons "    `below`, `brecOn`, their numbered forms `rec_1`, ..., and, for a field," (cons "    `mk`), which would be declared twice (`constant has already been" (cons "    declared`): `LLT4001`, fixture `member-generated-name`, and the set is the" (cons "    one the pinned Lean declares for sample inductives and structures, which" (cons "    `conformance_df_12` lists. A parameter named like a nullary constructor of" (cons "    its own type (`zero : nat`, `none`, `red`) is valid, the backend writing" (cons "    the constructors qualified; the module silences Lean's" (cons "    `linter.constructorNameAsVariable`, which reports it. A pattern binder is" (cons "    not a parameter: Lean reads a bare name in a pattern as the constructor it" (cons "    resolves to, so a binder `none` in the branch `Option.some` of an `option" (cons "    (option nat)` matches only `some none` (`Missing cases`). The constructors" (cons "    Lean resolves without a namespace are `none`, `some`, `isTrue`, `isFalse`," (cons "    `true`, and `false` (read off the pinned Lean by `conformance_df_12`), and a" (cons "    pattern binder of any of these names is `LLT4001` at `check`, fixture" (cons "    `binder-pattern-constructor-name`; every other binder named like a" (cons "    constructor (`zero`, `red`, `nil`) is a variable in a pattern too. The" (cons "    language-1.1 contract is frozen and does not carry this rule." (cons "11. **Source maps.** In a `lexlean/semantic-module/2` module every" (cons "    declaration is its own mapping node in both artifacts, relating its" (cons "    generated Lean and LaTeX to exactly its object in the source; the" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0073,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **Generic declarations.** A definition or theorem may declare ordered," (cons "   unique `type_parameters`; they lower to explicit `(T : Type)` binders" (cons "   before the value parameters. Every `call`, `function_ref`, and theorem" (cons "   `apply` supplies exactly the callee's type arguments, explicitly: there is" (cons "   no inference, so no use is ambiguous. Every type written in a" (cons "   definition's parameters, result, or body, or in a theorem's statement or" (cons "   proof arguments, mentions only declared type parameters; this also holds" (cons "   for language-1.1 definitions, whose scope is empty. A language-1.2 type" (cons "   parameter obeys binder hygiene (rule 10 of the recursive-data rules: no" (cons "   declaration of the module, built-in name, or module prefix root), and is" (cons "   moreover not spelled like any value binder of the same declaration. An" (cons "   imported document type is referred to through the module prefix, so a" (cons "   type parameter of its spelling captures nothing. A type parameter that its declaration" (cons "   mentions nowhere (no parameter, result, body, statement, or proof type)" (cons "   lowers as `(_T : Type)`, so Lean's unused-variable linter stays silent. An" (cons "   explicit type argument instantiates a `(T : Type)` binder and so never" (cons "   mentions the universe `Type`." (cons "2. **No polymorphic recursion.** A recursive call passes exactly the" (cons "   definition's own type parameters, in order (the `polymorphic-recursion`" (cons "   negative fixture). Consequently a closed instantiation of a definition" (cons "   reaches only finitely many instantiations: a recursive call repeats the" (cons "   current one, and every other call is to an earlier definition at type" (cons "   arguments built from the caller's. This is the monomorphization rule." (cons "   LexLean emits the generic definitions with explicit `(T : Type)` binders" (cons "   and the explicit type arguments of every use; it does not emit" (cons "   specialized copies, and no claim here depends on one." (cons "3. **Lambdas.** A `lambda` binds at least one typed parameter and lists its" (cons "   `captures`: strictly sorted, unique enclosing locals that are exactly the" (cons "   free locals of its body. Only the captures and the parameters are visible" (cons "   in the body, and a parameter may not shadow any enclosing local. A" (cons "   recursive definition may not call or reference itself inside a lambda." (cons "   A lambda lowers to `(fun (x : T) ... => body)`." (cons "4. **Application.** `apply` fully applies a function-typed value: the" (cons "   argument count equals the function type's parameter count and each" (cons "   argument has the parameter's type; a function value with no statically" (cons "   known function type is not applied. It lowers to `(f (a) ... )`." (cons "   `function_ref` names a prior definition with at least one value" (cons "   parameter at explicit type arguments and has its function type." (cons "5. **Identity.** Binder names are part of the semantic value and therefore" (cons "   of every source and semantic identity. A language-1.2 snapshot also" (cons "   records, for each source definition (and, in an elaboration, each" (cons "   elaborated definition), `alpha_id`: the SHA-256 of the canonical" (cons "   JSON of the definition after renaming its type parameters to `T0, T1, ...`" (cons "   and its value parameters and every term binder to `_0, _1, ...` in" (cons "   evaluation order (the parameters, then the body's subterms as they are" (cons "   evaluated: an application's function before its arguments, a" (cons "   conditional's condition, then branch, and else branch, operands left to" (cons "   right), each binder scoped to its own subterm, with every lambda's" (cons "   renamed captures sorted again; the order does not depend on how the" (cons "   definition serializes. Alpha-equivalent definitions share it; any" (cons "   other change alters it." (cons "6. **Executable closures.** Higher-order values are formal by default. A" (cons "   definition marked `\"executable\":true` is admitted only when every" (cons "   closure it forms is non-escaping and second-class. `executable` is a" (cons "   precondition of production eligibility, never production eligibility" (cons "   itself, which is decided per root and target by §17.13. A type *holds a" (cons "   function* when it is a function type, or a container, product, or" (cons "   document type that holds one through its type arguments or, transitively," (cons "   through the field types of its declaration. Its result type and every" (cons "   non-function parameter type hold no function; no type written in its body" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0074,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   (a type argument, a `let`, `nil`, or lambda parameter type, a primitive" (cons "   result type) holds one, so no data value in it carries a closure; a" (cons "   function-typed parameter takes and returns no function and occurs only" (cons "   where a closure may; a lambda or" (cons "   function reference occurs only as a direct argument to a function-typed" (cons "   parameter of an executable callee, or as the function of an `apply`; the" (cons "   function of an `apply` is a parameter, a lambda, or a function reference," (cons "   never a projection or other computed value; no `let` binds a function;" (cons "   and every called or referenced definition is itself executable (or the" (cons "   definition itself, recursively). Any violation fails closed in linking;" (cons "   nothing is silently lowered." (cons "7. **Accounting.** Function types, lambda parameter types, type arguments," (cons "   and every subterm are charged to `max_ir_nodes`. Recursive occurrences of" (cons "   an inductive group under a function type are rejected by positivity." (nil String)))))))))))))))
    ],

definition{
  name: i0077,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **Structural recursion** of a single definition follows the" (cons "   recursive-data rule 6 above." (cons "2. **Mutual groups.** Definitions carrying the same `mutual` label form one" (cons "   group: contiguous, at least two members, identical type parameters, and" (cons "   either every member structurally recursive (`recursive_argument`, a" (cons "   top-level match on it, no `termination`) or every member well-founded" (cons "   (rule 3); a group mixing the two is rejected. The *recursion call graph*" (cons "   of a group has an edge from each member to every member its body calls" (cons "   or references; every member calls into the group and the graph is" (cons "   strongly connected, so no member is outside the mutual recursion. For a" (cons "   structural group: Every member's decreasing type belongs to one *recursive" (cons "   family*: the naturals; the lists of one element type; or one inductive" (cons "   group at one instantiation of its type parameters, together with `List`" (cons "   or `Option` of its members exactly when the group's constructors nest a" (cons "   member under that container. In a member's" (cons "   top-level match, every pattern binder whose type belongs to the family is" (cons "   structurally smaller, and every call to a group member passes a smaller" (cons "   binder in that member's decreasing position. Members see each other; the" (cons "   recursion call graph is exactly these calls. A group lowers to one" (cons "   `mutual ... end` block whose members each declare" (cons "   `termination_by structural x1 ... xn => d`, so Lean must confirm the same" (cons "   structural recursion and no other; every `xi` other than the decreasing `d`" (cons "   is written `_`, because a named binder the measure never mentions is an" (cons "   unused variable that verification refuses (§17.11). A label names one group" (cons "   of one kind: a label carried by both an inductive group and a definition" (cons "   group is rejected. No member of the group being checked is called or" (cons "   referenced under a lambda, and none is referenced as a value." (cons "3. **Well-founded recursion.** A definition with `termination` has no" (cons "   `recursive_argument`; it is standalone, a group of one, or a member of a" (cons "   well-founded mutual group. Its `measure` is a binder-free term of type" (cons "   `Nat` over its parameters that mentions no member of its group. Its" (cons "   recursive calls (calls to any member of its group) pass the group's own type parameters, do not" (cons "   occur in another recursive call's argument or under a `lambda` or" (cons "   quantifier, and the definition is never referenced as a value. A call's" (cons "   arguments, and every enclosing `if` condition and `match` scrutinee," (cons "   mention only parameters and the binders of enclosing matches, and those" (cons "   binders repeat no parameter or other enclosing binder. Every `if` and" (cons "   `match` of the body is numbered in one fixed pre-order; each call site," (cons "   in that order, has the *decrease obligation*" (cons "   `h1 -> ... -> measure[args] < measure` over its enclosing nodes, outermost" (cons "   first, where the conclusion compares the *callee's* measure at the call's" (cons "   arguments with the caller's own measure, and an `if` contributes `c = b` for its condition `c` and branch" (cons "   polarity `b`, and a `match` contributes `s = v` for its scrutinee `s` and" (cons "   the value `v` the branch's pattern denotes over its binders (a Boolean" (cons "   literal, a pair for `Prod.mk`, and otherwise the constructor applied to" (cons "   the binders at the scrutinee type's arguments). `evidence` lists one" (cons "   prior theorem of the same module per call site, in order, whose type" (cons "   parameters equal the definition's, whose parameters are the definition's" (cons "   followed by every enclosing match binder with its type, outermost first," (cons "   and whose statement equals the obligation exactly. Linking rejects any" (cons "   other evidence, so no recursive call enters the linked IR without its" (cons "   exact, separately named decrease obligation: unguarded recursion cannot" (cons "   enter it. Whether an obligation is *true* is a proof, and LexLean, as for" (cons "   every proof, leaves it to Lean's kernel at verification (§22): a false" (cons "   evidence theorem with the exact statement links, and verification refuses" (cons "   both the theorem and the definition (Lean independently refuses a" (cons "   nonterminating well-founded definition), so the project is never" (cons "   verified (`recursion-false-evidence`). An `executable` well-founded" (cons "   definition may be reached by a production root (§17.13), and no" (cons "   production claim holds for a project that is not verified." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0078,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "4. **Well-founded lowering.** The definition lowers to" (cons "   `@[expose, semireducible] public def`, each `if` to" (cons "   `match (generalizing := false) __decreaseN : c with | true => ... | false => ...`," (cons "   each `match` to `match (generalizing := false) __decreaseN : s with ...`," (cons "   then `termination_by measure` and `decreasing_by all_goals first` with one" (cons "   `| (have __evidence := evidence T... p... _... __decreaseI...; subst_vars; exact __evidence)`" (cons "   per call site, passing the parameters, one `_` per enclosing match binder," (cons "   and the hypotheses of its enclosing nodes. `generalizing := false` keeps" (cons "   every earlier hypothesis stated over the parameters, exactly as the" (cons "   evidence states it; each binder appears in its match's hypothesis, so Lean" (cons "   solves the `_` by unification and a binder the branch ignores stays" (cons "   `_`; Lean still refines the decreasing goal by each match on a variable," (cons "   and `subst_vars` applies the same substitutions to the evidence. Each" (cons "   goal is closed by the first evidence theorem whose statement it is, after" (cons "   those substitutions; since every evidence theorem states exactly one call" (cons "   site's obligation, which theorem closes a goal is not observable, and" (cons "   Lean presents a cross-member goal of a mutual group as the callee's" (cons "   measure at the arguments against the caller's measure, the obligation's" (cons "   own conclusion. A well-founded mutual group lowers to one `mutual ... end`" (cons "   block of such definitions. A parameter" (cons "   neither the body nor the measure mentions lowers as `_name` and is passed" (cons "   as `_name`; generated names begin with two underscores, which no lowered" (cons "   source binder does. Semireducibility lets closed instances reduce in" (cons "   proofs; the evidence's observed axioms flow into the definition's exact" (cons "   policy." (cons "5. **Theorem binders** that neither the statement nor the proof mentions" (cons "   are bound as `_name`, so an evidence theorem may take every parameter of" (cons "   its definition without a Lean linter warning." (cons "6. **Closed members.** Every member of a semantic-module value must survive" (cons "   into the typed value: a member the closed schema does not define, such as" (cons "   an extra member of a unit variant, is rejected in linking in every" (cons "   language." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.cont,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0080,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **Ordered keys.** A map key, set element, or graph node type is one of" (cons "   `Nat`, `Int`, the fixed-width integers, `Bool`, `String`, or a product of" (cons "   such types. Each has one fixed total order: integers numerically," (cons "   `false` before `true`, strings by Unicode scalar sequence (equivalently" (cons "   UTF-8 byte order), and products lexicographically. Any other key type is" (cons "   rejected, because its iteration order would be unspecified. No host hash" (cons "   or pointer order exists anywhere in the semantics. The generated Lean" (cons "   `Key` instances realize the same orders, and `examples/collections`" (cons "   states for every key type that a linked literal equals Lean's own" (cons "   insertion of the source order, so pinned Lean checks linking's order." (cons "2. **Representation.** A map value is its strictly ascending entry list and" (cons "   a set its strictly ascending element list, so equal maps and sets are" (cons "   structurally equal and iterate identically. Generated Lean represents" (cons "   `map K V` as `List (Prod K V)` and `set K` as `List K`, and the" (cons "   canonical document names them `Map (K) (V)` and `Set (K)`. Literals and" (cons "   the closed operations are the only producers, and each operation maps" (cons "   strictly ascending lists to strictly ascending lists; `SM-28` and `SM-30`" (cons "   check this differentially, under Lean, against an independent ordered-map" (cons "   model on seeded random operation sequences. Memory is one entry or" (cons "   element per member. Every literal entry, element, node, and edge is" (cons "   charged to `max_ir_nodes` in linking, so an oversized literal is" (cons "   `LLS8002` before either backend runs; a computed collection is a runtime" (cons "   value whose size is bounded only by the operations that build it, and" (cons "   the cost of each operation is stated in item 4." (cons "3. **Literals.** A `map_literal`, `set_literal`, or `graph_literal` is keyed" (cons "   only by literal values (natural, integer, string, and Boolean literals" (cons "   and pairs of them); other keys are inserted with `map_insert` or" (cons "   `set_insert`. Each literal key is first checked as a term, so its" (cons "   spelling is canonical and a fixed-width key is in range; only then does" (cons "   it have a position in the order. Linking rejects a duplicate key," (cons "   element, node, or edge, and" (cons "   a graph edge whose source or target is not a declared node, then sorts" (cons "   every literal into canonical order. Reordered equivalent source therefore" (cons "   links to identical semantic data and semantic identities, and generates" (cons "   byte-identical Lean modules, LaTeX modules, and lexicon closures; only" (cons "   the source identity and the artifacts that record source positions (the" (cons "   build manifest, source maps, and coverage) record the textual order." (cons "4. **Operations.** Each collection primitive has one explicit result type:" (cons "   insertion replaces an existing key, removal of an absent key is the" (cons "   identity, lookup returns `Option`, keys/values/entries/elements are" (cons "   ascending lists, union inserts the right operand's elements into the" (cons "   left, intersection and difference keep the left operand's order, and" (cons "   `map_fold`, `set_fold`, and `list_fold` visit members in ascending (or" (cons "   list) order with the state as the step's first argument. A graph is" (cons "   `map node (set node)`. Its *nodes* are its keys together with every" (cons "   successor, so a successor inserted without an entry of its own is a node" (cons "   with no successors; `graph_successors` of an absent node is empty;" (cons "   `graph_reachable` is the breadth-first closure including the start node," (cons "   computed in at most node-count-plus-one rounds, each of which either adds" (cons "   a node or ends the search, so the bound never truncates the closure;" (cons "   `graph_topological` is Kahn's order over every node, taking the least" (cons "   ready node first, removing one node per round within the same bound, or" (cons "   none when a cycle remains. Costs, in key comparisons, for collections of" (cons "   `n` and `m` members, a graph of `V` nodes and `E` edges, and maximum" (cons "   out-degree `d`: insertion, removal, lookup, and membership `O(n)`;" (cons "   size, keys, values, entries, elements, and folds `O(n)` steps; union" (cons "   `O(m(n + m))`; intersection and difference `O(nm)`; successors `O(V)`;" (cons "   reachability `O(V^2(V + E))`; topological order `O(V^3(V + d))`. Every" (cons "   intermediate list holds at most the members of its result or of the" (cons "   graph's node set." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0081,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "5. **State threading.** State is explicit and pure: `list_fold`, `map_fold`," (cons "   `set_fold`, `iterate` (exactly `n` steps), and `iterate_until` (until the" (cons "   step returns none or its natural-number fuel is exhausted, returning the" (cons "   final state and whether a fixed point was reached) take the step as a" (cons "   function argument. The count and the fuel are required arguments, so" (cons "   every iteration performs at most that many steps; an iteration without" (cons "   one is rejected in linking. No ambient mutable state exists. A lambda or function reference passed as a step is in a closure" (cons "   position, so executable definitions may use these combinators." (cons "6. **Runtime.** In language 1.2 every definition of the portable runtime is" (cons "   exposed (`@[noinline]` is kept where language 1.1 has it), so a" (cons "   definition imported from another module reduces in the kernel through" (cons "   the primitives it applies; the language-1.1 runtime text is frozen. A" (cons "   module that writes a collection type or literal, or applies any" (cons "   collection primitive (including one applied only to a collection" (cons "   imported from another module), imports the runtime module" (cons "   `LexLeanRuntime.Collections`: the `Key` order instances and the" (cons "   operations above as exposed, total Lean definitions over lists, in the" (cons "   namespace `LexLeanCollections` (*Runtime modules* below)." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.cont,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0082,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Runtime modules.** The runtime of language 1.2 is LexLean source, not text" (cons "of the backend: five native core modules (§17.10) of the embedded project" (cons "`language/runtime-1.2/library/` (module prefix `LexLeanRuntime`, registry" (cons "`language/runtime-1.2/library.toml`, tag `lexlean/runtime-library/1`), whose" (cons "Lean the compiler generates like any other core module's:" (cons "`LexLeanRuntime.Portable` (namespace `LexLeanRuntime`: the checked" (cons "fixed-width arithmetic, byte strings, and decimal text the primitives apply)," (cons "`LexLeanRuntime.Collections` (namespace `LexLeanCollections`, rule 6)," (cons "`LexLeanRuntime.Models` (namespace `LexLeanModels`, rule 12 of the model" (cons "rules), and `LexLeanRuntime.Reasoning` (namespace `LexLeanReasoning`, rule 11" (cons "of the reasoning rules; it imports the first two), and `LexLeanRuntime.Laws`" (cons "(namespace `LexLeanLaws`, *Fold laws* below; it imports the first two). A" (cons "generated language-1.2" (cons "module publicly imports, after `Init` and before its own imports, exactly the" (cons "runtime modules it uses: `Portable` when it applies a primitive or has a" (cons "reasoning declaration, `Collections` under rule 6, `Models` when it has a" (cons "model declaration, `Reasoning` when it has a reasoning declaration, and" (cons "`Laws` when it has a `linear_arithmetic` or `law` proof; it carries no runtime" (cons "text. Every declaration of a runtime module pins its exact" (cons "axioms; a verification that needs a runtime module compiles it before the" (cons "project's modules, replays it with `leanchecker`, audits each of its" (cons "declarations, and refuses any observed axiom set but the pinned one" (cons "(§22.3–§22.6, `RT-03`). The first four runtime modules were migrated once," (cons "losslessly, from the texts the backend spliced, with the equivalence evidence" (cons "in `tests/runtime-library/MIGRATION.md`; `Laws` was exported once from Lean," (cons "with its evidence in `tests/runtime-library/LAWS.md`. A declaration is the same up to the" (cons "module that holds it, so a runtime name is `LexLeanRuntime.x`, never" (cons "`<module>.LexLeanRuntime.x` (`RT-01`). The decoder of core module 2 (§17.10)" (cons "is the one Lean text of the language-1.2 compiler that is not generated from a" (cons "native module: it is what loads native modules, so it cannot be one (`RT-04`)." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{

    arguments: [
      (cons "Languages 1.0 and 1.1 are frozen, and their released output spliced the" (cons "portable runtime (into a language-1.1 semantic module, inside its namespace)" (cons "and the decoder of core module 1 (into every language-1.0 or 1.1 core module)" (cons "as text. Those bytes are the one declared exception to generating Lean from" (cons "native modules: they are data, `language/runtime-1.2/frozen/portable-runtime-1.1.txt`" (cons "and `language/runtime-1.2/frozen/core-decoder-1.txt`, which the language-1.0" (cons "and 1.1 backends alone read, and `RT-02` holds them equal to the released" (cons "output." (nil String)))))))))

definition{
  name: i0085,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A program equality over all inputs (two folds that compute one value, an" (cons "integer kernel that is monotone or bounded) is proved by `linear_arithmetic`" (cons "or by `law`, and the facts both use are LexLean source, never assumed. These" (cons "rules are LexLean's own; the diagnostic each linking failure raises is" (cons "`LLT4001`, and a statement Lean does not accept is `LLV7002`." (nil String))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0086,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **The laws module.** `LexLeanRuntime.Laws` is a native runtime module" (cons "   (*Runtime modules* above) importing `Portable` and `Collections`. It" (cons "   declares, in the namespace `LexLeanLaws`, the sum of a function over a" (cons "   list (`sumNat`, `sumInt`), the bridges from the runtime to propositions," (cons "   and the laws below, each row pinning its exact axioms. It was exported" (cons "   once from Lean and its regenerated environment checked against the" (cons "   committed source declaration by declaration" (cons "   (`tests/runtime-library/LAWS.md`)." (cons "2. **The registry.** `language/runtime-1.2/laws.toml` (tag" (cons "   `lexlean/runtime-laws/1`) names what the compiler uses of the module:" (cons "   the ordered rewrite set of `linear_arithmetic`, and the laws a `law`" (cons "   proof applies, each with its explicit type parameters, its parameter" (cons "   types in the semantic schema, and its number of premises. It states no" (cons "   law. The runtime build refuses a registry that names a declaration the" (cons "   module does not have or registers a law twice." (cons "3. **Bridges.** `less_than` on `Nat`, `Int`, and every fixed-width type is" (cons "   `decide` of the order of mathematical values; `quotient` and `remainder`" (cons "   on `Nat` and `Int` are an `if` on a zero divisor around `/` and `％`, or" (cons "   `Int.tdiv` and `Int.tmod`, which are rewritten to Euclidean division and" (cons "   remainder with a sign case; `length` and `append` of a list are" (cons "   `List.length` and `++`; `set_fold`, `map_fold`, `set_size`, `map_size`," (cons "   `set_elements`, and `map_entries` are the list operations on the" (cons "   canonical list they are, a map's fold being the fold of its entries." (cons "4. **Additive folds.** A fold whose step adds (or, over `Int`, subtracts)" (cons "   what each element contributes is its initial value plus (minus) the sum" (cons "   of the contributions; the sum is linear in the contribution (sums," (cons "   differences, negation, and a constant factor on either side), a constant" (cons "   contribution sums to the constant times the length, and the sum of an" (cons "   appended list is the sum of the parts. The fold that conses every element" (cons "   onto its state is the reverse of the list onto it, and the fold that" (cons "   appends every element is the list after the state. A sum is invariant" (cons "   under every permutation of the list (`sumNat_perm`, `sumInt_perm`), and" (cons "   in particular under reverse. With these rewrites the equality of two" (cons "   additive folds over all lists, sets, or maps is linear over the sums of" (cons "   the contributions, which `omega` decides." (cons "5. **Applied laws.** `list_fold_fusion`: a function `h` that commutes with" (cons "   the steps, as a prior theorem states for all states and elements, carries" (cons "   the fold of one step to the fold of the other, `h (list_fold f z xs) =" (cons "   list_fold g (h z) xs`. `list_fold_pair`: two folds over one list are one" (cons "   fold over the pair of their states. `list_fold_append`: a fold over an" (cons "   appended list continues the fold of the first part." (cons "6. **Closed instances.** `decide` evaluates a closed statement and does not" (cons "   decide one with a free variable, so a statement over all inputs is proved" (cons "   by the laws and `omega`, never by enumerating inputs." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0088,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A model is a contract bound to a realization of the same interface, with" (cons "evidence about the realization. These rules are LexLean's own and are checked" (cons "in linking, before either backend runs; the diagnostic each failure raises is" (cons "named with the rule. Every model declaration *elaborates*, in linking, to" (cons "ordinary language-1.2 definitions and theorems. The Lean backend, production" (cons "eligibility, extraction, and the axiom audit read only the elaboration; the" (cons "canonical document and the snapshot read the source declaration together with" (cons "its elaboration (rule 13). No prompt text, free-form description, raw JSON," (cons "unchecked code, or untyped blob defines any meaning: every member of every" (cons "model declaration belongs to the closed `lexlean/semantic-module/2` schema," (cons "every closed set below is a closed enumeration of that schema, and an unknown" (cons "member or value is an ordinary schema failure (`LLT4001`)." (nil String)))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0089,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **References and order.** Every contract, realization, evidence, model," (cons "   predicate, validator, artifact, and theorem a model declaration names is" (cons "   a prior declaration of its module or an imported one. Every theorem named" (cons "   by a validator, a width obligation, a claim, a junction, or an entry is a" (cons "   prior theorem *of the same module* as the declaration naming it, as for" (cons "   termination evidence. A use of a generic contract, realization, or model" (cons "   is `{\"member\":M,\"type_arguments\":[T...]}` with exactly its type" (cons "   parameters; nothing is inferred. A model declaration's `axioms` row is" (cons "   the exact axiom policy of every Lean declaration it generates (rule 12)." (cons "2. **Artifacts** (`LLR3007`, `LLR3008`). `{\"kind\":\"artifact\",\"name\":W," (cons "   \"role\":R,\"sha256\":H,\"length\":N,\"schema\":S,\"type\":T}` names the project" (cons "   bytes configured as `[[artifact_source]]` with digest `H` (§10.1). Linking" (cons "   reads each configured artifact once, confined (§25.1), within" (cons "   `max_file_bytes`, and counted toward `max_total_source_bytes` (§10.2), and" (cons "   requires its SHA-256 to equal the configured digest. A missing file, a" (cons "   digest or length differing from the configuration or the declaration, or" (cons "   a declaration whose digest no configured source has is `LLR3007`. The" (cons "   closed schemas are `bytes` (decoding to `bytes`); `int_tensor` with an" (cons "   `element` among `int8`, `int16`, `int32`, `int64`, `uint8`, `uint16`," (cons "   `uint32`, `uint64` and a nonempty `shape` of at most 16 positive extents" (cons "   (little-endian, two's complement for the signed elements, row-major," (cons "   exactly width times the product of the extents bytes, decoding to the" (cons "   `shape`-deep nested `list int` of the exact element values); and" (cons "   `utf8_lines` (valid UTF-8 without CR, every line LF-terminated, decoding" (cons "   to `list string`). The closed roles constrain the schema: `parameters`" (cons "   and `table` take `int_tensor`, `vocabulary` takes `utf8_lines`, `binary`" (cons "   takes `bytes`, and `dataset` takes any. Bytes that do not decode under" (cons "   the schema, a declared type other than the schema's decoded type, or a" (cons "   role its schema does not admit is `LLR3008`. The rank bound, the role," (cons "   and the declared type are checked, and the bytes checked against the" (cons "   schema, before any byte is decoded. Each declaration is then charged" (cons "   before it materializes anything, because one digest may be named by any" (cons "   number of declarations and each holds its own copy: its length counts" (cons "   once more toward `max_total_source_bytes`, and the exact node count of" (cons "   its decoded value (computed from the length, the shape, and the line" (cons "   count, without building it) is charged to `max_ir_nodes` on top of every" (cons "   node linked and decoded before it in the link; exceeding either is" (cons "   `LLS8002` before decoding allocates. Decoding a project's artifacts" (cons "   therefore allocates at most a constant multiple of" (cons "   `max_total_source_bytes` bytes and `max_ir_nodes` nodes. There is no" (cons "   floating-point" (cons "   element, no JSON, ONNX, or other configuration schema, and no" (cons "   unspecified rounding. An artifact elaborates to `W.bytes : bytes`, its" (cons "   exact bytes, and `W : T`, the decoded value; generated Lean adds the" (cons "   kernel-checked theorem `W.decoded` that `W` is the decoding of" (cons "   `W.bytes` (by `decide` for tensors and lines, by `rfl` for bytes), so the" (cons "   typed value used everywhere is tied to the exact content-addressed bytes." (cons "   `W.bytes` is emitted from the bytes linking read and digest-checked" (cons "   against both the configuration and the declaration, in the same process" (cons "   that renders the module; verification does not re-digest the emitted" (cons "   literal, so that link rests on construction, while the link from the" (cons "   bytes to the typed value is the kernel's." (cons "3. **Contracts** (`LLT4006`). `{\"kind\":\"contract\",\"name\":C," (cons "   \"type_parameters\":[...],\"input\":{\"name\":x,\"type\":I}," (cons "   \"output\":{\"name\":y,\"type\":O},\"state\":{\"name\":s,\"next\":t,\"type\":S}?," (cons "   \"precondition\":P?,\"postcondition\":Q?,\"invariant\":J?,\"validators\":[...]}`." (cons "   The binder names are pairwise distinct legal names; `invariant` requires" (cons "   `state`; at least one predicate is present. Each predicate is a prior" (cons "   definition with the contract's type parameters, result `prop`, and the" (cons "   parameter types of the table below; anything else is `LLT4006`." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

      (cons "   | Contract | Precondition | Postcondition | Invariant |" (cons "   |---|---|---|---|" (cons "   | stateless | `(I)` | `(I, O)` | — |" (cons "   | stateful | `(S, I)` | `(S, I, S, O)` | `(S)` |" (nil String)))))
