definition{
  name: i0091,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   `validators` are strictly sorted by predicate, at most one per present" (cons "   predicate: `{\"predicate\":p,\"validator\":V,\"sound\":T,\"complete\":U?}`. `V`" (cons "   is a prior `executable` definition of the predicate's parameter types" (cons "   with result `bool`; `T` states exactly `V(args) = true -> p(args)` and" (cons "   `U`, when present, exactly `p(args) -> V(args) = true`, over the" (cons "   predicate's parameters named by the contract binders (statements are" (cons "   compared up to the names of bound variables). A missing, mistyped, or" (cons "   non-executable validator, an absent predicate, or an inexact theorem is" (cons "   `LLT4006`. A contract elaborates to no runtime declaration; generated" (cons "   Lean restates each link as `C.<predicate>_sound` and" (cons "   `C.<predicate>_complete` against the fixed `LexLeanModels.SoundN` and" (cons "   `CompleteN` (rule 12)." (cons "4. **Realizations** (`LLT4006`). `{\"kind\":\"realization\",\"name\":R," (cons "   \"type_parameters\":[...],\"input\":{\"name\":x,\"type\":I},\"output\":O," (cons "   \"state\":{\"name\":s,\"type\":S,\"initial\":v}?,\"descriptor\":D," (cons "   \"executable\":true?}` elaborates to the ordinary definition" (cons "   `R : (I) -> O`, or `R : (S, I) -> product S O` with `R.initial : S` when" (cons "   stateful, plus the companion definitions named below; companions are" (cons "   ordinary definitions under the realization's name and occupy their names" (cons "   (§17.8). Each descriptor term has the type its slot fixes (a guard" (cons "   `bool`, an action, default, or label `O`, an encoder or feature map" (cons "   `list int`, a denotation `O` or `product S O`); a term of another type is" (cons "   an interface mismatch, `LLT4006`. The closed descriptors:" (cons "   - `deterministic` `{\"body\":b}`: the denotation is `b`." (cons "   - `rule` `{\"rules\":[{\"name\":n,\"guard\":g,\"action\":a}...],\"default\":d}`:" (cons "     at least one rule, names unique; the denotation is the first rule, in" (cons "     declared order, whose guard holds, else `d`; the companion" (cons "     `R.selected : (I) -> nat` is the index of the firing rule, or the rule" (cons "     count for the default." (cons "   - `statistical` `{\"scheme\":\"linear_scoring\",\"features\":f,\"width\":w," (cons "     \"width_evidence\":T,\"weights\":Wt,\"bias\":B,\"labels\":[...]}`, stateless:" (cons "     the companions `R.features` (`f`) and `R.scores` (`Wt` times the" (cons "     features plus `B`, over mathematical integers) and the denotation, the" (cons "     label of the first maximal score." (cons "   - `neural` `{\"architecture\":\"integer_feedforward\",\"encoder\":e,\"width\":w," (cons "     \"width_evidence\":T,\"layers\":[...],\"decoder\":...}`, stateless: the" (cons "     companions `R.encoded` (`e`), `R.layer1` ... `R.layerK` (one per layer," (cons "     each `list int -> list int`), and `R.logits`, and the denotation" (cons "     decoding the final values. The closed layers are `dense`" (cons "     `{\"inputs\":n,\"outputs\":m,\"weights\":Wt,\"bias\":B}` (`Wt v + B` over" (cons "     mathematical integers), `relu` (negative values become zero), and" (cons "     `requantize` `{\"shift\":k,\"minimum\":lo,\"maximum\":hi}` (division by `2^k`" (cons "     truncating toward zero, then clamping to `[lo, hi]`; `lo <= hi` and" (cons "     `k <= 4096`). The closed decoders are `argmax` `{\"labels\":[...]}` (the" (cons "     label of the first maximal value) and `function` `{\"binder\":v," (cons "     \"body\":b}` (`b` over the final values bound to `v`)." (cons "   - `composite` `{\"form\":F}`: rule 7." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0092,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   Widths are checked statically: the first dense layer takes `width`" (cons "   inputs, each dense layer takes the current width, a weights slot is a" (cons "   `parameters` artifact of shape `[outputs, inputs]` (statistical:" (cons "   `[labels, width]`) and a bias slot one of shape `[outputs]`, and an" (cons "   argmax decoder has one label per final value; a label list is nonempty" (cons "   and each label is a closed term, mentioning no local. `width_evidence`" (cons "   states exactly" (cons "   `length(e) = w` over the input binder. Any other width, shape, role, or" (cons "   label count, or an inexact width theorem, is `LLT4006`. The kernels are" (cons "   ordinary terms over `list_fold`, `match`, the integer primitives, and" (cons "   `less_than`, so they mean exactly what Lean computes and nothing is" (cons "   approximated. A realization marked `executable` is checked as executable" (cons "   code, including its companions." (cons "5. **Evidence** (`LLT4009`). `{\"kind\":\"evidence\",\"name\":E," (cons "   \"type_parameters\":[...],\"contract\":Cuse,\"realization\":Ruse," (cons "   \"claims\":[...]}` about a contract and a realization of the same interface" (cons "   (otherwise `LLT4006`). `claims` are strictly sorted by kind and theorem." (cons "   The closed claim kinds, their generated statements (premises absent from" (cons "   the contract are dropped; `R` is the realization at its type arguments)," (cons "   and the fixed Lean helper each is restated against:" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0093,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   | Claim | Requires | Generated statement | Helper |" (cons "   |---|---|---|---|" (cons "   | `satisfies_contract` | postcondition | stateless: `P x -> Q x (R x)`; stateful: `J s -> P s x -> Q s x (first(R s x)) (second(R s x))` | `Satisfies`, `SatisfiesTotal`, `SatisfiesStep...` |" (cons "   | `preserves_invariant` | invariant | `J s -> P s x -> J (first(R s x))` | `Preserves`, `PreservesTotal` |" (cons "   | `initial_invariant` | invariant | `J R.initial` | `Initial` |" (cons "   | `equivalent_to` (`\"reference\":F`, a definition or model of the realization's signature) | — | `P x -> R x = F x` (stateful: over `s` and `x` with `J s`) | `Equivalent...` |" (cons "   | `dataset_agreement` (`\"dataset\":D,\"comparison\":c,\"comparison_sound\":T',\"examples\":n,\"agreements\":k`), stateless | — | `length(D) = n` and the count of examples `(a, b)` of `D` with `c (R a) b = true` is `k` | `Agreement` |" (nil String))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0094,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   For `dataset_agreement`, `D : list (product I O)` is a prior nullary" (cons "   definition, `c : (O, O) -> bool`, `k <= n`, and `T'` states exactly" (cons "   `c e o = true -> e = o`, so an agreement is an equality. Each claim's" (cons "   theorem states exactly its generated statement. An unknown claim kind is" (cons "   a schema failure (`LLT4001`); a claim whose contract lacks what it" (cons "   requires (a vacuous claim), an inexact, later, foreign, or non-theorem" (cons "   reference, or unsorted or duplicate claims is `LLT4009`. Evidence" (cons "   elaborates to no runtime declaration. Nothing is ever asserted without a" (cons "   theorem: a claim is *discharged* in linking and *verified* only by a" (cons "   verified attestation (rule 13)." (cons "6. **Models** (`LLT4006`, `LLT4007`, `LLT4009`). `{\"kind\":\"model\",\"name\":M," (cons "   \"type_parameters\":[...],\"contract\":Cuse,\"realization\":Ruse," (cons "   \"evidence\":[Euse...]?,\"entry\":[T...]?}`. The realization's input," (cons "   output, and state types equal the contract's at their type arguments" (cons "   (`LLT4006`); each listed evidence, listed once, is about exactly this" (cons "   contract and realization use (`LLT4009`). `entry` lists one theorem per" (cons "   *effective precondition* `e` of the realization (none for the" (cons "   noncomposite kinds; rule 7 for composites), in order, each stating" (cons "   exactly `P x -> e` (or `e` when the contract has no precondition), and" (cons "   for a stateful realization `J s -> P s x -> e` over its state and input" (cons "   (absent premises dropped); an omission or an inexact theorem is" (cons "   `LLT4007`. A claim discharges only the check it states: a dataset" (cons "   agreement or an equivalence discharges none, and satisfying the contract" (cons "   does not preserve its invariant. A model elaborates to the" (cons "   ordinary definition `M`, applying the realization, and `M.initial` when" (cons "   stateful. Its *required checks* are: `input_invariant` when the contract" (cons "   has an invariant; `output_invariant` when it has an invariant and no" (cons "   evidence discharges `preserves_invariant`; `postcondition` when it has a" (cons "   postcondition and no evidence discharges `satisfies_contract`; and" (cons "   `precondition` when it has a precondition." (cons "7. **Composition** (`LLT4007`). A composite stage is a prior model, stateless" (cons "   or stateful. Types match exactly. A stage's *checks after it runs* are the" (cons "   output invariant and the postcondition among its required checks (rule 6);" (cons "   each needs a sound validator, and they run right after the stage in that" (cons "   order, a refusal returning `ContractViolation.output_invariant` or" (cons "   `.postcondition`. So a stage whose only evidence is, for example, a dataset" (cons "   agreement composes, its postcondition checked at run time. A composite with" (cons "   any check at run time (a `checked` junction or a stage's checks after it" (cons "   runs) has the output `result O contract_violation`, `O` its output without" (cons "   checks." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0095,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   *State.* A sequence, fan-out, product, or branch with stateful stages" (cons "   declares a state whose type is the right-nested product of those stages'" (cons "   states in stage order (`left` before `right`, `then` before `else`), with" (cons "   any initial value of that type; a composite without stateful stages" (cons "   declares none (`LLT4007` otherwise). Each stateful stage reads its own" (cons "   component and its step replaces exactly that component: a sequence and a" (cons "   fan-out or product replace the component of every stateful stage they run," (cons "   and a branch replaces only the component of the arm taken, carrying the" (cons "   other unchanged. A refusal returns the state the composite was given, with" (cons "   the violation. A stage's state component is the one the composite was" (cons "   given, so its invariant is established at the composite's entry unless a" (cons "   checked junction validates it at run time." (nil String)))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0096,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   The closed forms:" (cons "   - `sequence` `{\"stages\":[...],\"junctions\":[...]}`: at least two stages," (cons "     one junction per stage after the first, each stage consuming the" (cons "     previous stage's output. A junction before stage `i+1` is" (cons "     `unconditional` (the stage has no precondition), `proved`" (cons "     `{\"evidence\":T}` (the stage has a precondition), or `checked` (the" (cons "     stage has a precondition or is stateful with an invariant, and has a" (cons "     validator for each; the invariant validator, on its component, and the" (cons "     precondition validator run before it, a refusal returning" (cons "     `.input_invariant` or `.precondition`); a `proved` junction before a" (cons "     stage without a precondition, or a `checked` one before a stage with" (cons "     nothing to check, is `LLT4007`. `T` is over the" (cons "     composite's state `s` (when stateful) and the earlier stage's input `y`," (cons "     and states exactly that the earlier stage's invariant of its component" (cons "     and its precondition imply the later stage's precondition of its" (cons "     component and the earlier stage's output: `J_i(s_i) -> P_i(s_i, y) ->" (cons "     P_{i+1}(s_{i+1}, out)`, each absent premise dropped and each stateless" (cons "     stage taking no component, where `out` is `M_i(y)`, or" (cons "     `second(M_i(s_i, y))` for a stateful stage. The effective preconditions" (cons "     are the first stage's invariant and precondition and the invariant of" (cons "     every later stateful stage whose junction is not checked." (cons "   - `fanout` `{\"left\":Ml,\"right\":Mr}`: one input, output" (cons "     `product O_l O_r`; effective preconditions each stage's invariant and" (cons "     precondition (`P_l x` and `P_r x` for stateless stages; `J_l(s_l)`," (cons "     `P_l(s_l, x)`, `J_r(s_r)`, and `P_r(s_r, x)` for stateful ones, each" (cons "     absent predicate dropped)." (cons "   - `product` `{\"left\":Ml,\"right\":Mr}`: input `product I_l I_r`, output" (cons "     `product O_l O_r`; effective preconditions each stage's invariant and" (cons "     precondition (`P_l (first x)` and `P_r (second x)` for stateless" (cons "     stages; `J_l(s_l)`, `P_l(s_l, first x)`, `J_r(s_r)`, and" (cons "     `P_r(s_r, second x)` for stateful ones, each absent predicate dropped)." (cons "   - `branch` `{\"guard\":g,\"then\":Mt,\"else\":Me}`: `g : bool` (another type is" (cons "     `LLT4007`), both arms of the composite's interface; effective" (cons "     preconditions `g = true -> R` for each invariant and precondition `R`" (cons "     of the then stage and `g = false -> R` for the else stage's." (cons "   - `scan` `{\"stage\":Ms,\"junction\":j}` over one stateful stage: input" (cons "     `list I`, threading the stage's state from `Ms.initial` and collecting" (cons "     outputs in order; it declares no state of its own. The junction is" (cons "     `unconditional` (no precondition, no checks after the stage runs, and" (cons "     any invariant carried by `initial_invariant` and `preserves_invariant`" (cons "     evidence), `proved` (that evidence, no checks after the stage, and `T`" (cons "     stating exactly `J s -> P s x`; the stage has a precondition), or" (cons "     `checked` (the stage has an invariant, a precondition, or a check after" (cons "     it runs, with a validator for each; the invariant and precondition" (cons "     validators run before each step and the checks after it after each" (cons "     step; the first refusal is returned; the output is" (cons "     `result (list O) contract_violation`)." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0097,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   Any other composition, junction, or stage is `LLT4007`. A composite's" (cons "   denotation is the ordinary typed composition of its stages' model" (cons "   functions and their validators." (cons "8. **Runtime boundary** (`LLT4008`). `checked_apply` names a model, its type" (cons "   arguments, its arguments (`x`, or `s, x` when stateful), and `checks`, a" (cons "   strictly sorted subset of `input_invariant`, `output_invariant`," (cons "   `postcondition`, and `precondition`, each of which names a predicate the" (cons "   contract has and validates with a sound validator. Its type is" (cons "   `result O contract_violation`, or `result (product S O)" (cons "   contract_violation` when stateful. It elaborates to ordinary code that" (cons "   binds the arguments once and runs the listed checks in the fixed order" (cons "   input invariant, precondition, the model, output invariant," (cons "   postcondition, returning `Result.error` of the violated predicate's" (cons "   constructor at the first refusal and `Result.ok` of the model's result" (cons "   otherwise; generated binders are spelled `__checked<n>_...`, a spelling" (cons "   no source name can take. In executable code (an executable definition" (cons "   or instance field, or a descriptor term of an executable realization), a" (cons "   `checked_apply` lists every required check of its model; a plain `call`" (cons "   or `function_ref` of a model is allowed only when the model has no" (cons "   required checks; and a realization function is never applied directly." (cons "   Anything else is `LLT4008`. Formal code may apply any model unchecked. A" (cons "   check is therefore erased exactly when a theorem discharges it, and a" (cons "   proof-only predicate is never presented as runtime enforcement." (cons "9. **The `contract_violation` type.** Its four nullary constructors are" (cons "   ordinary data, matched by `match`; they lower to the pairs of `Prod Bool" (cons "   Bool` `(false, false)` (precondition), `(false, true)` (input invariant)," (cons "   `(true, false)` (postcondition), and `(true, true)` (output invariant)," (cons "   so values cross modules unchanged, and the canonical document names the" (cons "   type `ContractViolation`." (cons "10. **Identity and accounting.** Model declarations are linked IR, so every" (cons "    digest, length, schema, role, type, descriptor, claim, junction, entry," (cons "    and check is part of the semantic ID (§21.4); artifact bytes are covered" (cons "    through their digests, and their configured digests through the source" (cons "    ID. Every source node and every elaborated declaration and obligation" (cons "    is charged to `max_ir_nodes`." (cons "11. **Elaboration in Lean.** Each elaborated definition and theorem lowers as" (cons "    any language-1.2 declaration does; a proposition-valued language-1.2" (cons "    definition is `@[expose, reducible]`, so a predicate over a finite" (cons "    domain is decidable exactly when its body is. A long list literal in an" (cons "    elaboration is a balanced `append` of literals of at most 32 elements." (cons "    Declarations a model declaration generates refer to their own module's" (cons "    members by qualified name." (cons "12. **Cross-checks and the fixed model semantics.** A module with a model" (cons "    declaration imports the runtime module `LexLeanRuntime.Models`" (cons "    (*Runtime modules*), namespace `LexLeanModels`:" (cons "    `Sound1`, `Sound2`, `Sound4`, `Complete1`, `Complete2`, `Complete4`, the" (cons "    `Satisfies`, `Preserves`, `Initial`, `Equivalent`, and `Agreement`" (cons "    helpers, and the decoders `inRange`, `encodeInt`, `tensorMatches`," (cons "    `utf8Char`, and `linesMatch`, each an exposed total definition. Every" (cons "    validator link and claim is restated against these fixed definitions by a" (cons "    generated theorem (`C.<predicate>_sound`," (cons "    `C.<predicate>_complete`, `E.<theorem>`) whose proof is exactly the" (cons "    user's theorem, or `decide` for `dataset_agreement`; every artifact has" (cons "    its `W.decoded` theorem. LexLean's statement generator is thus checked" (cons "    by pinned Lean: had it generated a statement not definitionally equal to" (cons "    the fixed helper's, Lean would refuse the restatement. `examples/models`" (cons "    produces, and verifies, every helper and every check branch, and" (cons "    `MD-12` fails if a helper of the emitted runtime is produced by none of" (cons "    its declarations. The axiom audit covers every generated declaration: each" (cons "    is allowed its owner's axioms, and their union must equal the owner's" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0098,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "    exact policy (§22.6)." (cons "13. **Document, snapshot, and attestation.** The canonical document renders" (cons "    each model declaration as a closed catalog of facts (interface," (cons "    descriptor, schema and digest but never the bytes, predicates," (cons "    validators, claims, junctions, entry theorems, required checks), each" (cons "    generated obligation with the theorem that states it, and each" (cons "    elaborated definition, an artifact's only by name and type (its bytes" (cons "    and decoded value are never document text, whatever their size); a" (cons "    claim reads \"discharged by\" its theorem and no" (cons "    document text says \"verified\". A language-1.2 snapshot declaration" (cons "    carries its `elaboration` (declarations, the alpha identity of each" (cons "    elaborated definition, obligations, cross-checks, and required checks) when it is a model declaration or applies a model" (cons "    through `checked_apply`. Only a verified attestation records the" (cons "    generated declarations, each with its policy and result, as verified." (cons "14. **Production.** Artifacts, realizations, models, validators, and" (cons "    checked applications are realized through their elaborations; contracts" (cons "    and evidence are erased (§17.13). Lean extraction of a root sees only" (cons "    elaborated definitions." (cons "15. **IEEE-754 bit patterns as exact dyadic rationals.** Because no floating-point" (cons "    type exists in LexLean, IEEE-754 `binary32` (1 sign bit, 8-bit biased" (cons "    exponent with bias 127, 23-bit trailing significand) and `binary64` (1 sign" (cons "    bit, 11-bit biased exponent with bias 1023, 52-bit trailing significand)" (cons "    parameter bit patterns enter exact integer derivations by decoding to exact" (cons "    dyadic rationals `(sign, mantissa, exponent)` representing" (cons "    $(-1)^{\\text{sign}} \\cdot \\text{mantissa} \\cdot 2^{\\text{exponent}}$ over" (cons "    `nat` and `int`:" (cons "    - Out-of-width bit patterns and non-finite biased exponents (`255` for" (cons "      `binary32`, `2047` for `binary64`, covering infinities and NaNs) decode" (cons "      to `none`." (cons "    - Zero and subnormal patterns (`biased = 0`) decode to `mantissa = fraction`" (cons "      and `exponent = -149` (`binary32`) or `exponent = -1074` (`binary64`)." (cons "    - Normal patterns (`1 <= biased <= 254` for `binary32`, `1 <= biased <= 2046`" (cons "      for `binary64`) decode to `mantissa = 2^23 + fraction` and" (cons "      `exponent = biased - 150` (`binary32`) or `mantissa = 2^52 + fraction`" (cons "      and `exponent = biased - 1075` (`binary64`)." (cons "    - Fixed-point scale ratios $\\operatorname{round}(a \\cdot b / c \\cdot 2^{32})$" (cons "      from three `binary32` bit patterns decode $a$, $b$, and $c$ to exact" (cons "      dyadic rationals, reject any `none` operand or a zero divisor" (cons "      ($\\text{mantissa}_c = 0$) as `none`, and compute the nearest integer" (cons "      (ties to even) by exact integer Euclidean division and bit-stepping over" (cons "      `iterate_until`, kernel-checked by `decide`." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.cont,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

(nil String)))))))
    ],

definition{
  name: i0101,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A reasoning machine is a *logic* (a state type with a relation between a state" (cons "and its successor), *inference rules* over the logic, a *verifier* that" (cons "checks a candidate answer against a specification, and a *reasoner* that" (cons "applies the rules under a closed strategy and answers only what its verifier" (cons "accepts or what a theorem proves correct. As with models, the rules below are" (cons "LexLean's own and are checked in linking, before either backend runs; the" (cons "diagnostic each failure raises is named with the rule. Every reasoning" (cons "declaration *elaborates*, in linking, to ordinary language-1.2 inductives," (cons "structures, definitions, and theorems over the existing `match`, `if`," (cons "`list_fold`, `iterate_until`, and set primitives, which the ordinary rules" (cons "then check again. There is no reasoner primitive, no runtime template of its" (cons "own, and no oracle, prompt, or model-based escape hatch: every member of" (cons "every reasoning declaration belongs to the closed `lexlean/semantic-module/2`" (cons "schema, and an unknown member, strategy, or claim is an ordinary schema" (cons "failure (`LLT4001`). The Lean backend, production eligibility, extraction," (cons "and the axiom audit read only the elaboration." (nil String)))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0102,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **References and order** (`LLT4010`). Every logic, rule, verifier," (cons "   relation, predicate, ranking, check, and theorem a reasoning declaration" (cons "   names is a prior declaration of its module or an imported one, and every" (cons "   theorem it names is a prior theorem of the same module. A use of a" (cons "   logic, rule, or verifier is `{\"member\":M,\"type_arguments\":[T...]}` with" (cons "   exactly its type parameters. Logics, rules, verifiers, and reasoners may" (cons "   declare `type_parameters`; a generic reasoner's whole elaboration takes" (cons "   them. A declaration's `axioms` row is the exact axiom policy of every" (cons "   Lean declaration it generates." (cons "2. **Logics** (`LLT4010`). `{\"kind\":\"logic\",\"name\":L,\"state\":{\"name\":s," (cons "   \"next\":t,\"type\":S},\"relation\":Rel,\"invariant\":{\"predicate\":J," (cons "   \"preserves\":P}?,\"ranking\":Mu?}`: `s` and `t` are distinct; `Rel` takes" (cons "   `(S, S)` to `Prop`, `J` takes `S` to `Prop`, and `Mu` takes `S` to `nat`." (cons "   `P` states exactly `J s -> Rel s t -> J t`. A logic elaborates to no" (cons "   definition; it generates `L.preserves : Preserves Rel J`." (cons "3. **Inference rules** (`LLT4010`). `{\"kind\":\"inference_rule\",\"name\":N," (cons "   \"logic\":Luse,\"binding\":{\"name\":b,\"type\":B,\"candidates\":c}?,\"guard\":g," (cons "   \"conclusion\":k,\"soundness\":T,\"progress\":U?,\"executable\":true?}`: over the" (cons "   logic's state binder (and `b`), `g` is `bool`, `k` is the state type, and" (cons "   `c` is `list B` over the state. `T` states exactly `g = true -> Rel s k`;" (cons "   `U`, which needs a ranking, states exactly `[J s ->] g = true -> Mu k <" (cons "   Mu s`, its premise present exactly when the logic has an invariant. A" (cons "   rule elaborates to `N.guard`, `N.conclusion`, `N.candidates` (with a" (cons "   binding), and the guarded application `N.apply = if N.guard then some" (cons "   N.conclusion else none`, and generates `N.apply_sound` and" (cons "   `N.apply_progress`. The guarded application is the only way an" (cons "   elaboration applies a rule." (cons "4. **Verifiers** (`LLT4010`). `{\"kind\":\"verifier\",\"name\":V,\"subject\":" (cons "   {x:X},\"candidate\":{r:C},\"specification\":Spec,\"check\":v,\"sound\":T," (cons "   \"complete\":U?}`: `Spec` takes `(X, C)` to `Prop`, `v` is an executable" (cons "   definition taking `(X, C)` to `bool`, `T` states exactly `v x r = true ->" (cons "   Spec x r`, and `U` the converse. A verifier generates `V.sound :" (cons "   Sound v Spec` and, with `U`, `V.complete : Complete v Spec`." (cons "5. **Reasoners** (`LLT4010`). `{\"kind\":\"reasoner\",\"name\":E," (cons "   \"type_parameters\":[...]?,\"logic\":Luse?,\"observation\":{x:I},\"observe\":o?," (cons "   \"rules\":[...],\"strategy\":...,\"answer\":{\"name\":s,\"type\":R,\"value\":a}?," (cons "   \"verifier\":Vuse,\"claims\":[...]?,\"executable\":true?}`. A rule-based" (cons "   reasoner (strategy `forward` or `search`) names its logic, observed" (cons "   state `o : S` over `x`, at least one rule, all distinct, over exactly its" (cons "   logic at exactly its type arguments, and executable when it is, and an" (cons "   answer `a : option R` over the state; its verifier checks an `I`" (cons "   subject and an `R` candidate. A `generate_and_verify` reasoner names no" (cons "   logic, observed state, answer, rule, or claim; its candidates have its" (cons "   verifier's candidate type." (cons "6. **Strategies and bounds** (`LLT4011`). The closed strategies are" (cons "   `{\"kind\":\"forward\",\"fuel\":f}`, `{\"kind\":\"search\",\"order\":" (cons "   \"breadth_first\"|\"depth_first\",\"fuel\":f,\"frontier\":k,\"deduplicate\":bool?}`," (cons "   and `{\"kind\":\"generate_and_verify\",\"generator\":gen,\"budget\":n}`. Every" (cons "   bound is a `nat` term over the observation, and it is optional in the" (cons "   schema only so that a missing bound is named: a missing or non-`nat`" (cons "   fuel, frontier, or budget, a literal zero frontier, and deduplication" (cons "   over a state type that is not an ordered key type are `LLT4011`. The" (cons "   generator is a `list R` term over the observation." (cons "7. **Claims.** Claims are strictly sorted by kind, each at most once" (cons "   (`LLT4010`). `initial_invariant` names a theorem stating exactly `J o`" (cons "   (the logic must have an invariant). `terminates` is admitted only on a" (cons "   forward reasoner whose logic has a ranking and whose every rule states" (cons "   progress, with an initial invariant claimed when progress assumes the" (cons "   invariant (`LLT4011` otherwise); its theorem states exactly `Mu o < f`." (cons "   `observation_invariant` names a predicate `Rl` over the observation and" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },

definition{
  name: i0103,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   a state, `Rl : (I, S) -> prop`, and two theorems stating exactly `Rl x" (cons "   o` (the predicate holds of the observed state) and `Rl x s -> Rel s t" (cons "   -> Rl x t` (the logic's relation preserves it), so it holds of the final" (cons "   state of every run, which the generated `E.next_preserves_relation`," (cons "   `E.saturate_relation`, and `E.run_relation` (forward) or" (cons "   `E.follow_relation` (search) prove (`LLT4010` when the predicate has" (cons "   another signature or a theorem states another obligation)." (cons "   `answer_correct` names a theorem of exactly one of three statements over" (cons "   the answer term `a`, tried in this order: `forall x s v, a = some v ->" (cons "   Spec x v` (the answer is correct on every state); `forall x s v, J s ->" (cons "   a = some v -> Spec x v` (correct on every state satisfying the logic's" (cons "   invariant `J`, which holds of every state a run reaches given the" (cons "   initial invariant, so this form needs the logic's invariant and an" (cons "   `initial_invariant` claim, `LLT4010` otherwise); or `forall x s v, Rl x" (cons "   s -> a = some v -> Spec x v` (correct on every state `Rl` holds of, so" (cons "   `Spec` may depend on the observation `x` through `Rl`, which `J`, a" (cons "   predicate of the state alone, cannot express; this form needs the" (cons "   `observation_invariant` claim). Each form erases the verifier's check" (cons "   in the reasoner's own verdict (rule 9), the second and third through" (cons "   the premise the generated `E.run_invariant`, `E.saturate_invariant`, or" (cons "   `E.follow_invariant` (and their `_relation` counterparts) discharge. A" (cons "   generate-and-verify reasoner makes no claim (`LLT4010`)." (cons "8. **Elaboration.** Every reasoner elaborates, in a fixed order, to:" (cons "   `E.Step`, an inductive with one constructor per rule carrying its" (cons "   binding (generate-and-verify: `E.Step.candidate` carrying the accepted" (cons "   candidate); `E.Ledger`, a structure of the six counters `iterations`," (cons "   `attempts` (guard evaluations), `firings`, `expansions`, `verifications`," (cons "   and `frontier` (the frontier's peak); and definitions over them. A" (cons "   rule-based reasoner has `E.observe`; `E.fire s t`, one arm per rule," (cons "   each exactly the rule's guarded application; `E.replay` and `E.follow x" (cons "   trace`, the left fold of `E.fire` from `E.observe x` whose first step" (cons "   that does not fire is `invalid_step`; `E.extract`, the answer term;" (cons "   `E.accept x s`, the extracted answer if the verifier's check accepts it;" (cons "   and `E.answer x trace`, the accepted answer of the state a trace replays" (cons "   to. A forward reasoner adds `E.select` (the first rule whose guard holds," (cons "   in declared order, and of a binding rule the first candidate in list" (cons "   order), `E.attempts` (the guard evaluations `E.select` makes), `E.next`," (cons "   `E.saturate x` (the state-only `iterate_until` of `E.next` under the" (cons "   fuel), `E.run x` (the traced loop, counting each step's iterations," (cons "   attempts, and firings), `E.account x` (the run's ledger with the final" (cons "   scan and the one verification of a saturated run), `E.conclude`," (cons "   `E.verdict x`, and `E x`. A search reasoner adds `E.Node` and" (cons "   `E.Search`, the successors of each rule and their concatenation in" (cons "   declared order, `E.fresh` (with deduplication, against a set of visited" (cons "   states), `E.attempts` (every guard evaluation of an expansion)," (cons "   `E.searchStep` (pop the head node; answer it if accepted; else expand it," (cons "   breadth-first appending and depth-first prepending its successors, cut" (cons "   to the frontier bound, recording a cut as truncation), `E.run` (the" (cons "   `iterate_until` of `E.searchStep` under the fuel), `E.failure`, `E.verdict" (cons "   x`, and `E x`. A generate-and-verify reasoner has `E.candidates x` (the" (cons "   generator), `E.verify x c` (the candidate if the verifier's check" (cons "   accepts it), `E.Trial`, `E.try` (check the next candidate while fewer" (cons "   than the budget have been checked, else record truncation), `E.run x`" (cons "   (the left fold of `E.try` over the candidates), `E.failure`, `E.verdict" (cons "   x`, `E x`, and `E.answer`. The generator is a term evaluated in full" (cons "   before the budget applies, so the ledger accounts the verifications the" (cons "   budget limits and not the work of generating the candidates." (cons "9. **Verdicts and the runtime boundary** (`LLT4012`). `E.verdict x :" (cons "   result R reasoning_failure` and `E x : result (product R (list E.Step))" (cons "   reasoning_failure` answer only a candidate the verifier's check accepted," (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))

}

definition{
  name: i0104,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   or, under `answer_correct`, the extracted answer, which the claim's" (cons "   theorem proves correct on every state, or on every state of a run, which" (cons "   satisfies the invariant or the observation invariant (rule 7). A forward" (cons "   reasoner" (cons "   answers only from a saturated state; a forward run cut short by its fuel" (cons "   is `exhausted` whatever its partial state holds. A search or generation" (cons "   cut short by a bound is `exhausted` and never `unsolved`; a saturated" (cons "   reasoner with no answer, a search whose frontier emptied, and a generator" (cons "   that proposed nothing are `unsolved`; an answer the check refused is" (cons "   `rejected`. Executable code that reaches a rule's `N.conclusion`, or a" (cons "   reasoner's unverified answer, other than through the reasoner's own" (cons "   elaboration, is `LLT4012`, whether it is called, applied, folded," (cons "   iterated, bound to a name, or mentioned in a lambda, a branch, a rule's" (cons "   guard, an observation, or a generator. The members that read an answer" (cons "   from a state or a search their caller supplies are guarded: `E.extract`" (cons "   of every reasoner that does not claim its answer correct on every state" (cons "   (the first form of rule 7); and, of a reasoner whose claim erased the" (cons "   check but holds only under the invariant or the observation invariant" (cons "   (the second and third forms), also `E.accept`, which is then `E.extract`," (cons "   `E.conclude` of a forward reasoner, and `E.searchStep` of a search," (cons "   which accepts the nodes of a frontier it is given. An answer proved only" (cons "   under an invariant is unconstrained on the states outside it, which" (cons "   direct code may supply. The remaining members start from the observation" (cons "   (`E.answer`, `E.start`, `E.verdict`, `E.run`, `E x`) or yield no answer" (cons "   (states, steps, counts, the node successors), so they stay reachable;" (cons "   where the check was erased by the first form every state's answer is" (cons "   proved, and where no claim erased it `E.accept`, `E.conclude`, and" (cons "   `E.searchStep` run the verifier's check, so none of those is guarded." (cons "10. **The `reasoning_failure` type.** Its four nullary constructors" (cons "    `ReasoningFailure.exhausted`, `.unsolved`, `.rejected`, and" (cons "    `.invalid_step` are ordinary data, lowered to the pairs of `Prod Bool" (cons "    Bool` `(false, false)`, `(false, true)`, `(true, false)`, and `(true," (cons "    true)`." (cons "11. **Generated theorems and the fixed reasoning semantics.** A module with" (cons "    a reasoning declaration imports the fixed, formal-only runtime module" (cons "    `LexLeanRuntime.Reasoning` (*Runtime modules*), namespace" (cons "    `LexLeanReasoning`: the definitions `Star`, `All`, `Preserves`," (cons "    `Sound`, `Complete`, `Reaches`, `Found`, and `SearchOk`, and lemmas" (cons "    proved by induction, `cases`, and core facts about natural numbers," (cons "    none of which depends on any axiom, as pinned Lean's `#print axioms`" (cons "    reports. Every logic," (cons "    rule, verifier, and reasoner generates theorems whose statements are" (cons "    fixed shapes over its elaboration and whose proofs are one of the closed" (cons "    templates of the snapshot schema, with names substituted, applying only" (cons "    runtime lemmas and the declaration's own and named theorems: guarded" (cons "    application soundness and progress, firing, replay and derivation" (cons "    (`E.derivation : Reaches Rel (E.observe x) (E.follow x trace)`)," (cons "    invariant preservation, the traced and state-only loops agreeing" (cons "    (`E.run_state`), the trace of every run replaying to its state" (cons "    (`E.run_trace`), the bounds the fuel, the frontier, and the budget give" (cons "    (a forward run's iterations and firings, a search's iterations," (cons "    verifications, expansions, and frontier peak, a generation's" (cons "    verifications, each at most its bound; the guard evaluations of a run and" (cons "    the firings of a search count work over a rule's candidate list, a user" (cons "    term, so they are accounted in the ledger and decided counter by counter" (cons "    for the example's runs, not bounded by a theorem)," (cons "    saturation under a `terminates` claim (`E.saturates`), and answer" (cons "    soundness (`E.verdict_sound : forall v, E.verdict x = ok v -> Spec x v`" (cons "    and `E.explained`: an explained answer replays from its trace and meets" (cons "    the specification). A generated theorem stated as an ordinary" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0105,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "    proposition is registered as a prior theorem, so later proofs may" (cons "    `apply` it. Each is allowed its owner's axioms, and their union equals" (cons "    the owner's exact policy (§22.6). `examples/reasoning` produces, and" (cons "    verifies, every runtime name and every template, and `RS-08` fails if" (cons "    one is produced by none of its declarations." (cons "12. **Identity, accounting, document, snapshot, and production.** Reasoning" (cons "    declarations are linked IR and part of the semantic ID (§21.4); every" (cons "    source node, elaborated declaration, obligation, and generated theorem" (cons "    is charged to `max_ir_nodes`. An elaboration is larger than its source" (cons "    by a bounded factor (a rule use in a reasoner is a few source nodes and" (cons "    about a hundred elaborated ones), so each logic, rule, verifier, and" (cons "    reasoner is charged for the most its elaboration can be, computed from" (cons "    its source terms and types and the number of rules it names, *before*" (cons "    any of it is built, running across the link like an artifact's decoded" (cons "    value (rule 2); exceeding the limit is `LLS8002` naming the declaration" (cons "    and \"before elaborating\". The bound is fitted to the committed shapes (many rules, wide" (cons "    states and bindings, large terms, and large type arguments of every" (cons "    use of a logic, rule, or verifier) and checked after the elaboration" (cons "    is built: an elaboration that exceeded it is a defect of the bound," (cons "    reported as the internal error `LLT4001` with the prefix `internal:`," (cons "    never a silent overrun. It is not a proof that no admitted input" (cons "    exceeds it. The rules" (cons "    of a reasoner are combined as balanced trees, in their declared order," (cons "    wherever the elaboration combines them (selection, guard-evaluation" (cons "    counts, successors, and the proof that successors replay), so the" (cons "    nesting of an elaboration, which every later pass recurses on, grows" (cons "    with the logarithm of the number of rules and not with the number. A language-1.2 snapshot carries each" (cons "    reasoning declaration's elaboration with its generated theorems (name," (cons "    type parameters, parameters, statement, and template). The canonical" (cons "    document renders each as a closed catalog (interfaces, rules in" (cons "    priority order, strategy and bounds, claims with the theorems that" (cons "    discharge them, obligations, elaborated declarations, and generated" (cons "    theorems with their templates); it shows no trace value and no document" (cons "    text says \"verified\". Inference rules and reasoners are realized through" (cons "    their elaborations, logics and verifiers are erased, and the runtime is" (cons "    in no production closure (§17.13); a root's eligibility report records" (cons "    each reasoner it runs with its strategy, bounds or budget, rules, ledger" (cons "    counters, and bounding theorems." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.cont,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

      (cons "`examples/reasoning` is the committed instance: a multi-step clinical forward" (cons "derivation with its invariant and termination, a generic terminating" (cons "countdown, a deduplicating breadth-first planner, a depth-first search and a" (cons "generate-and-verify reasoner over model-generated candidates, and an answer" (cons "proved correct on every state its run reaches, with its check erased, all" (cons "verified by pinned Lean, with the six-counter ledger of each run decided" (cons "counter by counter by Lean's kernel." (nil String))))))))

definition{
  name: i0108,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A text component models a step of a language-processing system: a" (cons "*tokenizer* turns text into tokens and back, a *context window* bounds the" (cons "tokens a model sees, and a *text generator* extends a prompt one token at a" (cons "time with a model's scores. As with models and reasoning machines, a text" (cons "declaration carries no meaning of its own: linking elaborates it into ordinary" (cons "language-1.2 definitions, inductives, and generated theorems, which the" (cons "ordinary rules check again, and the Lean backend, production eligibility, and" (cons "the axiom audit read only the elaboration. The universal laws are not stated" (cons "by the declaration and not spliced as Lean text: they are the declarations of" (cons "the embedded text-laws library, one native `lexlean/core-module/2` module" (cons "`LexLeanText.LexLeanText` of the project `language/text-1.2/library/` (§17.10)," (cons "which the compiler embeds, hashes into the language-1.2 semantics, and" (cons "generates Lean from, and which a module that declares a text component" (cons "imports. No prompt string is semantic code: `string` is data, and a prompt" (cons "reaches a model only as the tokens `encodeText` makes of it." (nil String))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0109,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **Closed declarations** (`LLT4001`). `tokenizer`, `context_window`," (cons "   `text_generator`, and `text_classifier` are declarations of" (cons "   `lexlean/semantic-module/2`; a member, scheme, disposition, policy," (cons "   generator, or distribution outside the closed schema is a schema" (cons "   violation. A declaration's `axioms` row is the exact axiom policy of every" (cons "   Lean declaration it elaborates to, sorted, unique, and qualified" (cons "   (`LLT4006`)." (cons "2. **Tokenizers** (`LLT4006`). `{\"kind\":\"tokenizer\",\"name\":T,\"scheme\":S," (cons "   \"vocabulary\":{\"member\":A}}` names a prior artifact of role `vocabulary` and" (cons "   schema `utf8_lines`; the scheme is `byte_level` or `longest_match`." (cons "   Tokens are `uint32`: the ids 0..255 are the single bytes, always present" (cons "   as the byte fallback, and the vocabulary's pieces take the ids from 256 in" (cons "   artifact order. `T` elaborates to `T.pieces`, `T.size`, `T.encode` and" (cons "   `T.decode` over `bytes`, and `T.encodeText` and `T.decodeText` over" (cons "   `string` through UTF-8, and generates `T.roundtrip` (`decode (encode b) =" (cons "   some b` for every byte string), `T.text_roundtrip`, and `T.in_range`" (cons "   (every token `encode` makes is below `T.size`)." (cons "3. **The laws library.** Every library function an elaboration applies is" (cons "   registered with its signature, so its applications type-check like any" (cons "   call, and every generated theorem is proved by one library lemma applied" (cons "   after unfolding exactly the elaborated definitions it names; the loader" (cons "   refuses a native module that does not declare every name an elaboration" (cons "   applies." (cons "4. **Context windows.** `{\"kind\":\"context_window\",\"name\":W,\"capacity\":n," (cons "   \"overflow\":D}` elaborates to `W.capacity : nat`, `W.fit : list uint32 ->" (cons "   option (list uint32)`, and `W.push : list uint32 -> uint32 -> option (list" (cons "   uint32)`. Under `refuse` a context that does not fit is `none`; under" (cons "   `keep_last` the most recent `n` tokens are kept. It generates" (cons "   `W.bounded` and `W.push_bounded` (every admitted or pushed context fits)," (cons "   `W.exact` (a context that fits is admitted unchanged), and `W.suffix`" (cons "   under `keep_last` (what is kept is the end of the context) or" (cons "   `W.refuses` under `refuse` (a context that does not fit is not admitted)." (cons "5. **Bounds** (`LLT4016`). A window's capacity and a generator's" (cons "   `max_tokens` are at least 1: a text component never runs unbounded or" (cons "   with an unstated bound. The schema admits any non-negative integer there" (cons "   and the compiler refuses 0 with `LLT4016`, so the one rule is stated by" (cons "   the code that enforces it; a negative value is a schema violation" (cons "   (`LLT4001`)." (cons "6. **Text generators** (`LLT4006`). `{\"kind\":\"text_generator\",\"name\":G," (cons "   \"tokenizer\":T,\"window\":W,\"scorer\":{\"member\":M},\"max_tokens\":k,\"stop\":s," (cons "   \"policy\":P}` names a prior tokenizer, a prior context window, a prior" (cons "   stateless model whose contract maps `list uint32` to `list int` and whose" (cons "   evidence discharges every runtime check (it is applied on every step)," (cons "   and a prior definition of one `uint32` token. The scorer's width is not" (cons "   compared with the vocabulary size when linking: a choice outside the" (cons "   vocabulary is the runtime `unknown_token`. It elaborates to `G.score`," (cons "   the scorer; the closed inductives `G.End` (`stop`, `exhausted`) and" (cons "   `G.Failure` (`unknown_token`, `context_overflow`, `invalid_utf8`), the" (cons "   members of `text_failure`; `G.run`, the library's loop: the window admits" (cons "   the prompt, then each step scores the context, chooses a token by the" (cons "   policy, ends at the stop token, refuses a choice that is not a token of" (cons "   the vocabulary (`unknown_token`), and pushes the token through the window" (cons "   (`context_overflow` when `refuse` refuses it), for at most `k` steps, the" (cons "   last of which ends `exhausted` and keeps its output; `G.generateTokens`," (cons "   the run read into `result (product (list uint32) G.End) G.Failure`; and" (cons "   `G.generate`, the same over `string`, whose output that is not UTF-8 is" (cons "   `invalid_utf8`. It generates `G.bounded` (at most `k` tokens are" (cons "   generated) and `G.context_bounded` (the run equals the run whose scorer" (cons "   is never consulted on a context that does not fit the window)." (cons "7. **Explicit nondeterminism.** Under `greedy` the choice is the first" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0110,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "   maximum and no elaborated definition takes a seed, so a greedy generator" (cons "   is deterministic by type. Under `sample` the generator is `xorshift32`" (cons "   (Marsaglia's 13, 17, 5 triple over `uint32`) and the distribution" (cons "   `proportional` (a negative score weighs nothing; the draw is the next" (cons "   seed modulo the total weight against the prefix sums, and with no weight" (cons "   the first maximum): `G.run`, `G.generateTokens`, and `G.generate` take the" (cons "   seed as their last input and return the next seed with their result, so" (cons "   the same prompt and seed give the same output. No claim is made about the" (cons "   quality of the generator, and `0` is its fixed point." (cons "8. **Classifiers** (`LLT4006`). `{\"kind\":\"text_classifier\",\"name\":C," (cons "   \"tokenizer\":T,\"model\":{\"member\":M}}` names a prior tokenizer and a prior" (cons "   stateless model whose contract maps `list int` to its label type and whose" (cons "   evidence discharges every runtime check (it is applied on every text). It" (cons "   elaborates to `C.features : list uint32 -> list int`, the count of each" (cons "   token id below `T.size` in order, so that it has exactly `T.size` entries;" (cons "   `C.classifyTokens : list uint32 -> L`, the model applied to the features;" (cons "   and `C.classify : string -> L`, the same through `T.encodeText`, so that a" (cons "   text reaches the model only as tokens. It generates `C.features_width`" (cons "   (`C.features` has exactly `T.size` entries), `C.features_total` (the" (cons "   features of an encoded text sum to its token count, so no token is" (cons "   dropped, which `T.in_range` makes true because every token indexes the" (cons "   vocabulary), and `C.features_fit` (the library's fixed-width map" (cons "   `fitWidth T.size`, which pads a list of integers with zeros or truncates it" (cons "   to `T.size` entries, leaves the features unchanged, so a linear or neural" (cons "   stage whose evidence states its input width can be applied to an arbitrary" (cons "   `list int`). The `axioms` row is the exact policy of everything it" (cons "   elaborates to, as for a tokenizer." (cons "9. **Composition and adapters** (`LLT4006`). Text components compose with" (cons "   the model constructs and add no declaration kind. A stateful model whose" (cons "   state is a `list uint32`, whose step is `W.push` of a context window `W`," (cons "   and whose contract's postcondition states that the pushed context fits" (cons "   (`fitsOption` of `W.capacity`, a function of the laws library, which a" (cons "   statement may name like any registered function) composes under `scan` like" (cons "   any stateful model, its `satisfies_contract` evidence proved by the" (cons "   generated `W.push_bounded`. The proof is of the postcondition and not of" (cons "   an invariant's preservation: a preservation obligation has the premise" (cons "   `Invariant state →` and a theorem's `apply` proof is one `exact`, and the" (cons "   proof language has no `Option` case on a non-local (the push), so the" (cons "   invariant form has no `apply` proof from `push_bounded` today." (cons "   An adapter is a model over `string` whose realization applies" (cons "   `C.classify` of a classifier or `G.generate` of a generator, and whose" (cons "   `satisfies_contract` evidence is proved from the generated laws of that" (cons "   classifier or generator; a realization that does not meet its contract" (cons "   fails verification like any other model, and one that applies a" (cons "   declaration of another kind is refused." (cons "10. **No prompt is code.** No operation of the closed language maps a" (cons "   `string` or `bytes` operand to a function, a term, or source: every" (cons "   primitive that takes either has a data result, the primitive registry and" (cons "   the production registry's primitive rows list the same operations, and a" (cons "   text declaration of `lexlean/semantic-module/2` states no member of type" (cons "   `string`. A prompt is data, and reaches a model only as the tokens" (cons "   `encodeText` makes of it." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.cont,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0113,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. **Typing.** `to_int` takes one operand of a fixed-width integer type" (cons "   (`Int8`, `Int16`, `Int32`, `Int64`, `UInt8`, `UInt16`, `UInt32`, or" (cons "   `UInt64`) and has result `Int`. `to_nat` takes one operand of an unsigned" (cons "   fixed-width integer type (`UInt8`, `UInt16`, `UInt32`, or `UInt64`) and has" (cons "   result `Nat`. Any other operand type, result type, or argument count is" (cons "   rejected in linking (`LLT4001`) before either backend runs." (cons "2. **Elaboration.** A widening conversion is a total, exact conversion of its" (cons "   operand's value in Lean (`Int` and `Nat` are unbounded). Linking elaborates" (cons "   each application into an ordinary language-1.2 term before Lean rendering," (cons "   production eligibility, or production lowering runs: the operand is bound" (cons "   once to a generated local `__widen<n>` (`n` counted in evaluation order" (cons "   within each declaration), each bit $k$ of the width is tested with `bit_and`" (cons "   and `equal` against the fixed-width mask $2^k$, and the bit weights are" (cons "   summed in a balanced tree over `Int` (`subtract` and `negate`, with the sign" (cons "   bit of a signed width weighted by $-2^{w-1}$ and bit 63 of `UInt64` expressed" (cons "   as $2^{62} + 2^{62}$ so every literal lies within 64-bit signed range; in the" (cons "   production calculus `Int` is 64-bit signed, so `to_int` on a `UInt64` at or" (cons "   above $2^{63}$ overflows the calculus's `Int64` range at the sum of those two" (cons "   $2^{62}$ terms, while `to_nat` on `UInt64` is total in both, `conformance_sm_32`)" (cons "   or over `Nat` (`add`)." (cons "   Generated Lean, production eligibility, production lowering, and the" (cons "   preservation certificates read the elaborated declaration, so a root that" (cons "   indexes a `Bytes` value and widens the resulting `UInt8` lowers to bit" (cons "   tests and exact sums with no decimal text (`parse_decimal` or" (cons "   `format_decimal`). The canonical LaTeX document renders the source" (cons "   conversion (`toInt`, `toNat`), and the snapshot records the declaration's" (cons "   `elaboration` alongside the source declaration (`SM-32`)." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

A language-1.1 project becomes a" (cons "language-1.2 project only by changing its `language` row, rewriting each" (cons "`semanticdata` discriminator from `/1` to `/2`, changing builtin glossary" (cons "references from `1.1.0` to `1.2.0`, and regenerating the lock with" (cons "`lexlean lock`. No command migrates a project" (cons "implicitly: building an" (cons "unmigrated project under a newer compiler keeps its declared language and its" (cons "historical identities and artifact bytes." definition{
  name: i0127,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "- *targets*, each a machine profile with `allocation` (whether heap" (cons "  allocation exists) and the bit widths of the natural-number and" (cons "  mathematical-integer representations. Language 1.2 registers `rust-core`" (cons "  (no allocation, 64-bit widths) and `rust-std` (allocation, 64-bit widths);" (cons "- *effects*: `allocation` (heap storage whose exhaustion stops the" (cons "  realization with an explicit failure), `overflow` (a `Nat` or `Int` value" (cons "  outside the target width stops the realization with an explicit failure," (cons "  never wrapping or truncating), and `recursion` (a stack depth bounded only" (cons "  by the input; exhausting the stack aborts the process, and no failure is" (cons "  stated for it);" (cons "- *constructs*: one row per semantic construct kind (`type.<kind>`," (cons "  `term.<kind>`, `primitive.<operation>`, `declaration.<kind>`, the" (cons "  refinements `declaration.inductive.recursive` and" (cons "  `declaration.definition.recursive`, and `constructor.nat_succ`) with its" (cons "  `disposition` (`runtime`, `formal-only`, or `erased`), whether it requires" (cons "  `allocation`, the representations (`nat`, `int`) for which its result may" (cons "  `overflow`, and whether it implies `recursion`. A construct requires" (cons "  allocation when its values live on the heap (strings, byte strings, lists," (cons "  maps, sets, a type that contains itself) or when it builds new heap" (cons "  storage; reading, comparing, measuring, or folding storage that already" (cons "  exists requires none, since the type of that storage already does. Each" (cons "  runtime row's `allocation` equals its realization's (§17.14)." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{

    arguments: [
      (cons "**Closure.** Eligibility is decided after linking and before any backend runs," (cons "over the root's *runtime closure*: the root and, transitively across modules," (cons "every definition it calls or references and every instance it resolves, each" (cons "instantiated at its type arguments (monomorphization). Every type the closure" (cons "realizes is visited through the declarations it names, under their" (cons "instantiation. Termination evidence theorems are *erased* dependencies:" (cons "recorded, never realized, and never walked. Proofs and theorem statements are" (cons "never part of a runtime closure." (nil String)))))))))
    ],

}

The `contract_violation` type is `runtime` plain data, and" (cons "`less_than` a `runtime` key comparison." (nil String))))))))))))

The" (cons "`reasoning_failure` type is `runtime` plain data. The `LexLeanReasoning`" (cons "runtime and every generated theorem are formal-only and in no closure. A" (cons "reasoner's search is bounded by its explicit fuel, frontier, or budget, whose" (cons "terms are ordinary closure members, and a natural-number counter of its" (cons "ledger is subject to the same width rule as any natural number, so a root" (cons "that runs a reasoner admits `overflow` where the target requires it." definition{

  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Rules.** A root is eligible for a target exactly when none of the following" (cons "holds; otherwise `check` fails with `LLT4005`, naming the root, the target, the" (cons "violation nearest the root, the closure member it occurs in, and the call path" (cons "that reaches it:" (nil String)))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0134,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. a root parameter or result holds, directly or inside the fields of a named" (cons "   type, a universe, a proposition, an uninstantiated type parameter, or a" (cons "   function — a root takes and returns first-order data only, so a closure can" (cons "   never escape it;" (cons "2. the closure reaches a construct whose disposition is `formal-only` or" (cons "   `erased`, a declaration that is not a definition, a definition that is not" (cons "   declared `executable`, or a dependency that does not resolve. Linking" (cons "   already refuses a call to a theorem, an unresolved reference, and a" (cons "   non-executable callee of an executable definition (§17.12), so from linked" (cons "   source only the root itself can be non-executable; the analysis checks" (cons "   every case regardless, and its unit tests exercise the others on IR that" (cons "   bypasses linking;" (cons "3. a natural-number or mathematical-integer literal in the closure lies" (cons "   outside the target width;" (cons "4. the closure requires allocation and the target provides none;" (cons "5. the closure realizes an effect the root does not admit. The realized" (cons "   effects are `allocation` when the closure requires allocation on a target" (cons "   that provides it, `overflow` for every construct whose registry row names" (cons "   the representation of its result, and `recursion` for every recursive" (cons "   definition and every construct whose registry row implies it." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0135,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "Two realization obligations follow from these rules and bind every later" (cons "production stage. A `Nat` or `Int` value crossing a root boundary is" (cons "represented in the target width, so a caller cannot supply a value outside it," (cons "and every value the realization computes outside it is the `overflow`" (cons "failure, never a wrapped or truncated value. A primitive realized by a" (cons "runtime item is a loop and carries no recursion. A primitive realized by a" (cons "library template (§17.14: the map, set, fold, iteration, and graph" (cons "primitives) carries the recursion of the template's rendering: a template" (cons "whose function calls itself only in tail position is rendered as a loop" (cons "(§17.16) and uses no stack for the call, so the folds, the iterations, and the" (cons "lookups (`list_fold`, `map_fold`, `set_fold`, `iterate`, `iterate_until`," (cons "`map_lookup`, `map_contains`, `set_contains`, `graph_successors`) carry none," (cons "while a template with a call whose result is used (an insertion, a removal, a" (cons "union, an intersection, a difference, the key and value lists, and the graph" (cons "searches) is rendered as a call and carries it, because the depth of its calls" (cons "is bounded only by its input. The registry states exactly that, and" (cons "`conformance_tc_06` derives it from the realization table and `RB-14` from the" (cons "rendering, rather than trusting the row. A function all of whose self calls are" (cons "tail calls is rendered as a loop (§17.16) and does not recurse, whether it is a" (cons "template or a definition of the project, so the registry's `recursion` for a" (cons "recursive definition (`declaration.definition.recursive`) means *recursion" (cons "unless the lowering makes every such definition a loop*. Eligibility therefore" (cons "lowers the closure of a root whose walk found the effect, with the report the" (cons "walk produced, and requires `recursion` of the root only if the lowered program" (cons "recurses: some function of it is reachable from itself through calls and" (cons "closures other than the self tail calls rendered as loops" (cons "(`realization::program_recurses`). A root that does not lower there keeps the" (cons "registry's answer, which never admits less. A root whose closure applies a" (cons "primitive that carries `recursion`, or has a definition that recurses by a call" (cons "that is not a tail call or that is mutual, admits it; a declaration may admit" (cons "more than the analysis requires, as for every effect." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0136,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The analysis is a conservative over-approximation of effects: it may require a" (cons "root to admit an effect its realization never exhibits, and it never admits a" (cons "root whose realization could exhibit an effect, a construct, or a value" (cons "representation that the declaration does not cover. Production compilation" (cons "never approximates or erases computational meaning to admit a root." (nil String))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0137,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Report.** Every module with a production root has the build artifact" (cons "`production/<full-module-path>.eligibility.json`" (cons "(`lexlean/production-eligibility/1`, `schemas/production-eligibility.schema.json`)," (cons "recorded in the manifest with kind `production-eligibility`. Per root it" (cons "records the declared effects, the runtime closure in discovery order with each" (cons "member's construct, type arguments, and shortest call path, every realized type" (cons "with its construct, the erased dependencies, every construct with the closure" (cons "members using it, and, per target, each realized effect with every" (cons "`(construct, instance)` source and every natural-number or integer" (cons "representation crossing the root boundary (`parameter <name>` or `result`," (cons "directly or inside a container or a named type's fields) with the width in" (cons "which the target realizes it, so the first realization obligation above is" (cons "stated per root. A root whose closure runs a reasoner also records, in" (cons "`reasoning`, one row per reasoner: its qualified name, its strategy" (cons "(`forward`, `breadth_first`, `depth_first`, or `generate_and_verify`)," (cons "whether a search deduplicates, the canonical semantic JSON of its fuel, or of" (cons "its budget, and of its frontier, its rules qualified and in priority order," (cons "the six counters of its ledger, and the generated theorems that bound them" (cons "(`E.iterations_bounded`, `E.frontier_bounded`, `E.verifications_bounded`," (cons "`E.saturates`), so the search a production claim relies on is explicit. Each" (cons "member records its shortest call path, so the" (cons "report grows with the square of the length of a chain of calls and the cube of" (cons "a chain of roots; it counts toward `max_total_source_bytes` and a module whose" (cons "report passes it is `LLS8002` (§17.17 *Limits*). The report is a deterministic" (cons "function of the linked semantic modules and the registry. Verification checks every root's" (cons "runtime closure and erased dependencies against the closure Lean's own" (cons "compiler front end extracts (§22.10); any difference fails verification." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))

definition{

  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "Production compilation targets one closed calculus, defined before any" (cons "optimizer and independently of every renderer. The calculus is LexLean: the" (cons "language-1.2 project `compiler/` defines its syntax (`TargetSyntax`), its" (cons "denotation (`TargetSemantics`), encoders of LexLean collection values" (cons "(`TargetOracle`), the reasoning declarations the reasoning fixtures are" (cons "compared with (`ReasoningOracle`, `GradeOracle`, `BudgetOracle`," (cons "`PlannerOracle`, and `ScreeningOracle`, §17.15), and the statements about its" (cons "fixtures (`TargetFixtures`, and `ReasoningFixtures` for the reasoning" (cons "fixtures, so neither module outgrows the project's file limit). Lean" (cons "elaborates, replays, and axiom-audits these modules like any other" (cons "LexLean program, and `compiler/` passes the verify, golden, and" (cons "reproducibility gates of §28.6. No meaning is read from rendered Rust text." (nil String)))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []

}

definition{
  name: i0141,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Source.** The calculus and the machine of §17.16 are source: the committed" (cons "`.lex.tex` modules `TargetSyntax`, `TargetSemantics`, `TargetOracle`," (cons "`RustSyntax`, `RustSemantics`, the reasoning oracles, and `Main`, and the" (cons "project's configuration (which is the production example's under its own name," (cons "checked against it), are written and edited as `.lex.tex` (§17.21). No program" (cons "generates or compares them with its own output. What is generated from them," (cons "and compared byte for byte by `cargo xtask check-calculus`, are the fixtures and" (cons "the modules that state them, the Rust packages, and the shipped copies of the" (cons "calculus modules; and what is checked against them are the renderer's own" (cons "tables: `conformance_sp_07` reads the runtime items and their failure and heap" (cons "classes from the committed `RustSyntax` and `RustSemantics` and equates them" (cons "with `lexlean::calculus::rust::runtime::Item`."     ],

definition{
  name: i0142,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Programs.** A program (`lexlean/target-program/1`," (cons "`schemas/target-program.schema.json`) is a list of algebraic data types, each" (cons "a non-empty list of constructors given by their field types, and a list of" (cons "monomorphic functions, each with parameter names, parameter types, a result" (cons "type, and a body. Types are `unit`, `bool`, `nat`, `int`, the fixed widths" (cons "`u8`..`u64` and `i8`..`i64`, `string`, `bytes`, `ordering`, `option`," (cons "`result`, `list`, `pair`, an indexed ADT, and `fn` with at least one" (cons "parameter. Expressions are a literal, a local, `let`, `cond`, an exhaustive" (cons "`match` over constructor shapes, `build` of a shape, a direct `call`, a" (cons "`closure` of a function over a proper prefix of its parameters, `apply`, a" (cons "primitive `prim`, `first`, `second`, and `field` of a single-constructor ADT." (cons "The primitives are natural and integer arithmetic and comparison, checked" (cons "fixed-width arithmetic, bitwise operations and shifts, scalar equality," (cons "Boolean connectives, append, length, index, and slice, UTF-8 encoding and" (cons "decoding, byte comparison, exact split and join, decimal formatting and" (cons "parsing, fixed-width conversion, and `compare`, the total key order of" (cons "§17.12 (numeric, `false` before `true`, strings by scalar sequence, pairs" (cons "lexicographically) returning an `ordering`. Locals are natural numbers" (cons "scoped lexically; there is no mutable state, no reference, and no aliasing:" (cons "every value is an immutable tree, and a byte string is an immutable buffer." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0143,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Static rules.** A program is valid exactly when every type names declared" (cons "ADTs, every local is bound, every `call`, `closure`, `apply`, `build`, and" (cons "primitive receives exactly the operand types of its signature, every match is" (cons "exhaustive and has no unreachable arm, every arm binds exactly its shape's" (cons "fields once, every function's body has its result type, and every literal has" (cons "its type and lies in its realization: `nat` in `[0, 2^64)`, `int` and the" (cons "fixed widths in their two's complement ranges, all spelled as canonical" (cons "decimals, bytes as lowercase hexadecimal. An ordering literal is admitted; a" (cons "closure literal is not. `lexlean::calculus::load` reads a program, checks" (cons "every rule, and returns its canonical form; any malformed member or violated" (cons "rule is `LLB6005`, and nothing is repaired or guessed." (nil String))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0144,
  parameters: [],

    arguments: [
      (cons "**Canonical form and identity.** The canonical form of a valid program renames" (cons "every local of a function to its first-binding order (parameters, then" (cons "binders in evaluation order), so alpha-equivalent programs have" (cons "byte-identical canonical JSON (§21.7). A program's identity is the SHA-256 of" (cons "its canonical bytes. An invalid program has neither." (nil String))))))
    ],

definition{
  name: i0145,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Denotation.** `TargetSemantics.run fuel program entry arguments` binds the" (cons "entry's parameters and evaluates its body by `eval`, structural recursion on" (cons "`fuel`. Operands evaluate left to right; a call evaluates its operands, then" (cons "the callee's body in a fresh environment of its parameters; `apply` passes a" (cons "closure's captures before its operands. Every primitive is defined by" (cons "reference to the language-1.2 primitive of the same meaning, so a value of" (cons "the calculus means what LexLean says it means, and `compare` is LexLean's own" (cons "map key order. The result is an `Outcome`: a value with its exact step count," (cons "`overflow` with its step count, `stuck`, or `exhausted`." (nil String))))))))))
    ],

(cons "It bounds the work of the Rust renderings below, each of which realizes every" (cons "step with work at most its charge. A `nat` or `int` result outside its" (cons "realization is `overflow`, never a wrapped or truncated value. The static" (cons "rules are designed so that a valid program is never `stuck`; the conformance" (cons "suite checks this on every fixture program at its stated arguments and at" (cons "seeded random well-typed arguments under sampled fuels, which is build" (cons "evidence, not a proof. `exhausted` reports only that the fuel bounded the" (cons "evaluation and is not an observation of the program." definition{
  name: i0149,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Realizations.** `lexlean::calculus::realization::TABLE` maps every construct" (cons "whose production disposition (§17.13) is `runtime` to the calculus elements" (cons "realizing it, and the conformance suite checks that the table and the" (cons "registry's runtime rows are in bijection and that every reference exists. A" (cons "map is its ascending entry list `List (Pair K V)`, a set its ascending element" (cons "list, and a graph its map from node to successor set, exactly the" (cons "representation of §17.12; every collection, fold, iteration, and graph" (cons "primitive is a library template (`lexlean::calculus::library`) written as a" (cons "transcription of the `LexLeanCollections` definition over `compare`," (cons "instantiated monomorphically. Agreement with LexLean is checked pointwise:" (cons "each template's fixtures state, and the kernel decides, that its outcome" (cons "equals LexLean's own primitive on that input, which is evidence on those" (cons "inputs, not a proof for all inputs. The model constructs are realized by the" (cons "elements of their elaborations (§17.12): an artifact, a realization, and a" (cons "model are functions (a model's body a call), a checked application is a" (cons "sequence of `let`s, validator calls, and conditionals building a `Result`, the" (cons "`contract_violation` type is a pair of Booleans, and `less_than` is `compare`" (cons "matched to a Boolean. A construct requires allocation exactly" (cons "when its realization does: its values are strings, byte strings, lists, or a" (cons "type that holds itself behind a heap handle (`indirection`), or it builds" (cons "such a value; reading, comparing, measuring, or folding existing storage" (cons "allocates nothing. The conformance suite checks every runtime row's" (cons "`allocation` against its realization. Lean's `&&` and `||` do not evaluate" (cons "a decided right operand, so they are realized by `cond`. A type parameter is" (cons "realized by its closed type argument (§17.13)." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],

    type_arguments: []

  executable: true
}

definition{

    arguments: [
      (cons "- `rust-core` is `#![no_std]` and declares no `extern crate`, so neither" (cons "  `alloc` nor `std` is linked and nothing is allocated. It renders exactly" (cons "  the programs that need no heap: a program whose realization requires" (cons "  allocation (a string, a byte string, a list, or a type that holds itself)" (cons "  is refused with the reason, never approximated." (cons "- `rust-std` uses the standard library. A string and a byte string are" (cons "  immutable shared buffers (`Rc<str>`, `Rc<[u8]>`); a list is persistent" (cons "  and laid out as a run of contiguous cells (the newest at the end of a" (cons "  buffer, each list a prefix of the buffer it was built in) with single" (cons "  cells in front of it: a `cons` onto a run whose buffer no other list" (cons "  holds extends the buffer in place, and a `cons` onto any other list is" (cons "  one cell, so building and taking apart a cell is constant work on every" (cons "  list, no buffer is written while another list can see it, and a list" (cons "  built by one consumer occupies one buffer; and an ADT field or a closure" (cons "  capture whose type holds its owner is boxed behind `Rc`, and nothing else" (cons "  is." (nil String)))))))))))))))))
