definition{
  name: i0155,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "In both, `nat` is `u64` and `int` is `i64`, each operation checked and every" (cons "out-of-range result the explicit `Err(Overflow)` propagated by `?`; the" (cons "fixed widths are the native integer types with `checked_*` operations;" (cons "orderings are `core::cmp::Ordering`; ADTs are enums; function values are" (cons "defunctionalized into one enum per function type; and using a local moves it" (cons "when nothing after the use reads it and otherwise clones a handle or a value" (cons "of fixed size, never a structure, so ownership never changes an observable" (cons "value and no step does hidden work. Every loop of the" (cons "runtime counts its iterations and every bulk copy counts its length in a" (cons "work counter, and on every fixture with an observable outcome the count" (cons "never exceeds the denotation's steps; the release of a list, whose cells were each counted when built, is" (cons "not counted again. Calls are Rust calls with no tail-call or stack-depth" (cons "guarantee, evaluation is single-threaded and sequential, the functions form" (cons "no stable ABI, and heap exhaustion aborts the process. Observable behavior is" (cons "the printed value or `overflow`; steps, fuel, and the work count are not" (cons "observable. The claim covers exactly programs of this calculus rendered this" (cons "way: no other Rust construct (unsafe code, foreign functions, threads," (cons "asynchronous code, floating point, interior mutability, input and output" (cons "beyond the harness) is within it. Every fixture whose entry takes and" (cons "returns first-order data is committed as a package in each profile that" (cons "admits it, under its lint gate (§17.16), and compared with the renderer by" (cons "`cargo xtask check-calculus`. Every fixture with an" (cons "observable outcome is rendered, compiled by the pinned `rustc` 1.97.1" (cons "(`RUSTC-1-97-1`, an authority this repository cites) with warnings denied," (cons "linked with a harness, and run; its output must equal the denotation's, its" (cons "work count must not exceed the steps, and planted value and work" (cons "discrepancies must be detected. This is `build` evidence for the renderings," (cons "not a proof of them; preservation of meaning from LexLean to the calculus is" (cons "§17.17, and from the calculus to Rust is a separate obligation." The kinds are" (cons "   observation, preprocessing, advice, retained state, dispatch, fallback," (cons "   communication, randomness, scheduling, and execution, and a charge is" (cons "   `steps`, a constant per invocation, `free`, or `undeclared`. The" (cons "   comparison boundary (§10.8) is `complete`, `prepared_state`, or" (cons "   `prepared_plan`, and the prepared artifacts bound in the common initial" (cons "   state of every competitor each name the preparation action that produced" (cons "   it and the SHA-256 of its bytes;" (cons "3. the universe U_sys (§8.3): a carrier, its completeness evidence, and its" (cons "   `SystemUniverseId`. The only admitted carrier is a grammar of complete" (cons "   systems: an argument type, a result type, plans shared by every system," (cons "   each with the preparation actions it needs before an invocation, and" (cons "   strictly increasing dispatch thresholds. Its members are exactly the fixed" (cons "   systems, one per plan, then for every threshold every dispatch from a" (cons "   small plan to a large plan, both ranging over every plan, in that order." (cons "   A system is the complete selector-plus-executor (§8.5): it realizes as one" (cons "   program whose entry function (index 0) is its selector and whose plans" (cons "   sit at indices 1 onward. The only admitted completeness evidence is" (cons "   `grammar_equality`, which cites the kernel theorem `Gnaf.expandComplete`." (cons "   The `SystemUniverseId` is the framed SHA-256 `lexlean-gnaf-universe-v1`" (cons "   of the problem, the machine, and the carrier in canonical JSON, computed" (cons "   from the request before any evaluation; a stated identity is only an" (cons "   equality check against it;" (cons "4. the objective and order (§9.2, §9.3): `scalar` is total cost over the" (cons "   domain, totally ordered, lower is better; `vector` is the pair of total" (cons "   cost and size, ordered componentwise with no weighting;" (cons "5. the claim class (§12.4), every class of the authority, with" (cons "   `profile_defined_comparison` carrying its profile, class, and result shape" (cons "   and `instance_optimal` its two constants, and the scope the claim ranges" (cons "   over: the grammar universe, all calculus programs, or all Rust programs."       (cons "1. an empty domain;" (cons "2. a carrier that is not a grammar: one system's internal plans taken as" (cons "   the universe (`internal_plan_universe`), an optimizer's output, the" (cons "   candidates a search discovered, or a cache;" (cons "3. completeness evidence that is missing, cites the universe's own" (cons "   identity, or cites an optimizer;" (cons "4. a request beyond the capacity its machine binds: more fuel, domain" (cons "   arguments, or systems than the capacity admits, or a declared charge" (cons "   above it (`beyond_capacity`);" (cons "5. the `unit` operand-size treatment, under which a primitive whose work" (cons "   grows with its operands is an unbounded action at unit cost" (cons "   (`unit_cost_operands`, §8.2);" (cons "6. an action kind accounted twice (`duplicate_action`);" (cons "7. an action some system performs (observation, dispatch, fallback," (cons "   execution) that is unaccounted, or charged other than by `steps`: the" (cons "   calculus counts that work as steps, so a zero, constant, free, or" (cons "   undeclared charge hides or replaces it (`hidden_cost`);" (cons "8. an admitted preparation action (preprocessing, advice, retained state)" (cons "   charged by `steps` (the calculus has no preparation phase whose steps it" (cons "   counts), by zero, or `undeclared` (`hidden_cost`), or `free` other than" (cons "   on a prepared boundary whose common initial state binds an artifact it" (cons "   prepared (`unbound_preparation`, §10.8); and an admitted communication," (cons "   randomness, or scheduling action, which the sequential deterministic" (cons "   calculus machine does not have, so no system of the grammar realizes it" (cons "   (`unrealizable_action`);" (cons "9. a prepared artifact of an action that is not admitted `free`, which" (cons "   would exclude work that is charged (`stray_prepared_artifact`);" (cons "10. a plan needing a preparation action the machine does not account" (cons "    (`unaccounted_action`);" (cons "11. `restricted_universe_optimal`, which §12.4 makes a legacy alias that a" (cons "    conforming answer never emits as a base class (`claim_alias`); a scalar" (cons "    claim (`global_optimal`, `argmin_complete`) over the vector order, a" (cons "    vector claim (`pareto_optimal`, `frontier_complete`) over the scalar" (cons "    order, or any other claim class, which this model does not decide" (cons "    (`unsupported_claim`);" (cons "12. a scope beyond the grammar universe. No coverage bridge from the grammar" (cons "    to all calculus programs or to all Rust programs is proved, so such a" (cons "    claim is refused (`uncovered_scope`) rather than inferred from the" (cons "    grammar's answer." definition{
  name: i0163,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Answer.** For a valid request, every system is evaluated on every domain" (cons "argument with the request's fuel. The first argument on which the system" (cons "does not return the reference's value decides: overflow, a stuck" (cons "evaluation, or a different value makes the system inadmissible, and an" (cons "exhausted evaluation leaves it unresolved. A system that agrees everywhere" (cons "is admitted with its cost, its total steps plus, on every invocation, the" (cons "declared constant of each preparation each plan it can run needs, and its" (cons "size, one per expression node and match arm of every function reachable" (cons "from its entry. A plan's preparation is therefore charged exactly to the" (cons "systems that can run it, and code a system cannot run is not its size. An" (cons "unresolved system is never removed and never given an infinite cost" (cons "(§8.4): any unresolved system makes the answer `incomplete`. Otherwise the" (cons "answer is `infeasible` when nothing is admitted; for `scalar`, `argmin`, every" (cons "admitted system attaining the minimum cost and that minimum; for `vector`," (cons "`frontier`, every admitted system no other strictly dominates. Both are" (cons "computed by the model's one generic order, `Gnaf.argmin` and" (cons "`Gnaf.frontier`, over rows of an identity and a cost; equal costs never" (cons "dominate each other, so every equal-cost system is kept (GNAF-REJ-21)." (cons "Because a dispatching system pays for its observation and selection, the" (cons "best internal plan and the per-input envelope of the plans are not complete" (cons "systems' costs, and the model never answers with them. `Gnaf.certifies`" (cons "accepts a claimed argmin or frontier exactly when it is the request's own," (cons "as an identity set, and `Gnaf.minimaAttained` says whether some admitted" (cons "system attains the componentwise minima of the admitted costs." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0164,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Host side.** `lexlean::gnaf` transcribes the model step for step: `load`" (cons "reads a request and checks what the model presupposes, reporting `LLB6006`" (cons "for a malformed request; a reference program, plan, or realized system that" (cons "is not valid under §17.14; a domain argument outside the reference entry's" (cons "parameter type or on which the reference returns no value under the" (cons "request's fuel; grammar argument and result types other than the entry's;" (cons "repeated thresholds; a plan preparing by an action that is not preparation," (cons "or naming one twice; and a stated universe identity other than the one the" (cons "request's components determine. `answer` evaluates it; and request, status," (cons "and answer terms are emitted so the kernel confirms each committed request." (cons "The host evaluator's capacity is part of the machine it offers: a machine" (cons "asking for, or a request using, more than fuel 4096, 1024 domain" (cons "arguments, 65,536 systems, or charges of 2^32 is `LLS8002`. Within that" (cons "capacity every declared charge fits the host's integers, so a step total" (cons "the host cannot represent is `LLS8002` as well, never a cost the model would" (cons "not report. Evaluation runs on a stack sized for the request's fuel, and a" (cons "panic of the evaluation thread is `LLI9001`." (nil String))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{

  parameters: [],

      (cons "**Fixtures.** The requests under `compiler/gnaf/` (`lexlean/gnaf-fixture/1`," (cons "`schemas/gnaf-fixture.schema.json`) each name a request and its expected" (cons "answer. They include a Pareto frontier of incomparable systems; ties from a" (cons "duplicated plan, in an argmin and in a frontier; GNAF-VEC-02 posed over the" (cons "calculus, four systems of which exactly three form the frontier; a" (cons "dispatching system that is the argmin although the best single plan is not" (cons "and although the per-input envelope of the plans is lower than any system" (cons "attains; an inadmissible plan excluded; an unknown cost making the answer" (cons "incomplete; a plan's preparation moving the argmin; prepared artifacts" (cons "bound in the common initial state; and a refused request for every rule" (cons "above. The generated module `GnafFixtures` (compared with its generator by" (cons "`cargo xtask check-calculus`) states for each request that `Gnaf.universeOf`" (cons "reduces to the host's expansion and `Gnaf.answer` to the expected answer," (cons "for each answered request that `Gnaf.statusesOf` reduces to the host's" (cons "statuses, and for the posed GNAF-VEC-02 that no frontier with a member" (cons "omitted is certified while the frontier in another order is, and that no" (cons "system attains the componentwise minima; each is decided by Lean's kernel." (cons "A wrong answer, an answer computed from a universe with a system omitted, a" (cons "tie with a member dropped, and a universe with a system omitted are" (cons "rejected by Lean. The GNAF schemas are generated with the fixtures, and" (cons "their calculus definitions are `schemas/target-program.schema.json`'s own." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0166,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Reasoning plans.** A reasoner's rule search is execution: every guard it" (cons "evaluates and every rule it fires is work of the system that runs it, charged" (cons "as calculus steps like any other work, and it is never an action charged" (cons "separately. The `compiler` project's oracle modules state exactly the declarations" (cons "(equal as structured data once definition lists are put in key order and the" (cons "oracle's own module name replaces the example's, not as bytes) of every" (cons "reasoner the reasoning example's production roots run: `ReasoningOracle`" (cons "those of `examples/reasoning/src/Clinic.lex.tex` (the forward reasoners" (cons "`Triage` and `Review`), `GradeOracle` the `Grade` reasoner of `Main` and what" (cons "it is declared with (over the clinical rules, whose module the oracle" (cons "imports under its own name), and `BudgetOracle`, `PlannerOracle`, and" (cons "`ScreeningOracle` the modules `Budget` (the generic forward reasoner `Spend`" (cons "and `Refund`), `Planner` (the breadth-first search with deduplication" (cons "`Plan`), and `Screening` (the depth-first search `Screen` and the" (cons "generate-and-verify reasoner `Dose`). Lean elaborates each there to the" (cons "definitions the example verifies (`RS-12`). The fixtures `reasoning-*`" (cons "(§17.14) hand-transcribe those elaborations to calculus programs, function" (cons "for function and through the realization library's `list_fold`," (cons "`set_insert`, `set_contains`, and `iterate_until` instances: the forward" (cons "engine of `Triage`, `Review`, and `Grade` (whose answer is proved correct," (cons "so its transcription runs no check), the generic `Spend` at a" (cons "natural-number payload with its binding rule, the node, frontier, visited" (cons "set, frontier cut, and truncation of `Plan` and `Screen`, and the budgeted" (cons "fold over `Dose`'s candidates. Each is stated equal to the oracle's verdict by Lean's kernel; the" (cons "fixtures of `Triage` (traced), `Plan`, `Screen`, and `Dose` are stated equal" (cons "to the oracle's explained answer, with the steps that reach it, and to the" (cons "six counters of the oracle's run, so the order a search expands its nodes" (cons "and tries its candidates in is decided by the kernel. Each charges at least" (cons "the guard evaluations and firings of the oracle reasoner's ledger on the" (cons "same argument in steps, a floor that a transcription searching nothing" (cons "fails and a generation, which fires no rule, meets with its verifications" (cons "alone. That ledger is the" (cons "one the example's committed theorems (`triage_ledger`, `plan_ledger`, and the" (cons "others) state for the run, which Lean's kernel decides counter by counter." (cons "The requests `reasoning-argmin` and `reasoning-frontier` pose an analogous" (cons "problem over three forward-chaining plans (priority-order chaining, one" (cons "sweep, and a goal-directed decision) over findings held as the bits of a" (cons "natural number, because GNAF realizations admit no algebraic types: the" (cons "plans that search more are charged more and the argmin is the plan that" (cons "searches least. Each plan is also a fixture, `reasoning-gnaf-priority`," (cons "`-sweep`, and `-goal`, whose function is the plan the request declares and" (cons "which Lean's kernel decides computes, on every patient of the request's" (cons "domain, the level the oracle's `Triage` derives from the same findings" (cons "(observed as the vital signs that state them), so the plans are, on that" (cons "domain, the problem `Triage` solves. `reject-reasoning-free-search`" (cons "(`hidden_cost`) charges the search nothing, and" (cons "`reject-reasoning-discovered-universe` (`discovered_universe`) takes the" (cons "candidates one search encountered as its universe; both are refused. No" (cons "reasoning claim is a claim over a universe its search discovered." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0167,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Authority vectors, dependency manifest, and honesty.** The authority's" (cons "normative fixtures GNAF-VEC-01 (the compositions of its operations, with" (cons "their path identities, and the S0 certificate invalid for S1), GNAF-VEC-02," (cons "GNAF-VEC-04, GNAF-VEC-17 (the pointwise envelope, the uniform worst case," (cons "and the fair randomized expectation), GNAF-REJ-14, and GNAF-REJ-29 (§16) are" (cons "theorems of `Gnaf` over the same `argmin`, `frontier`, and certification the" (cons "answers are computed by, decided by the kernel. `compiler/gnaf.manifest.json`" (cons "is the dependency manifest §20 requires: the draft's revision and SHA-256," (cons "every kind, operation, machine, cost, proof, interchange, and address profile" (cons "with its role, the certificate profiles of §17.18, every restriction of the" (cons "admitted universe, and the claim classes the model actually answers. An answer" (cons "enumerates the universe; a claim over it is certified without enumerating it" (cons "only by a certificate (§17.18), and an enumeration answer, a correct system," (cons "or a GNAF-normal one is never that certificate. The authority's requirements are" (cons "`some-true` here: reproduced from the cited document, a copy of which is" (cons "vendored at `model/authorities/UOR-GNAF-v1-draft.2.md` and whose SHA-256" (cons "`cargo xtask validate-model` recomputes. The model and its fixtures are" (cons "`build` evidence that LexLean realizes them, not a proof of the authority." (cons "UOR-NAF is informative to UOR-GNAF (§20) and is neither cited as evidence" (cons "nor relied upon. A request's answer is a statement about the grammar" (cons "universe under the calculus's step accounting; it is not a claim about" (cons "machine time, about calculus programs outside the grammar, or about Rust" (cons "programs." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,

}

definition{
  name: i0170,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**AST.** The AST (`rust::ast`) has no node for arbitrary text. Every" (cons "identifier is generated from a closed kind and an index (`v<n>` for a local" (cons "of the program, `m<n>` for a match scrutinee or a bound condition, `a<n>` for" (cons "an operand, `c<n>` for an applied closure, `r<n>` and `h<n>` for a value" (cons "taken out of a box or a pair, `k<n>` and `p<n>` for a closure's captures and" (cons "parameters, `f<n>` for a program function) or is an exported name the package" (cons "manifest declares. Every called function is a program function or a runtime" (cons "item of the closed table `rust::runtime::Item`; every type is a calculus type" (cons "or one of the representation types `Rc<T>` (a field or capture whose type" (cons "holds its owner), `R<T>` (a fallible result), and `&T` (a borrowed export" (cons "parameter). Every construct carries its *origin*: the calculus element" (cons "(§17.14) of the term, shape, or literal it was lowered from, or the" (cons "structural realization it is, and the width of a fixed-width element. The" (cons "constructs are every item, every binding, and every expression other than a" (cons "block, a list's `uncons`, and a predecessor, which are parts of the" (cons "construct around them; a pattern is checked through the match that carries" (cons "it." (cons "The printer lays out every item, block, and arm in one fixed way, so the" (cons "rendering's bytes are canonical without a formatter. A string literal is" (cons "written by LexLean's own escaper: printable ASCII other than `\"` and `\\` as" (cons "itself, those two escaped, and every other scalar as `\\u{..}`; a byte string" (cons "is an array of `0x..` bytes. No byte of a rendering depends on the Unicode" (cons "tables of the toolchain that built LexLean." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0172,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "- a unit carries nothing: a unit binder is `_`, a unit read is the literal" (cons "  `()`, a unit result type and a unit block value are left implicit, an" (cons "  operand that computes a unit is bound (`let () = e;`) in operand order" (cons "  before the call that receives `()`, and a fallible function or dispatch" (cons "  returning a computed unit binds it before `Ok(())`;" (cons "- a conditional or match whose branches are the Boolean literals is its" (cons "  condition or that condition's negation (a zero test negated is the" (cons "  nonzero test); a conditional whose branches are the same code up to the" (cons "  names they bind is its condition, evaluated and discarded, then that code;" (cons "  and a match each of whose arms rebuilds exactly what it matched is its" (cons "  scrutinee;" (cons "- a conditional on a Boolean literal and an `if` without `else` holding only" (cons "  another bind their (inner) condition first; an empty unit `else` is left" (cons "  implicit; a block that binds a value and returns that binding is the" (cons "  value; a field of a computed record binds the record first;" (cons "- a read of a binding is a move when nothing after it, on any path, reads" (cons "  the binding, and a clone otherwise; a projection of a pair component" (cons "  (`let (h, _) = v;`) moves the component out of the pair when nothing" (cons "  after it reads the pair whole or that component and every other component" (cons "  read after it is read once (so that each of those reads is a move too)," (cons "  and clones the pair otherwise. `rust::moves` decides this after lowering," (cons "  backwards through the evaluation order (a block's bindings then its tail," (cons "  operands left to right, a condition then the branch taken, a scrutinee" (cons "  then the arm taken, and a closure before the operands it is applied to;" (cons "  branches are alternatives). A move, a clone, and a copy all read the" (cons "  binding, so the choice is invisible to the denotation and to every" (cons "  correspondence row; it decides what the machine does, not what it" (cons "  computes;" (cons "- a type no value inhabits (a function type no closure of a live function" (cons "  inhabits, or a named type, pair, or result built only from such types):" (cons "  a function with a parameter of such a type is never called and its body is" (cons "  the empty match on that parameter; a match arm whose shape holds such a" (cons "  value is never taken and is omitted, as Rust's exhaustiveness admits; and" (cons "  any other computation of such a value is refused, as `LLB6005`" (cons "  (*unsupported type*), because Rust would make the code after it" (cons "  unreachable." definition{

  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [

A function that can overflow" (cons "returns `R<T>` and every call to it ends in `?`, except in tail position," (cons "where its result is returned as it is; any other function returns its value" (cons "and no call to it propagates. The operand of an `Err` that ends a loop which" (cons "is the value of a function that can overflow is in tail position: the loop" (cons "breaks with it, so it is the value the function returns. The length of a" (cons "sequence cannot exceed `2^64 - 1` on any machine that holds it, so `length`" (cons "cannot overflow." (nil String))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0176,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. *hygiene*: a name used but not bound, or bound twice in one function," (cons "   and an item declared twice;" (cons "2. *ownership*: a value moved twice, or read after it was moved, on any" (cons "   path; a pair component moved out twice, or a binding read or moved" (cons "   whole after a component was moved out of it; branches are alternatives;" (cons "3. *hidden allocation*: in `rust-core`, any heap type, heap runtime" (cons "   function, string or byte string literal, list construction or match, or" (cons "   box;" (cons "4. *arithmetic*: a fallible call that does not propagate outside tail" (cons "   position, an infallible call that propagates, a fallible function's tail" (cons "   that is not a fallible value, and a function that propagates a failure" (cons "   its result type does not carry;" (cons "5. *correspondence*, instance by instance: an emitted construct with no row" (cons "   in `validate::CORRESPONDENCE`, whose row does not name the element of its" (cons "   own origin, whose width (a fixed-width runtime function's or literal's)" (cons "   differs from its origin's, or whose origin the program does not use; and" (cons "   a type the crate names whose row names no element the program uses. The" (cons "   pair and result constructs that a loop's state and break carry (`type:pair`," (cons "   `type:result`, `destructure:pair`, `construct:pair`, `construct:ok`," (cons "   `construct:err`) name `expr:call` among their origins, so they are admitted" (cons "   in any program that calls, not only where a self tail call is rendered as" (cons "   a loop: the rule is coarser there by that much. The" (cons "   origin is derived from the term being lowered, independently of the" (cons "   construct chosen for it, so a call of `nat_mul` lowered from a `nat_add`" (cons "   term, or a `u16` addition lowered from a `u8` one, is refused even in a" (cons "   program that uses both. Types are checked against the program's elements" (cons "   as a whole. That the parts of a construct are composed faithfully (which" (cons "   arm is which, which function a call names, the order of captures and" (cons "   fields) is not a check of the AST: it is established by the conformance" (cons "   differential below, against which planted lowering mutations are" (cons "   recorded. A negated zero test or predecessor is refused as well: a zero" (cons "   test is negated as its complement, and the printer parenthesizes any" (cons "   comparison under `!`." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))

      (cons "**Packages.** A package manifest (`lexlean/rust-package/1`," (cons "`schemas/rust-package.schema.json`) names a crate and its version (three" (cons "canonical decimals, each at most `2^64 - 1`, as Cargo reads them; the schema" (cons "pattern and the renderer agree), a profile, a target program, the exported" (cons "functions, and its `sources`: the semantic IDs (§21.4) of the LexLean builds" (cons "whose linked IR states the program, as lowercase SHA-256 hex, strictly" (cons "ascending, and empty for a program no LexLean build states. The renderer" (cons "checks their form and records them; that they name the build stating the" (cons "program is established by the conformance suite against the verified build" (cons "(below). That the program faithfully realizes the semantic objects of that" (cons "build (realization-to-source preservation) is the obligation of the" (cons "production pipeline that derives target programs from LexLean roots, not of" (cons "this backend: `sources` binds a package to the build that states its program," (cons "and the backend never derives a program from a build. Its `effects` are effects" (cons "of §17.13, strictly ascending and registered, that the package states of" (cons "itself. For a package a production root exports they are the set eligibility" (cons "requires of the root on the profile, which eligibility decides and the renderer" (cons "does not derive; for a package no root exports (a fixture) they are the effects" (cons "its program shows, `overflow` for an export that can fail and `recursion` for a" (cons "function that reaches itself, never `allocation`. They are not the effects the" (cons "realization can exhibit: the argument check an entry carries recurses over a" (cons "recursive argument type, and the graph templates' `nat_add` fuel bound can" (cons "overflow, and neither is among a root's required effects (issue 105). The" (cons "renderer refuses a list that is unregistered, unordered, or states `allocation`" (cons "on `rust-core`. Each export names a" (cons "program function, its Rust name, how it takes each parameter (`own`;" (cons "`borrow`, by shared reference; or `copy`, only for a `Copy` type: a scalar," (cons "or an option, result, or pair of `Copy` types), and whether it can fail" (cons "(`none` or `overflow`). A package is refused, as `LLB6005`, when:" (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0181,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Scaffolding.** What a package says of itself beside its program is data, not" (cons "renderer text. The edition (2021), that the crate is not published, the gate" (cons "on rustc's lints, the Clippy group the gate starts from, and the" (cons "allowed lints, each with its Clippy group and its reason, are the declarations" (cons "of one language-1.2 semantic module, `RustPackage`, written in the authoring" (cons "notation at `language/package-1.2/library/src/RustPackage.lex.tex`. It is under `language/`," (cons "so the language-1.2 compiler-semantics ID (§21.2) covers it, and a changed" (cons "lint, edition, or gate changes LexLean's identity. The renderer reads it, and" (cons "neither evaluates it nor splices it into a rendering (the library's path is" (cons "not in it: `src/lib.rs` is the package's layout, `package::LIBRARY_PATH`, which" (cons "the provenance schema also fixes): `rust::scaffold` decodes" (cons "the module's semantic data, accepting exactly the shapes the module uses and" (cons "the declarations it names, and `package.rs` prints `Cargo.toml` over the" (cons "record. The crate attributes are derived, not stored: `#![no_std]` is the" (cons "target row of `language/production-1.2.toml` with `allocation = false`, and" (cons "`#![forbid(lint)]` restates each gate on rustc's lints at level `forbid` (so" (cons "`#![forbid(unsafe_code)]` and `unsafe_code = \"forbid\"` come from one row, and" (cons "a `lib.rs` built outside Cargo still forbids unsafe code). No source of the" (cons "renderer holds a string the module states (`scaffold::audit`). The module" (cons "states that no allowed lint is in Clippy's `correctness` group as a theorem" (cons "over the same list. The reader refuses a lint in that group and checks the" (cons "theorem by its shape (the predicate is false exactly on `correctness`, and the" (cons "theorem equates it over the list with `true`); the module is the project" (cons "`language/package-1.2/library`, whose check types it, and the kernel's replay" (cons "of the theorem's proof is that of `verify`, which no gate of this repository" (cons "runs on it." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],

}

definition{
  name: i0182,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "| Lint | Clippy group | Reason |" (cons "| --- | --- | --- |" (cons "| `type_complexity` | complexity | asks for a type alias: a generated type is exactly its calculus type, and an alias is a name with no calculus counterpart |" (cons "| `too_many_arguments` | complexity | asks to group parameters into a struct: a function takes exactly its calculus parameters, and the struct is a type with no calculus counterpart |" (cons "| `large_enum_variant` | perf | asks to box a large variant: boxing changes the representation and allocates, which rust-core cannot and the calculus does not |" (cons "| `result_large_err` | perf | asks to box a large error: boxing changes the representation and allocates, which rust-core cannot and the calculus does not |" (cons "| `result_unit_err` | style | asks for an error type in place of unit: a function returns exactly its calculus result type, and the error type has no calculus counterpart |" (cons "| `single_match` | style | asks for `if let` or `if` in place of a match with an empty arm: every calculus match states an arm for every shape, and `if let` leaves one implicit |" (cons "| `manual_unwrap_or` | complexity | asks for a library combinator in place of the program's own match: the combinator is outside the closed runtime |" (cons "| `manual_unwrap_or_default` | suspicious | asks for a library combinator in place of the program's own match: the combinator is outside the closed runtime, and its advice is a rewrite that preserves the match's meaning, so allowing it masks no defect |" (cons "| `manual_map` | style | asks for `Option::map` and a Rust closure in place of the program's own match: a calculus closure is defunctionalized, never a Rust closure |" (cons "| `manual_ok_err` | complexity | asks for a library combinator in place of the program's own match: the combinator is outside the closed runtime |" (nil String)))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.table,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0186,
  parameters: [],

    arguments: [
      (cons "**Evidence.** Every fixture whose entry takes and returns first-order data is" (cons "committed as a package under `compiler/rust/<target>/<fixture>/` in each" (cons "profile that admits it, exporting its entry as `run`, with two more that pass" (cons "parameters by reference and by copy; every committed package's `sources` is" (cons "the semantic ID of the `compiler` project's build, whose `TargetFixtures`" (cons "module states each fixture's program. The fixtures include a dispatch of" (cons "several closures of mixed failure, captures read by copy, clone, box, and as" (cons "a unit, a recursive closure, a closure applied where it is built, a function" (cons "type no closure inhabits, a boxed field read, a string that needs escaping," (cons "and each shape of **Lowering**. The negative manifests and their stated" (cons "errors are committed under `compiler/rust/negative/`. `cargo xtask" (cons "check-calculus` compares all of them with the generator. The conformance" (cons "suite builds every committed package offline in one workspace under its lint" (cons "gate; calls each export whose fixture has an observable outcome from a" (cons "separate crate and compares the printed outcome with the denotation's; runs" (cons "a differential of every primitive instance each profile admits on its" (cons "boundary values and seeded inputs; checks that the packages it runs emit" (cons "every correspondence row and every node of the AST; renders every" (cons "package in two separate processes with different working directories and" (cons "environments and compares the bytes with each other and with the committed" (cons "packages; validates every manifest and provenance against its schema; and" (cons "checks every package's `sources` against the published, verified build: the" (cons "semantic ID of `compiler/expected/build/manifest.json`, named by the" (cons "committed verification records, whose `TargetFixtures` source map binds the" (cons "committed module source, which states the package's program as the" (cons "declaration `<fixture>Program`. The binding is to the whole build, the" (cons "smallest object LexLean gives a semantic ID; the declaration is found by the" (cons "fixture's name, which provenance does not record. That the build verifies is" (cons "established by `cargo xtask verify-examples` and `cargo xtask check-golden`," (cons "which reverify and compare those committed records; the suite checks only" (cons "that a record of the build is committed under its name."     arguments: [
      (cons "**Lowering.** `production::lower` lowers a root's eligibility closure into one" (cons "program: one function per definition instance, monomorphized at its type" (cons "arguments; one per instance declaration; one per lambda, lifted with its" (cons "captures first; and the functions of each collection template instance" (cons "(§17.14's library) the closure uses. Every closed document type becomes one" (cons "ADT whose constructors follow declaration order; a structure or class is one" (cons "constructor; `and` and `or` become `cond`; map, set, and graph values are" (cons "lists of entries or elements as the Lean rendering builds them. The lowered" (cons "program is valid, already in first-binding order, byte-identical however" (cons "often it is lowered, its closure is exactly the eligibility report's runtime" (cons "closure, and its layout names the origin of every function and ADT. A" (cons "disagreement is the internal error `LLI9001`. A root whose parameters can" (cons "hold a map, set, or graph is lowered with a boundary (**Boundary** below)" (cons "appended after the closure, so the root stays function 0. The lowering, the" (cons "certificate generator, and their shared source reader obey §17.13's exhaustiveness" (cons "audit: they name every construct and match none by default." (nil String)))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,

A match" (cons "on `C` constructors states each arm after the others, so its proof" (cons "grows with `C` squared: the pairs of a program are the sum of the squares of" (cons "the arms of its matches. The root is refused when the greatest of the three" (cons "bounds exceeds the limit, and not when their sum does: the certificates are" (cons "large for different reasons, and the sum of what they have in common is no" (cons "bound on the largest (a match on 200 constructors returned with a string of" (cons "330 000 characters has a certificate A of 1.1 MB, from the match, and a" (cons "certificate B of 1.0 MB, from the string; no certificate is the sum). The early refusal refuses, and only" (cons "refuses early: a root whose certificates fit under `max_file_bytes` is not" (cons "refused by it (a match on 240 constructors, whose certificate A is 3.9 MB in the notation it is recorded in; a" (cons "string literal of 450 000 characters, whose certificate B is 1.4 MB; a match" (cons "on 350 constructors returned with a string of 940 000 characters), and what the" (cons "bound does not see, the guarantee below stops. Certificate B is not bounded" (cons "from these counts. The rendering of a field read prints the record's whole" (cons "pattern, and a derivation repeats the types and patterns of the nodes around" (cons "the node it derives, so the crate and certificate B grow with the square of a" (cons "record's arity or of the depth of a nesting, which no count of the program's" (cons "nodes bounds. definition{
  name: i0197,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The guarantee is the byte budget. Every certificate is weighed against" (cons "`max_file_bytes` before any file is staged or the toolchain is touched, so no" (cons "oversized certificate is ever accepted and every refusal is `LLS8002` before" (cons "any Lean work. Certificates A, B, and E are built as native core modules" (cons "(`lexlean/core-module/2`, kernel-term DAGs in which a repeated subterm is one" (cons "node, so the types of a program cost its certificates nothing beyond their" (cons "first occurrence, and the chain above verifies 16 deep while its 2^16 type nodes" (cons "are charged to `max_ir_nodes`) and weighed in the authoring notation they are" (cons "recorded in (`record_source`):" (cons "- **Before generation**, lowering's lower bound (`COSTS_A`, `COSTS_B`," (cons "  `COSTS_E`) and certificate B's field-read floor (`8` bytes per pattern entry)" (cons "  refuse any root whose Linear-Program lower bound or field-read floor already" (cons "  exceeds `max_file_bytes`." (cons "- **During generation**, certificate B's aligner counts each new kernel node of" (cons "  a function's derivation at `12` bytes (`NODE_BYTES`, the shortest row of the" (cons "  authoring notation) and stops with `LLS8002` at the end of a step once that" (cons "  floor passes `max_file_bytes`. Because a record-read node takes about `35`" (cons "  bytes in the recorded notation (and the corpora's nodes `14` to `18`), a" (cons "  derivation can hold about three times `max_file_bytes` of recorded notation" (cons "  before that per-node floor stops it (`conformance_sp_02` checks that a quarter" (cons "  of the recorded size of `record copy 100` stops B during derivation)." (cons "  Certificates A and E have no per-node counter during construction; each builds" (cons "  its `CoreModule` in memory for a root that passed lowering's lower bound." (cons "- **When a certificate is finished**, its recorded notation (`record_source` for" (cons "  A and B, `lean_text_legacy` and `record_source` for E) is weighed once against" (cons "  `max_file_bytes` (`certificate.rs`, `data_b.rs`) and refused with `LLS8002`" (cons "  if it exceeds the limit (fixture `certificate-generation-limit`: the sum of" (cons "  500 fields of a record passes lowering's floor and B's 12-byte-per-node" (cons "  derivation floor and is refused at its exact notation weight of `8 513 069`" (cons "  bytes against `4 194 304`, holding about `2.03` times the limit in memory" (cons "  before refusal)." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0199,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The lowered program is bounded in depth too, by a limit of the implementation" (cons "and not a rule of the language. The program is written as canonical JSON and" (cons "read back (`Program::to_file_bytes`) by code that recurses with its nesting, so" (cons "a program whose JSON nests deeper than 512 arrays and objects (about 250 nested" (cons "expressions, measured on the serialized text) is `LLS8002` in phase lowering," (cons "naming the depth it has, before anything is generated from it (the negative" (cons "fixture `lowering-depth-limit`, a root calling a chain of 509 generic" (cons "definitions whose instances nest a pair one level deeper each, is refused at" (cons "515). The reason is the stack that round trip runs on: a debug build takes about" (cons "a quarter of 2 MiB at the limit, which a test builds and checks. Raising it is a" (cons "change to the constant `PROGRAM_JSON_DEPTH` and to that test, and carries no" (cons "promise of a later lift." (nil String)))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0200,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The roots of a project are bounded together, in two places. A closure member" (cons "of the eligibility report (§17.13, a build artifact) records its shortest call" (cons "path, so a closure that is a chain of `D` calls holds `D^2 / 2` steps, and the" (cons "closures of `N` roots that call one another `N^3 / 6`: `lexlean check` of a" (cons "chain of 3000 functions took 638 MB and of 7000 about 3 GB, and of a chain of" (cons "roots 250 took 199 MB, 500 1.28 GB and 15 seconds, 1000 9.5 GB and 90 seconds," (cons "and 2000 exhausted a 16 GB host (the report of 1000 roots would have been" (cons "5 GB). A path is now shared with the paths that extend it (one step for each" (cons "member, dropped without recursion), and the report counts toward" (cons "`max_total_source_bytes`, once more beyond the sources it is made from (§17.12" (cons "rule 2, the rule for artifact declarations): the analysis totals the bytes of" (cons "the reports of all the modules of the project together, root by root, as it" (cons "builds them (a module's report counts with those of the modules before it), stops" (cons "a single closure as soon as its members alone pass the limit, then measures the" (cons "module's report as it will be written (the length of the file, the one function" (cons "`check` and `build` both answer to, so that they pass and refuse the same limits:" (cons "`conformance_sp_02` finds the smallest limit each passes at, which is the sum of" (cons "the lengths of the written reports), and a project" (cons "whose reports pass it is `LLS8002` from `check` (fixtures" (cons "`eligibility-report-limit`, a chain of 100 roots under a limit of 4 194 304, and" (cons "`eligibility-reports-total-limit`, three modules of 60 roots each, which fit" (cons "alone and not together). At the default limit a call chain of 2359" (cons "functions from one root is accepted and one of 2360 refused, and a chain of 243" (cons "roots is accepted (a report of 66 508 069 bytes, 4.5 seconds and 75 MB to" (cons "check, 14 seconds and 675 MB to build) and one of 244 refused in the same time" (cons "and memory. The certificates of the roots repeat their closures too, so their" (cons "recorded modules grow with the square of the number of chained roots (about 1.9 KB of" (cons "certificate A and 0.6 KB of each certificate B in the recorded notation for every" (cons "function of a closure, where the text certificates took 1.1 KB and 2.4 KB;" (cons "243 roots would take about 75 MB), each of them under `max_file_bytes`. The" (cons "lowered programs, the Rust crates, and the certificates of all the roots" (cons "therefore also count toward `max_total_source_bytes`, as each root generates" (cons "them, and a project that passes the limit is `LLS8002` before the toolchain is" (cons "touched (fixture `certificates-total-limit`, a chain of 40 roots under a limit" (cons "of 2 000 000; a chain of 170 roots under the default)." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0201,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "Lean's own budgets are the fourth bound, and they are not the compiler's. On" (cons "the text-era certificates, the pinned Lean checked certificate B at a time that" (cons "grew faster than the fourth power of the arms of the widest match it derived" (cons "(the text certificate B of a match on 130 constructors took 17 seconds, on 200" (cons "constructors 42, on 300 constructors 200, and one on 440 constructors did not" (cons "finish in 15 minutes), and a text certificate A of 23 bytes for each pair of" (cons "arms took 59 seconds at 200 constructors (as kernel data, a pair of arms is at" (cons "least `63.6` bytes of certificate A, so the kernel checks fewer constructors per" (cons "megabyte). Lean's default budgets are exhausted well before on the staged" (cons "decoder and any elaborator work: 100 constructors were checked within" (cons "`maxHeartbeats` 200 000 and `synthInstance.maxHeartbeats` 20 000, 160 exhausted" (cons "the second, 200 the first, a function returning a large literal (a program" (cons "carrying its parameters as data) cost `isDefEq` as much again, and exhausting" (cons "either in the middle of a proof ended as `Application type mismatch`, an error" (cons "that blamed a valid certificate. Every certificate therefore sets both budgets" (cons "to 0, and in certificate B's native core module the `program`, `krate`, and" (cons "`flags` definition rows have `compiled: false` ( as `__prog` and the encoders do" (cons "in certificate A, where only `denote` and what it calls set `compiled: true`)," (cons "so the native decoder adds them to the kernel environment without invoking the" (cons "code generator on them (they are the statement's data, unfolded by the kernel" (cons "and run by nothing, and compiling a large crate term alone exhausted the code" (cons "generator's budget). The limit that remains is the" (cons "wall clock: `child_timeout_ms` (300 seconds by default) bounds every Lean" (cons "process, and its exhaustion is `LLS8002`. Each child process (`lake`, which" (cons "starts `lean`, and the PDF provider: every child of `verify` is started the" (cons "same way) leads a process group of its own, and on a" (cons "timeout the whole group is killed and awaited, so no `lean` is left running at" (cons "full speed and growing after the limit has been reported. The group is" (cons "signalled directly by the compiler (no `kill` executable and no `PATH` are" (cons "involved, so a host without one, such as the shipped image, ends the work all" (cons "the same), and a group that could not be signalled, or that is still there two" (cons "seconds after `SIGKILL`, is stated in the diagnostic (`the processes it" (cons "started may still be running: …`), never passed over; a process of the group" (cons "that is a zombie is ended, not running (a compiler that runs as PID 1, as the" (cons "shipped image does, is the parent of the grandchildren it killed, reaps the" (cons "ones it can, and does not wait for the rest). A child in a group of its" (cons "own is not reached by the terminal's Ctrl-C, nor by the `SIGTERM` that" (cons "`timeout(1)` or a service manager sends to the compiler alone, so the" (cons "`lexlean` executable watches for `SIGINT`, `SIGTERM`, `SIGHUP`," (cons "and `SIGQUIT` from the first child on, ends every live child group when one" (cons "arrives through `lexlean::verify::end_live_children` (§24.6), and then lets" (cons "the signal end the process as it would have (the exit" (cons "status is the signal's). The library never installs a signal handler and" (cons "never changes a signal disposition: a host that calls `lexlean::cli::run`" (cons "keeps its own signals and its own termination, and ends the children it" (cons "started through the same function (§24.6), called from its own signal" (cons "handling or shutdown path. A signal that the process was started ignoring (what" (cons "`nohup` does to `SIGHUP`, and a non-interactive shell to `SIGINT` and" (cons "`SIGQUIT` of a background job) is not watched and stays ignored; on a host that" (cons "can report neither `/proc/self/status` nor `ps -o ignored`, `SIGHUP` is not" (cons "watched. The test suite of the crate runs the real `lexlean` and sends it each, while a child" (cons "is running, and requires that nothing the child started is left, that an" (cons "ignored signal ends neither the run nor its child, and that an embedded run" (cons "leaves the host's `SIGTERM` handling alone (`SIGKILL` cannot be caught by any" (cons "process, so a compiler killed that way leaves its children). The staging" (cons "directories of `verify` (`<build_root>/verified/.staging-*`), which an" (cons "interrupted or killed run cannot remove, are removed by the next `verify` of" (cons "the project, under the project's mutation lock. Where the platform has no" (cons "process groups the tree is killed with `taskkill`, a `taskkill` that fails is" (cons "stated like a group that could not be ended unless the process is no longer" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{

  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "listed by `tasklist` (then it had already exited), and the console delivers Ctrl-C to" (cons "the children. A match on 180 constructors verifies" (cons "(`conformance_sp_02`), and so does one on 300, whose certificate B alone takes" (cons "3 minutes and a half; the reference machine reaches the 300 seconds between 300" (cons "and 400 constructors, where, with `max_file_bytes` raised, `lexlean verify` of a match on 400 ends with" (cons "`error[LLS8002]: child_timeout_ms exceeded by lean in phase" (cons "LexLeanPreserve.C….R0` (certificate A, of 3.7 MB as Lean text). The default limit `max_file_bytes`" (cons "itself allows about 250 constructors, since certificate A, recorded in the authoring notation, is about 3.9 MB at 240 and grows with the square of the arms, so for a match of more than about 250 constructors the limit, not the timeout, refuses the root, with `LLS8002` in the phase certificates (the Lean text was 4.1 MB at 420, and the data certificate is not smaller than the text for a wide match); the timeout is the limit of a match that wide, and a registered one. The extraction record" (cons "of a named root nests with the arms of a match (two levels each), and is read" (cons "without the 128-level recursion limit of the JSON reader on a thread with a" (cons "large stack, so no number of arms makes a verified root fail on it." (nil String))))))))))))

definition{

  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "Two limits of the front end, not of the certificates, are recorded here" (cons "because they bound what verification can be given. Checking a project holds" (cons "memory that grows linearly with the number of its functions when they are" (cons "independent (1500, 3000, 7000, and 14000 trivial functions take 56, 101, 223," (cons "and 435 MB), before any limit of this section or of `max_ir_nodes` is reached;" (cons "a chain of calls is the case above. And a semantic module is JSON read with the" (cons "reader's recursion limit of 128 levels: a term nested deeper than that (a chain" (cons "of 130 nested `let`s) is `LLT4001`, `invalid semantic-module JSON: recursion" (cons "limit exceeded`, whatever `max_scope_depth` says; a chain of 100 is read." (nil String))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0204,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Proof.** The proof follows the source term construct by construct through" (cons "the library's compatibility lemmas. A recursive definition's relation is" (cons "proved by the same recursion as the definition, structural, mutual, or well" (cons "founded with the source's own termination evidence; a function-typed" (cons "parameter carries its closure's index, captures, width predicate, and" (cons "relation. A collection operation is proved by its template's library" (cons "theorem, instantiated at the calling module's runtime functions, whose" (cons "defining clauses the certificate discharges by unfolding them. A relation" (cons "is elaborated with every definition its body calls locally irreducible" (cons "(`attribute [local irreducible] … in`): each callee is related by its own" (cons "theorem, and the elaborator never evaluates one over constant data, such as" (cons "a model's decoded weights, while comparing two matches. The target" (cons "program enters the proof only as the literal the kernel evaluates." (nil String))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0205,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Boundary.** §17.12 states that a map is its strictly ascending entry list" (cons "and a set its strictly ascending element list, an invariant the Lean type" (cons "`List` does not carry: a caller of a rendered root can hand over any list." (cons "The lowering therefore appends, for a root with a parameter whose type" (cons "can hold a map, set, or graph (a graph is a map from node to set), one" (cons "*validator* per closed type that carries the invariant, and an *entry*:" (nil String)))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0206,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "- a validator is a total function to `bool` of one value. A set's checks that" (cons "  consecutive elements ascend (`less_than` on the key order), a map's that" (cons "  consecutive keys ascend and each value is valid, a list's, option's," (cons "  product's, and result's that its components are valid, and a document's," (cons "  per constructor, that each field that carries the invariant is valid in" (cons "  field order. A component without the invariant has the trivial validator." (cons "  The validators of a recursive group of document types call one another and" (cons "  are defined by the same structural recursion as the group's encoders: one" (cons "  validator for each type of the group and each container nested in it, in" (cons "  one mutual block. (§17.12's positivity rule keeps a recursive occurrence out" (cons "  of a map or set, so the containers of a group are lists, options, products," (cons "  and results; its maps and sets hold other types.)" (cons "- the entry, the program's last function, takes the root's parameters," (cons "  calls each validator of a validated parameter in parameter order, and" (cons "  returns `some` of the root's result when all return true and `none`" (cons "  otherwise. A root with no such parameter has no entry and its callers" (cons "  invoke function 0." (cons "- a validator is referenced only by the entry and by validators; no function" (cons "  of the closure references one, and the entry references only validators" (cons "  and the root. A validator is the guard of the boundary: it restricts the" (cons "  entry to the values the source treats as maps, sets, and graphs, linked to" (cons "  the proposition of §17.12 by the theorems below. Certificates A, B, and E" (cons "  hold for every list, whatever its order: the templates transcribe Lean's" (cons "  functions exactly and nothing in a certificate uses the invariant, so none" (cons "  assumes it, and none claims the closure's operations preserve it. That each" (cons "  operation maps strictly ascending lists to strictly ascending lists, so" (cons "  that a validated input stays valid inside the program and no validator is" (cons "  called there, is §17.12 item 2's differential evidence (`SM-28`, `SM-30`)," (cons "  `build` level; a validator inside the program would be a runtime check of" (cons "  what that evidence says holds, as one for termination evidence would be of" (cons "  what a proof establishes. `production::lower::audit_boundary` checks that" (cons "  no validator is referenced from the closure, and a violation is `LLI9001`." (cons "- the entry validates exactly the parameters whose types carry the" (cons "  invariant, each by the validator of its own type, and each validator" (cons "  checks exactly the components of its type that carry it. The certificate" (cons "  generator decides which from the source types of the root's parameters, by" (cons "  its own recursion and not from the lowering's layout, and refuses with" (cons "  `LLI9001`, before any theorem is written, a lowering whose entry or" (cons "  validators differ: a parameter whose check is omitted, a validator that" (cons "  checks nothing for a type that carries the invariant, a component whose" (cons "  check is dropped. `accepts`, below, is built from that judgement." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

It then states the entry's observation `denoteEntry`, the" (cons "conjunction `accepts` of its validated parameters' propositions, and" (nil String))))))))))))

    arguments: [
      (cons "The library is LexLean source, not Lean source (§17.10): every module is a" (cons "native core module of language 1.2, and the Lean the verification compiles is" (cons "generated from it, so there is no hand-written text of these definitions to" (cons "quote. definition{
  name: i0218,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Library.** `language/preservation-1.2/library/` is a LexLean project" (cons "(`lexlean/project/1`, language 1.2, module prefix `LexLeanPreservation`)" (cons "whose `src/` holds the proof library as one native core module per file" (cons "(`Core`, `Values`, `Primitives`, `Fixed`, `Keys`, `Templates`, certificate" (cons "B's `RustBase`, `RustCorr`, `RustLemmas`, `RustMatches`, `RustSound`, the" (cons "boundary validators' `Validate`, and certificate E's `Compose`), each of the" (cons "schema `lexlean/core-module/2` (§17.10) and migrated once and losslessly from" (cons "the Lean library it replaces (`tests/preservation-library/MIGRATION.md`); the" (cons "exporter that made them is not in the release tree. The directory holds no" (cons "file of Lean source. The project, with the title glossary" (cons "`glossary/preservation.titles` its modules use, locks, checks, and builds like" (cons "any project of language 1.2; its lock is not committed, because the lock" (cons "records the compiler-semantics ID, which is a digest of the tree that would" (cons "hold it, and `cargo xtask check-reproducibility` builds it twice in distinct" (cons "directories. It is not verified as a project, because its modules import the" (cons "calculus modules, which are generated Lean (§17.14): what elaborates, replays," (cons "and is audited is the library the certificates of every verification import." (cons "`language/preservation-1.2/library.toml` (`lexlean/preservation-library/2`) lists the modules in dependency order, and" (cons "nothing else: every declaration, with the exact axioms its native row pins, is" (cons "the module's own data, so there is no second list to drift from it. Embedded" (cons "in the compiler, the modules are compiled to Lean by the ordinary pipeline," (cons "and that generated text is what every certificate of a verification imports." (cons "`language/preservation-1.2/modules/` ships the calculus modules" (cons "`LexLeanTarget.TargetSyntax` and `LexLeanTarget.TargetSemantics` byte-equal to" (cons "the compiler project's golden modules (the compiler project's module prefix" (cons "is `LexLeanTarget`), which `cargo xtask check-calculus` compares. No" (cons "certificate may contain `sorry`, `admit`, `axiom`, `opaque`, `unsafe`," (cons "`partial`, `native_decide`, `kernel`, `implemented_by`, `extern`, `#eval`," (cons "`run_cmd`, `run_tac`, a syntax extension, or `sorryAx`, `ofReduceBool`, or" (cons "`ofReduceNat` under any qualification; set an option other than" (cons "`autoImplicit`, `maxRecDepth`, `maxHeartbeats`, `linter.unusedVariables`, or" (cons "`linter.unusedSimpArgs`; or import a module outside its environment. A native" (cons "library module may state a declaration only of the closed kinds of §17.10," (cons "imports only `Init` and modules of the library, and pins every declaration to" (cons "an exact axiom policy; a `partial` or `unsafe` row is the implementation" (cons "record of a definition the module also states, and the audit prints axioms for" (cons "the library's declarations, not for those records. The roots `LexLeanTarget`," (cons "`LexLeanPreservation`, and `LexLeanPreserve` belong to the certificate" (cons "environment; a generated module under one of them is an environment conflict." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0219,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Lists.** Certificate text writes every list and array, of any length, as a" (cons "term the pinned Lean elaborates without binders (`production::lean_list`):" (cons "one of at most 32 elements as the literal `[…]` or `#[…]`, and a longer one as" (cons "the chain `(a₀ :: a₁ :: … :: [])` or `List.toArray` of it, which is the term" (cons "Lean's list macro writes for a short literal. The macro writes a literal of" (cons "more than 32 elements as `％[… | List.nil]`, which Lean splits in halves under" (cons "`let` binders, and a witness whose elements are postponed to metavariables," (cons "such as the operands of a call in a certificate B derivation, does not unify" (cons "through them: a valid certificate would be rejected (`LLV7015`). The chain is" (cons "as deep as the list, which `maxRecDepth` 100000 covers." (nil String)))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

Every library declaration's axioms must equal its registry row, and" (cons "every root theorem's must be exactly `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`, and" (cons "`propext`." (nil String)))))))

definition{
  name: i0221,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Verification.** `lexlean verify` checks certificates A, B, and E after" (cons "named-root extraction (§22.1 stage 12): it renders each root's program in" (cons "each of its targets, derives certificate B, composes certificate E, stages the shipped environment," (cons "compiles each module and certificate silently with the pinned Lean, replays" (cons "each certificate through `leanchecker`, and audits the axioms. Every root is" (cons "lowered and its certificates generated, under the limits (**Limits**), before" (cons "the toolchain is touched. A shipped" (cons "module that fails its token audit or does not compile silently, or a library" (cons "declaration whose axioms differ from its registry row, is `LLV7014`; a" (cons "certificate A that does not compile silently, fails its replay, or whose" (cons "root theorem's axioms are not exactly the three above is `LLV7013`; a" (cons "rendering the aligner derives no correspondence for, or a certificate B that" (cons "fails any of the same checks, is `LLV7015`; a certificate E that fails" (cons "them is `LLV7016`. Each of the three names the module, the declaration of the" (cons "certificate Lean's first error lies in (the relation `__rel_k` of a function," (cons "the derivation `fun<k>` of one, the composition `root`), the root, and the" (cons "first error with the lines that say what it concerns, bounded to twelve lines" (cons "and 1500 bytes. These are class `language`, exit 1, as `LLV7002` is: the" (cons "pinned Lean rejected a module LexLean generated from the user's program, and" (cons "verification fails closed with nothing published; that the defect lies in the" (cons "lowering or generator rather than the user's source is a reading of the" (cons "message, which the declaration and root locate, not a different class, and an" (cons "inconsistency inside LexLean's own code is `LLI9001`. A pinned Lean that is" (cons "killed with no message, runs out of memory, or overflows its stack while" (cons "checking a certificate is `LLS8002`, not `LLV7013` and not `LLV7014`: the" (cons "machine could not check the module. So is one that exhausts its heartbeat or" (cons "recursion budget on a module of at least a quarter of `max_file_bytes`. On a" (cons "smaller module such a failure is Lean's verdict on the certificate and is" (cons "`LLV7013`, `LLV7015`, or `LLV7016` with the declaration and the error, because" (cons "a wrong certificate whose proof makes `isDefEq` loop produces it as readily as" (cons "an oversized one. It publishes each certificate" (cons "under `preserve/`, the audit output, process records, the lowered program of" (cons "each root (`preserve/program/R<i>.json`) and the rendered crate of each" (cons "target (`preserve/crate/R<i>.<target>.rs`), and" (cons "`preserve/preservation.json` (`lexlean/preservation/1`," (cons "`schemas/preservation.schema.json`): the registry's SHA-256, the root" (cons "theorem axioms, and per root its targets, certificate module, theorem, and" (cons "the certificate's byte length and SHA-256, and per target its certificate" (cons "B's module, theorem, byte length, and SHA-256, its certificate E's module," (cons "theorem, byte length, and SHA-256, the byte length and SHA-256 of its crate," (cons "and its entry (the function of the program a caller of the crate invokes, the" (cons "boundary entry when the program has one and function 0 otherwise, and its" (cons "symbol `f<n>`), and per root the byte length and SHA-256 of its program, which" (cons "the attestation binds. The entry is the function certificate E states of the" (cons "crate (`Rust.fnIdent`) and the crate defines, and `conformance_sp_05` checks" (cons "the three agree. Certificate B is about that crate and E about the root's" (cons "rendering in it. `preserve/crate/R<i>.<target>.rs` is the crate certificate B" (cons "is about, printed before any export wrapper; it is the object of the" (cons "certificates and not a package a user builds. `lexlean verify` does not" (cons "assemble an exporting package: the export wrappers, the package files, the" (cons "text printer, and `rustc` are outside E, and what they do is measured by the" (cons "rustc differential (`SP-11`), whose harness packages export the recorded entry" (cons "alone, so that a caller of those packages cannot reach the unvalidated root." (cons "Nothing in the verified build prevents a package built by other means from" (cons "exporting the root; certificate E states what the recorded entry returns." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0222,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Rust machine.** The meaning of a rendered crate (§17.16) is declared by two" (cons "generated LexLean modules of the `compiler` project, verified and shipped" (cons "like the calculus modules (`language/preservation-1.2/modules/`):" (cons "`RustSyntax` states every construct of the closed Rust AST, without origins," (cons "and `RustSemantics` its evaluator. The machine's values are the calculus's" (cons "(§17.14): a `u64` is a `nat` below `2^64`, an `i64` an `int` in range, a fixed" (cons "width its scalar, `Str`, `Bytes`, and `List` the string, bytes, and list, an" (cons "`Rc` or a box the value it holds, an ADT variant the ADT value of the same" (cons "constructor, and a defunctionalized closure the calculus closure of the same" (cons "function and captures. A move, clone, copy, dereference, or unboxing reads" (cons "the binding; a block binds its lets in order; a match takes the first arm" (cons "whose pattern matches; `?` on `Err` raises out of the function, which returns" (cons "`Err(Overflow)` exactly when its result type is `R<T>`; an application" (cons "dispatches through the `apply` arm of the closure's function and capture" (cons "count. A *machine primitive* is a function of the machine's standard library that the" (cons "runtime reaches and cannot state in the AST. `RustSyntax` declares eight" (cons "(`Machine`), each at a width: `checkedAdd`, `checkedSub`, `checkedMul`," (cons "`checkedQuot`, `checkedNeg`, `checkedShl`, and `checkedShr` of a width, and" (cons "`tryFrom` to a width, and a call names one as the callee `Callee.machine`, beside" (cons "`Callee.function` and `Callee.runtime`. Its meaning is the calculus primitive of" (cons "the same name on the same operands, the `Option` value that primitive states" (cons "(`checkedAdd` of two `u8` is `Some` of their sum, or `None` when it exceeds" (cons "255), and the call is stuck on an operand of another width, a shift's amount" (cons "excepted; a primitive is not a runtime item, so it does not pass through the" (cons "item tables. The literal `Lit.overflow` is the unit struct `Overflow` of the" (cons "runtime, which the machine represents by the unit value, as an `Err(Overflow)`" (cons "carries it. Neither is yet written by the renderer: the scalar items are" (cons "expressed over them by the stage of #91 that follows (`SP-18`). A *loop*" (cons "(`Expr.loop`, `SP-19`) is the one construct of the machine that repeats: it names" (cons "a state, a local the enclosing block has bound, and a body block. The body is" (cons "evaluated in the environment with the state, and its value is a `Result`: `Ok`" (cons "of a next state rebinds the state to that value and evaluates the loop again, and" (cons "`Err` of a done value ends the loop with it as the value of the loop, so a loop" (cons "breaks with a value and has no labels. A body whose value is neither is stuck," (cons "and the loop recurses through `eval` with the fuel the body left, so a loop that" (cons "does not end within the fuel is exhausted, never a wrong value. The renderer" (cons "prints a loop as `{ let mut s = s; loop { let step = { body }; match step { Ok(next) =>" (cons "{ s = next; } Err(done) => break done, } } }`, binding the body's value first because" (cons "Rust's lint gate refuses a block with bindings as a scrutinee; the validator lets the body move the state, which" (cons "the loop's own binding holds, and nothing else, because the body runs again after" (cons "every `Ok`. The lowering produces a loop for a function every self call of" (cons "which is a tail call and some self call of which changes a parameter the body" (cons "reads (`calculus::rust::loops` decides it on the calculus, and the lowering" (cons "fails when it disagrees; `RB-14`): the parameters the self calls change are the" (cons "state, a tuple nested to the right when there are several and the parameter" (cons "itself when there is one, held in a binding the function makes from them" (cons "(the changed parameters are renamed in the signature, and the body begins by" (cons "binding the original names from it); each tail self call is `Ok` of the next state and" (cons "every other tail value is `Err` of the value, so the loop breaks with the value" (cons "the function returns; a parameter every self call passes on as it got it stays" (cons "a parameter, which the body clones. A function with a self call that is not a" (cons "tail call, or with a parameter nothing reads (which the loop's state could" (cons "not carry), is rendered as a call. Every runtime" (cons "item (`rust::runtime::Item`) is declared as the calculus" (cons "primitive it realizes on the same operands (the successor as `nat_add` with" (cons "one), fallible exactly when the renderer types it `R<T>`, and present in" (cons "`rust-core` only when it needs no heap; an overflow of an item that cannot" (cons "fail is the machine's *abort*, which no machine can avoid: the only such" (cons "overflow is the length of a sequence of `2^64` or more elements. An item" (cons "of a width is a distinct Rust function per width, so the machine indexes it" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0223,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "by its width: a checked, bitwise, shift, or formatting item takes only" (cons "operands of its width (a shift only its shifted operand), and is stuck on" (cons "any other, so `fixed_u16::checked_add` on two `u8` values means nothing," (cons "as in Rust, rather than the `u8` addition the calculus primitive would" (cons "compute. Likewise a list, bytes, or text item (`length_list`," (cons "`length_bytes`, `length_string`, and the append, index, and slice items)" (cons "takes only a first operand of its own sequence, so `length_bytes` on text" (cons "does not count its characters. The correspondence's value typing carries" (cons "what these guards read: a fixed-width value's width, and a list's, bytes'," (cons "or text's constructor. Every certified root's crate" (cons "is built as a `RustSyntax` kernel term (`production::certificate::data_b`," (cons "audited for default arms) that Lean elaborates and the machine evaluates; on the seeded inputs of" (cons "the differential, its outcome equals the calculus interpreter's on the" (cons "lowered program. That rustc agrees with this declaration is `build`" (cons "evidence, measured by the conformance suite, never assumed by a proof." (nil String))))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0227,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The proof is not written per root. The library declares an inductive" (cons "correspondence `Corr` between program constructs and the Rust the renderer" (cons "writes for them, each constructor one lowering shape with decidable side" (cons "conditions (a rendering's folds, patterns, loads of boxed fields, rebuilt" (cons "match arms, uninhabited parameter types), and proves once, by induction on" (cons "fuel and on derivations, that every derivation is sound for every fuel" (cons "(`sound`) and therefore that a crate each of whose functions has a" (cons "derivation is simulated (`simulate`). A certificate is the aligner's" (cons "derivation (`production::certificate::data_b`) of each function, whose side" (cons "conditions the kernel decides by evaluating the `Eq.refl` the rule states," (cons "and its theorem is `simulate` applied to them. A function with a parameter of a type no value inhabits" (cons "needs no derivation: no call reaches its body. The rule set is closed:" (cons "the aligner declares its rules as a list that `cargo xtask validate-model`" (cons "(audit-production) requires to equal the constructors of `Corr`, each a case" (cons "of `sound`'s induction and each emitted by the aligner, and the aligner's" (cons "block judgment names every construct of the calculus with no wildcard or" (cons "binding arm. A rendering that no rule derives is refused, not certified by" (cons "default. The correspondence and its side conditions are vocabulary of the" (cons "hand-written library rather than generated, because they are the statement" (cons "the library's proofs are about; the machine they relate is the generated" (cons "`RustSemantics`." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))

So the rendered entry realizes the encoded source result as `some` for" (cons "arguments that satisfy §17.12's invariants and refuses every other with" (cons "`none`. `RepresentableL` restricts the theorem to the arguments a Rust caller" (cons "can pass: a natural number is a `u64` and an integer an `i64`, wherever it" (cons "occurs in an argument (the proof does not use it; the statement is the" (cons "smaller). The machine has no stack and no heap bound: a call that exhausts" (cons "the stack or fails to allocate aborts the process in Rust, the theorem says" (cons "nothing of it (§17.16 gives no stack-depth guarantee), and the only abort the" (cons "machine itself models is the overflow of an item that cannot fail, the length" (cons "of a sequence of 2^64 or more elements. The" (cons "proof is the library's `compose` applied to the two theorems and to a" (cons "generated proof that every encoded argument is well typed for the root's" (cons "parameters, `WT (enc x) t`, written construct by construct over the" (cons "encoders: one theorem per encoder, mutual over a recursive group as the" (cons "encoders are, with the library's lemmas for every primitive and container" (cons "value. A certificate E that does not compile silently, fails its replay," (cons "or whose theorem's axioms are not exactly the three above is `LLV7016`." (nil String))))))))))))))))))
