The recursive body, how each" (cons "parameter is carried (changed, kept, or dropped), the state and result types," (cons "and the numbers of the holder and of the renamed parameters are the witnesses" (cons "of `FunLoop`. `funSem_loop` proves the simulation of such a function from the" (cons "simulation of the recursive body, the iteration of the loop on the iteration" (cons "of the calls, and `funSem_of` takes the second disjunct to it; `Corr`, its" (cons "recursor, and `sound` are untouched. The aligner recovers the witnesses by" (cons "inverting the rendering of loops, which reads the holder bound to the state," (cons "the destructuring that begins the loop body, the renamed parameters, and the" (cons "`Ok` and `Err` wraps of the tails, and then requires the library's form of the" (cons "recovered body, transliterated, to be exactly the crate's function: a loop that" (cons "is not in the image of the rendering is refused with *Certificate B rejected*" (cons "(`LLV7015`) and the reason, not aligned by guess. Two things of the recursive" (cons "body leave no trace in the loop form and are chosen: a kept parameter is read" (cons "by `Clone`, which the library accepts as it accepts every read, and the" (cons "argument of a dropped parameter is recovered only when it is a temporary the" (cons "block binds."       (cons "| Premise | Fact when it holds | Evidence evaluated |" (cons "| --- | --- | --- |" (cons "| `excluded p` | the system is never admitted | the system on the domain argument at position `p` |" (cons "| `pays_at_least ps` | an admitted system's cost is at least the listed arguments' steps plus its preparation | the system on the arguments at the strictly increasing positions `ps` |" (cons "| `measured` | the system's whole status | the system on the whole domain |" (cons "| `reduced s` | an admitted system has a strictly cheaper admitted rewriting in the universe | nothing: the schema `s` (below) and two static comparisons; the schema's preservation is the module's `preserves`, decided by the kernel for the committed instances and proved for all by the library |" (nil String)))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.table,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0248,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A premise that does not hold, or an `unresolved` measurement, yields no fact." (cons "Every fact is a statement about the system at the request's fuel and at" (cons "every larger one: `LexLeanPreservation.stable` makes an evaluation that" (cons "returns a value at one fuel return the same value, in the same steps, at" (cons "every larger fuel, so an admitted system stays admitted at its cost, an" (cons "inadmissible system stays inadmissible, and a lower bound stays a lower bound." (cons "A system the request's fuel leaves unresolved is therefore not removed and not" (cons "given an infinite cost (§17.15): it either is bounded by a premise that holds" (cons "at every fuel or leaves the certificate incomplete." (nil String))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0250,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. `lower_bound b attainers` (UOR-GNAF Theorem 11.1). Every attainer is" (cons "   measured and costs exactly `b`; every other system is inadmissible, is" (cons "   shown to cost at least `b` (strictly more, for `argmin_complete`, so that" (cons "   no tied system is left out of the attainers), or reduces. The verdict is" (cons "   an `optimum` (for `global_optimal`: these systems attain the least cost)" (cons "   or an `argmin` (for `argmin_complete`: exactly these do)." (cons "2. `frontier_cover members` (Theorem 11.8). Every member is measured and no" (cons "   member is strictly dominated, in the order of §17.15, by another; every" (cons "   other system is inadmissible, reduces, or has a cost, measured or bounded" (cons "   from below with its exact size, strictly dominated by a member. Equal" (cons "   costs dominate neither, so every tied system is a member. The verdict is" (cons "   the `frontier`." (cons "3. `reduction` (proof-complete reduction). Every system is measured," (cons "   inadmissible, or reduced; the claim is the optimum, or the frontier, of" (cons "   the measured systems under the order of §17.15 (`Gnaf.argmin`," (cons "   `Gnaf.frontier`), and is verified by the route above. A system that is" (cons "   neither is a `reduction_incomplete` obligation." (cons "4. `representation normal bridge` (UOR-GNAF §10.6, for `global_optimal`" (cons "   only). The representation metric is the size of the code a system can run" (cons "   (§17.15). `normal` is a system of the least size among the universe, which" (cons "   needs no evaluation and establishes only that status. A `bridge`" (cons "   `{slope, offset}` states that the cost of an admitted system is at least" (cons "   `slope * size + offset`; every system must show it (measured, or bounded" (cons "   from below), and the bound for the universe is then the bridge at the" (cons "   least size, which `normal` must attain exactly. Without a bridge the metric" (cons "   supports no execution claim: the verdict is incomplete with" (cons "   `bridge_missing` and the status `representation_minimal`." (cons "5. `normality system` never certifies. The verdict is incomplete with" (cons "   `optimality_unproved` and the status `locally_normalized` for a measured" (cons "   GNAF-normal system, `system_not_normal` for one a schema improves, and" (cons "   `normal_unmeasured` for one that is not measured." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0251,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Transformation schemas.** The schemas are closed: `collapse` (a dispatch" (cons "whose two plans are one runs that plan), `specialize_small` (a dispatch whose" (cons "domain arguments are all below its threshold runs its small plan), and" (cons "`specialize_large` (all at or above it runs its large plan); each rewrites the" (cons "dispatch to the fixed system of that plan. A schema is *eligible* for a" (cons "system only if it is a dispatch, no plan's code calls the entry (function 0) or" (cons "applies a closure, every domain argument is a list, and the domain's lengths" (cons "satisfy the schema; it *improves* the system when it is eligible and the" (cons "rewritten system's size and preparation are no greater. A system is" (cons "GNAF-normal when no schema improves it (UOR-GNAF §6.3, §6.4). A `reduced`" (cons "premise holds exactly when its schema improves the system, which the checker" (cons "decides by evaluating nothing. What it asserts is a closed definition of the" (cons "module, not a comment on it. The *observation* of a system on one argument of" (cons "the request's domain is its status on that argument alone" (cons "(`Certification.argumentStatus`, `Gnaf.statusOn` on the one argument): admitted" (cons "when the system returns the reference's value, at a cost. A rewriting *keeps" (cons "the observations* (`sameObservations`) when it is admitted on every argument" (cons "on which the system is, and is *strictly cheaper* (`strictlyCheaper`) when it" (cons "is admitted there in fewer steps; both are defined at an explicit fuel" (cons "(`sameObservationsAt`, `strictlyCheaperAt`) and at the request's" (cons "(`sameObservations`, `strictlyCheaper`), and an argument on which the system" (cons "is not admitted relates nothing, because no claim consumes it. `preserves` is" (cons "`improves` together with both, and `certificateReductionsPreserve` states that" (cons "every `reduced` premise of a certificate is a preserving application." (cons "Equivalence is therefore stated for admitted observations, not for every" (cons "outcome: a system that is inadmissible or unresolved on an argument is not" (cons "claimed to stay so. The kernel decides it for every committed instance" (cons "(`GnafCertificates` states `<id>Preserves`, by reflexivity, for each" (cons "certificate with a `reduced` premise, and the ineligible reductions state" (cons "`false`); the library proves it for every instance, as `schema_preserves_at`" (cons "(for every fuel) and `improves_preserves` (`improves` implies `preserves`)," (cons "stated in the module's own relations and by `schema_cheaper`, with the" (cons "entry-irrelevance of evaluation (`eval_tail`) and the factoring of a dispatch" (cons "(`dispatch_cheaper`)." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0252,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Verdict.** `Certification.check` returns one of three verdicts and no other:" (cons "`refused`, for a certificate that does not bind its request (another" (cons "verifier, a request the model rejects, another universe identity, objective," (cons "claim class, or scope, a route its claim class cannot take, or more or fewer" (cons "premises than systems); `certified`, an `optimum`, `argmin`, or `frontier` as" (cons "positions of the expansion; or `incomplete`, the first obligation left open" (cons "in the expansion's order together with the strongest status the certificate" (cons "does establish: `correct_only` (a system admitted at a cost), `locally_normalized`," (cons "`representation_minimal`, or `not_certified`. An incomplete verdict is the" (cons "honest non-global status and is never a fallback claim: nothing is concluded" (cons "from what was not shown." (nil String))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

  result: DocRows::Item,

    arguments: [
      (cons "- a certified `optimum` or `argmin` bounds below the cost of every admitted" (cons "  well-formed system, at the request's fuel and at every larger one, by the" (cons "  stated bound, a stated system attains it, and, for an `argmin`, every" (cons "  system attaining it is stated (`lower_bound_attainment`);" (cons "- a certified `frontier` is exactly the Pareto frontier of the admitted" (cons "  systems: every member is admitted and undominated, and every admitted" (cons "  system is a member or strictly dominated by one (`frontier_complete`);" (cons "- no `normality` certificate is certified (`normality_never_certifies`), and a" (cons "  certified optimum is GNAF-normal, the converse being false" (cons "  (`certified_optimum_is_normal`);" (cons "- each improving schema application satisfies the module's `preserves`: its" (cons "  rewriting keeps the system's observations over the request's domain, at every" (cons "  fuel, and is strictly cheaper (`schema_preserves_at`, `improves_preserves`," (cons "  and, for the cost of whole systems, `schema_cheaper`), by the" (cons "  entry-irrelevance of evaluation (`eval_tail`) and the factoring of a" (cons "  dispatch (`dispatch_cheaper`)." definition{
  name: i0255,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The library is repository evidence: it is not shipped and it does not enter the" (cons "semantics ID. It is LexLean source (`GC-11`): `compiler/proofs/` is an ordinary" (cons "closed project of thirteen language-1.2 native `lexlean/core-module/2` modules" (cons "(`src/`, with the title glossary `certification.titles`) whose module prefix is" (cons "`LexLeanCertification`, and it holds no file of Lean source. `compiler/proofs/" (cons "library.toml` lists the modules, in dependency order, and nothing else: every" (cons "declaration and its exact axioms are read from the native data, where each row" (cons "pins its axiom policy exactly, and a module may import only `Init`, an earlier" (cons "module of the library, the preservation core, and the calculus and generated" (cons "modules it is stated against (`LexLeanTarget.TargetSyntax`," (cons "`TargetSemantics`, `Gnaf`, `GnafFixtures`, `Certification`," (cons "`GnafCertificates`). The Lean the library is checked as is the Lean the" (cons "compiler generates from those modules, byte for byte what building the project" (cons "writes. It is verified by `GC-09` against the compiler's generated modules and" (cons "the shipped preservation library (§17.17). The library was a hand-written Lean" (cons "project of the same thirteen modules, converted once and without loss; the" (cons "evidence is `tests/certification-library/MIGRATION.md`. The model and its" (cons "fixtures are verified by `verify` with the project." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0256,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**What is closed semantics and what is library.** In the `Certification`" (cons "module, as LexLean definitions Lean elaborates, replays, and axiom-audits:" (cons "the transformation schemas, their rewriting, eligibility, static size and" (cons "preparation, `improves` and `isNormal`; the premises, facts, routes," (cons "certificate, obligations, established statuses, and verdicts; the observations" (cons "of a system and its rewriting, `sameObservations`, `strictlyCheaper`, and" (cons "`preserves`; the lower bound, the attainment of a bound, the argmin and the" (cons "frontier of the admitted systems (`Gnaf.argmin`, `Gnaf.frontier`, reused, not" (cons "redefined); and the checker. In `GnafCertificates`, for each committed" (cons "certificate: the certificate, its verdict, and, where it reduces, the verdict" (cons "of `certificateReductionsPreserve`, each decided by the kernel. In the library," (cons "and only there: the statements quantified over every request, certificate," (cons "grammar, domain, and fuel: soundness of the checker against `Gnaf.expand`, the" (cons "attainment of a bound, the completeness of a frontier, normality, and the" (cons "preservation of every improving schema application. They are not proved in" (cons "the closed language, and that is a choice of this work, not a limit of the" (cons "language: the closed proof language has universal quantification, implication," (cons "case analysis, and induction, and the closed `Gnaf` module proves" (cons "`Gnaf.expandComplete` over every grammar and selector (§17.15). What the" (cons "library's statements need is the metatheory of the evaluator, which no closed" (cons "module carries: that evaluation does not depend on the entry function" (cons "(`eval_tail`), that a dispatch factors into the plan it runs" (cons "(`dispatch_cheaper`), and that a run's outcome does not change with more fuel." (cons "Moving them into a closed module is achievable in principle, by stating that" (cons "metatheory over `TargetSemantics` there, and is not attempted or claimed here." (cons "The library is checked against the module's definitions, which it cites, not" (cons "copies (`GC-09` elaborates ascriptions of its preservation theorems at the" (cons "module's relations)." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

The certificate fixtures carry no" (cons "per-certificate universal statements over the universe (the library's" (cons "theorems are those); they carry the verdict and the preservation of each" (cons "reduction. Nothing in the issue's acceptance criteria needs them: the claim" (cons "statuses `correct_only`, `locally_normalized`, Pareto and frontier complete," (cons "globally optimal and argmin complete, and `not_certified` are all verdicts." (nil String)))))))))))))

A misreading that LexLean's calculus" (cons "and its checkers share would be invisible to every one of them. `lean4-prod`" (cons "(`https://github.com/auser/lean4-prod`) is an independent generator of Rust" (cons "from Lean 4: it imports the compiled Lean module LexLean generates (§18)," (cons "lowers a root through Lean's own compiler (LCNF), and renders Rust by its own" (cons "rules.       (cons "**Pin.** `oracle/lean4-prod.toml` (`lexlean/oracle-pin/1`) states the" (cons "oracle's remote, the exact commit, `git_tree` (the commit's tree, for a reader" (cons "with git), the path prefixes of the files the integration uses (`lean/`," (cons "`rust/`, and `rust-toolchain.toml`), and the SHA-256 of their canonical frame:" (cons "the domain `lexlean-oracle-tree-v1`, then, for each file in ascending byte" (cons "order of its path, the path and the SHA-256 of its bytes, each preceded by its" (cons "length as a big-endian `u64` (a `.git`, `.lake`, or `target` directory is not" (cons "part of the tree). Its role is `validation-evidence`; any other value, a" (cons "missing or unknown field, a commit or digest that is not lowercase" (cons "hexadecimal of the right length, and `vendored = true` make the pin" (cons "malformed. The pin states the licence finding it rests on: every Rust" (cons "manifest of the oracle declares `license = \"MIT\"`, but the repository ships no" (cons "licence text and no copyright notice, which the MIT licence requires a copy to" (cons "carry. Nothing of the oracle is therefore vendored; it is *acquired*. The" (cons "pin's `rust_toolchain` and `lean_toolchain` are LexLean's own, and must equal" (cons "the channel of `rust-toolchain.toml` and the content of `lean-toolchain`; so" (cons "must the `lean/lean-toolchain` of the oracle's own files, which the digest" (cons "covers. A bump of either toolchain that leaves the pin behind makes the oracle" (cons "*unavailable* with a message that names both values (a toolchain drift, to be" (cons "repaired by repinning below), not a build that fails somewhere else." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))

    arguments: [
      (cons "- the fetch is made in a directory beside the cache entry, verified there, and" (cons "  only then renamed into place; it is removed on every failure, so a fetch that" (cons "  fails leaves nothing that a later run could take for a checkout;" (cons "- a checkout is used only if the files the pin names hash to the pin's digest" (cons "  and, for a git checkout, `HEAD` is the pinned commit; a cache entry that does" (cons "  not (an interrupted run, a damaged cache) is discarded and fetched once more;" (cons "- files that are not the pin are a *pin mismatch*, reported at once and never" (cons "  retried, whatever the remote says; a transient failure of the network (of" (cons "  `git fetch`, and of the fetch of the oracle's crates.io dependencies) is tried" (cons "  again twice, after 10 s and after 45 s, three tries in about a minute; a" (cons "  remote or a commit the upstream no longer has is not tried again and names" (cons "  the repin procedure below;" (cons "- the oracle is built in a copy of the used files with the pinned toolchains:" (cons "  `lake build` of the exporter, `cargo fetch --locked` into a Cargo home of the" (cons "  cache's own (so that nothing the host happened to have cached is read, and the" (cons "  lock file the pin's digest covers is what is fetched), and then" (cons "  `cargo build --release --locked --offline`;" (cons "- every invocation of the oracle's tools, and every build and run of the crates" (cons "  it generates, runs in a process group of its own under a time limit (the" (cons "  pin's `timeout_seconds` for one invocation of the oracle's tools, four times" (cons "  that for building the oracle, 15 minutes for the build of the generated" (cons "  crates, 2 minutes for one run of one of them) and is ended with" (cons "  everything it started when it overruns; an overrun is *unavailable* for the" (cons "  oracle's tools and for the build, and a finding of its root for a generated" (cons "  program that does not finish." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],

definition{
  name: i0269,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "*Residual risk and repinning.* The pin makes the oracle verifiable, not" (cons "available: what is fetched is exactly the reviewed files or nothing is" (cons "validated, but two things stay outside this repository and its audits. The" (cons "oracle's repository is third-party and, for the licence reason above, cannot be" (cons "vendored or mirrored by this repository; its crates.io dependencies are fixed by" (cons "the lock file the digest covers, but are not vendored and (not being shipped)" (cons "not in `cargo deny`'s scope. If the upstream deletes or rewrites the pinned" (cons "commit, or a dependency is removed from the registry, acquisition fails with" (cons "the reason, the normative host fails the cases that need the oracle (it never" (cons "passes them), and the differential stays red until the pin is moved. To" (cons "repin: fetch the new commit; record its commit, git tree, file count, and" (cons "`tree_sha256` in `oracle/lean4-prod.toml` (a mismatch prints the digest and the" (cons "count of the files it found, and `oracle::tree_digest` computes them); run" (cons "`just vv`; turn each new difference into a row of `oracle/known-differences.toml`" (cons "or `oracle/known-non-overlap.toml` (raising the table's `version` and recording" (cons "the digest of its rows in `oracle/table-versions.toml`) or fix LexLean; and, if" (cons "the upstream is gone, push the reviewed commit to a repository the organisation" (cons "controls and set `remote` to it (the digest is of the bytes, so it does not" (cons "change). The pin file must name the toolchains `rust-toolchain.toml` and" (cons "`lean-toolchain` name; when either is bumped, repin in the same change." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0270,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Corpus.** The corpus is three kinds of project, all compiled by LexLean to" (cons "Lean and handed to the oracle's exporter in the same way: every production" (cons "root of `examples/production`, `examples/production-coverage`," (cons "`examples/models`, and `examples/reasoning`; the *probe project*, a minimal" (cons "root for each runtime construct of the production registry" (cons "(`language/production-1.2.toml`, §17.13) that none of those roots realizes" (cons "alone in a root the oracle accepts, so that the oracle's verdict on a" (cons "construct is a verdict on that construct and not on whatever else a large root" (cons "holds; and the *rejection projects*, the committed negative fixtures that" (cons "LexLean refuses as production roots (`production-polymorphic-root`," (cons "`production-higher-order-escape`, `production-runtime-incompatible-type`," (cons "`production-literal-width`, `production-phantom-type-parameter`," (cons "`production-named-type-boundary`, and `production-universe-boundary`), with the" (cons "production marking removed so that LexLean still compiles them to Lean. LexLean's" (cons "verdict on a rejection project is the diagnostic its fixture records." (cons "The corpus has 104 roots; the oracle generates Rust for 32 of them and" (cons "refuses 72; 25 of the crates it generates for eligible roots compile and are" (cons "run on 1446 inputs, and each of the other seven is a recorded difference (six" (cons "crates that rustc rejects, and the crate of the rejected root with the" (cons "literal beyond 64 bits). Of the 72 refusals, 66 are of roots LexLean takes" (cons "(33 `unresolved-external-call`, 22 `escaping-local-function`, 4" (cons "`opaque-lowering`, 3 `codegen-PolymorphicType`, 2 `codegen-UnboundedInt`, 2" (cons "`codegen-UnresolvedCall`) and 6 of roots LexLean rejects (4 `opaque-lowering`," (cons "1 `export-refused`, 1 `codegen-OpaqueType`; the seventh rejected root is the" (cons "one the oracle generates): `opaque-lowering` is 8 over the corpus. LexLean and the oracle agree on the verdict for the six" (cons "and each records its own reason: the refusal kind of every rejected root is a" (cons "row of `oracle/known-differences.toml` (`both-refuse:<kind>`), so a rejected" (cons "root that the oracle refuses for another reason is a disagreement." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0271,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Overlap.** A runtime construct of the registry is *inside the overlap* when" (cons "it is realized in the closure of a compiled root: a corpus root the oracle" (cons "accepts, whose crate rustc compiles, and whose closure LexLean's eligibility" (cons "report (§17.13) says holds the construct. That is a statement about the" (cons "program the oracle compiled, not about every path through it: whether an input" (cons "reaches the construct is a matter of the seeds (below), which are derived from" (cons "the types and literals of the root to reach its arms and its edges, and" (cons "whose reach the suite checks (constructors) and plants against (arithmetic" (cons "edges), but does not prove complete. A construct is *outside the overlap* when" (cons "the oracle refuses, or cannot compile, every root that realizes it. The" (cons "registry is partitioned by `oracle/known-non-overlap.toml`" (cons "(`lexlean/oracle-overlap/1`, versioned by the digest of its rows): a construct" (cons "is either realized in the closure of a compiled root or is a row of the table," (cons "never neither. A row for a construct whose disposition is `formal-only` or" (cons "`erased` (no runtime value realizes it, in either compiler) is of kind" (cons "`not-runtime` and states the disposition, which the registry must still state." (cons "A row for a runtime construct names its *probe*, a root of the corpus whose" (cons "closure realizes the construct, the kind the oracle's answer to that probe has," (cons "whether the probe realizes the construct alone (`construct`) or is a root of a" (cons "certified example whose closure holds it among others (`closure`), and the" (cons "reason. Its kind is `oracle-refuses` (the oracle refuses the probe, in the" (cons "stated kind of refusal), `oracle-breaks` (the oracle generates the probe but" (cons "its crate does not compile under rustc, refusal `does-not-compile`), or" (cons "`instantiated` (no root's closure holds the construct, because it is" (cons "instantiated before it is realized; the probe is a root that instantiates it)." (cons "The conformance suite re-asserts every row: the probe must still realize the" (cons "construct, the oracle must still answer it in that way, and no compiled root" (cons "may realize the construct; a row that fails any of these, a row for a name that" (cons "is no construct of the registry, and a construct in neither the compiled roots" (cons "nor the table are refused." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0272,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Comparison.** The comparison is of meaning and never of bytes: the oracle's" (cons "Rust and LexLean's differ in every function, and both may change without the" (cons "validation changing. For each root it compares, in order:" (nil String))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0273,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "1. *acceptance*: whether each side takes the root, and, for a root only the" (cons "   oracle refuses or only LexLean refuses, the kind of refusal. The oracle's" (cons "   refusals are its own structured words (`prod-export failed:` from the" (cons "   exporter, `Code generation refused:` from the generator), and are classified" (cons "   as `escaping-local-function`, `opaque-lowering`, `unresolved-external-call`," (cons "   `unsupported-local-function`, `export-refused` (an exporter refusal of none" (cons "   of those kinds), or `codegen-<its error>`;" (cons "2. *contract*: the root's signature as a shape that depends on neither side's" (cons "   names (a closed data type is the shapes of the fields of its constructors in" (cons "   order), its failure typing (a call that can fail with an overflow), how a" (cons "   side passes and returns its collections, and that the Rust text of the oracle's" (cons "   signature is what the oracle's own IR states of it;" (cons "3. *behavior*: the observable outcome (the JSON form of §17.16's harnesses) on" (cons "   every seeded input of the differentials of RB-06 and SP-11 and on the" (cons "   boundary inputs below, where an" (cons "   overflow is an overflow whichever of its errors the oracle raised, and where" (cons "   LexLean's own rendering, built and run beside the oracle's, must also print" (cons "   the interpreter's outcome;" (cons "4. *resources*: the allocations a call of the oracle's code performs, counted" (cons "   by a global allocator of the harness, against the allocation class that" (cons "   eligibility states of the root (§17.13): a root eligible for `rust-core`" (cons "   must not allocate; and that no call panics." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0275,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Seeds.** The seeded inputs of the production differentials find what a" (cons "sample finds; the places where two implementations of the same arithmetic part" (cons "(`MIN / -1` of a signed width, the arm of a match no sample built, a split into" (cons "exactly as many fields as its limit) they do not reach. The differential" (cons "therefore adds, to every eligible root, *boundary inputs* derived from the root" (cons "and from nothing of the oracle: for each parameter type, a pool of the values at" (cons "its edges (zero, one, the bounds of each integer width and their neighbours," (cons "minus one for a signed one; the empty, one-element, and several-element lists," (cons "sets, maps, strings, and byte strings; both Booleans, all three orderings; every" (cons "constructor of every option, result, and document type, each with the first" (cons "values of its fields); the numbers and strings the lowered program states" (cons "itself and their neighbours; and the bit widths of the fixed-width types it" (cons "uses and their neighbours. The inputs are the singles of each pool, a greedy" (cons "pairwise cover of the pools of the parameters (every pair of pool values of two" (cons "parameters occurs together, the earliest pool values first, up to a bound), the" (cons "parameters of one type taking the same value, and the strings of the root" (cons "joined by its delimiters into one to six fields. The interpreter, which is the" (cons "reference, gives each input its outcome, and an input it cannot finish within a" (cons "small fuel is left out. The suite audits the reach in two ways: every" (cons "constructor of every parameter type of every root is built by some input, and" (cons "this is a failing test when a bound cuts one; and the arithmetic and" (cons "control edges are *planted*: `OR-04` plants a wrapping division, negation, and" (cons "addition at the bounds, a shift past the width, a constant in a match arm, a" (cons "split limit one too small, and others in copies of the oracle's crate, each of" (cons "which the differential must detect, and each of four of which (the division at" (cons "`MIN / -1`, the saturating subtraction made wrapping, the arm of `None` with" (cons "`Err false`, the split limit) the seeded inputs of the production differentials" (cons "alone did not detect. What the seeds do not" (cons "establish is stated in the limits: they are a bounded, derived set of inputs, not" (cons "a proof that the two agree on all." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0276,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Differences.** Every way the two sides differ on a root is a difference with" (cons "a kind (`acceptance`, `contract`, `behavior`, or `resource`) and a name" (cons "(`oracle-refuses:<kind>`, `oracle-accepts:<class>`, `shape:parameter:<i>`," (cons "`shape:result`, `failure-typing:only-lexlean`, `failure-typing:only-oracle`," (cons "`borrowed-parameter`, `signature-drift`, `outcome:<lexlean kind>/<oracle kind>`," (cons "`allocation:rust-core-root`, `oracle-crate-does-not-run`, and, for a root LexLean" (cons "rejects that the oracle refuses, `both-refuse:<kind>`, which states the" (cons "oracle's reason for a verdict the two share). The *known" (cons "deliberate differences* are the rows of `oracle/known-differences.toml`" (cons "(`lexlean/oracle-differences/1`): a kind, a name, the reason the" (cons "two compilers are meant to differ there, and the exact roots on which they" (cons "do. The table and `oracle/known-non-overlap.toml` are versioned by content: each" (cons "`version` names the SHA-256 of the table's rows (canonical JSON; comments and" (cons "layout do not count), recorded in `oracle/table-versions.toml`, and the run" (cons "fails when a row changes without a new version, when a version is raised" (cons "without its digest recorded, and when a version changes nothing. A difference of the run that no row names is a *disagreement*; a row that" (cons "lists a root on which the difference does not arise is stale and refused, so" (cons "that every difference is stated, tested, and re-asserted on every run, and" (cons "neither a new difference nor a vanished one can pass unseen. Each" (cons "disagreement carries both sides' evidence: LexLean's contract, targets," (cons "effects, and outcomes, the oracle's refusal or signature and outcomes, the" (cons "inputs, and the digest of the oracle's crate." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0277,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Failure.** The oracle can say two things about a program: it generates Rust" (cons "for it, or it refuses it in its own words. Anything else is not an answer. An" (cons "oracle that was not acquired, whose checkout is not the pin, that did not" (cons "build, a tool of it that crashed, was killed, overran its time, wrote nothing" (cons "or an IR defining nothing, a generator that failed without a refusal, and a" (cons "comparison over no root or no case, are *unavailable*, and unavailable is not" (cons "validated. On the normative host (Linux x86-64) an unavailable oracle fails" (cons "the case that needed it; on any other host the case runs only its" (cons "host-independent assertions and prints `NOT VALIDATED` with the reason. A" (cons "disagreement fails closed: the case fails and prints, for every difference," (cons "the evidence of both sides. An oracle crate that does not compile is a" (cons "difference (`oracle-crate-does-not-run`) and never an omission. A generated" (cons "program that does not finish is a finding of its root, and the build of the" (cons "generated crates overrunning is unavailable, each with the bound named." (nil String)))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0278,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Renderings.** A candidate rendering of a root (a loop for a recursion, a" (cons "closed form for an iteration) is accepted when it builds against the interface" (cons "of LexLean's package of the root, prints on every input the outcome the" (cons "interpreter gives, and does no more than LexLean's own rendering by two" (cons "measures: the *work* its own runtime counts (`tick`), which is the rendering's" (cons "statement about itself, so a rendering that does not count reports none and" (cons "bounds nothing by this measure alone; and the *allocations* that an allocator" (cons "installed by the harness, out of the rendering's reach, counts over the whole" (cons "run. A rendering that does more work and neither counts it nor allocates is not" (cons "caught by either measure; that is a limit of the comparison, and not a claim it" (cons "makes. Its build and run are bounded in time like the oracle's." (nil String))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

Every" (cons "record's schema is closed, so an `oracle` field in a build manifest, a" (cons "preservation record, an attestation, a semantic snapshot, a compiler input, or" (cons "a certificate is refused." (nil String)))))))))))

(cons "They are `build` evidence that LexLean and the oracle agree where the tables" (cons "say they do, and a measurement of where they do not; they are no proof of" (cons "either." definition{

  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A compiler that consumes another system's interface needs a program that" (cons "speaks it, and that program must be as closed as every other source of the" (cons "repository: no Lean text, no template, and no name that the compiler has not" (cons "typed. A *tooling module* is that program. It is one more module form, beside" (cons "the ordinary declaration environments (§15), the native `coremodule` (§17.10)," (cons "and the `semanticmodule` (§17.11, §17.12): after the module header and title it" (cons "is exactly one `\\begin{toolingmodule}` environment containing exactly one" (cons "`\\toolingdata{...}` argument, canonical JSON of the closed schema" (cons "`lexlean/tooling-module/1` (`tooling/schemas/tooling-module.schema.json`), or the" (cons "authoring notation of the same module (§17.21.7). A module never mixes two" (cons "forms." (nil String))))))))))))

A user project, a language-1.1 project, and every" (cons "request of the public API and of the command line are refused with `LLT4013`:" (cons "no configuration member, option, or request field makes a project accept one." (cons "A tooling module has no production root, is never analysed for production" (cons "eligibility (§17.13), and appears in no semantic snapshot." (nil String)))))))

definition{

    arguments: [

The decoder reads at most 128 levels of" (cons "nesting and refuses a deeper value with `LLT4013`, so no input overflows the" (cons "stack. A member, declaration, term, signature, or atom the schema does not" (cons "state, and a member written with its default (`opaque` or `traversal` as" (cons "`false`), is refused with `LLT4013`. Every node of the module is charged to" (cons "`max_ir_nodes` (`LLS8002`), like every other module's IR." (nil String))))))))))

Binder kinds, defaults, and" (cons "  universes are parts of it, so a change of any of them is a different" (cons "  signature;" (cons "- `plumbing` names the constants Lean's elaborator inserts that no term names" (cons "  (instances, matchers, recursors, projections, and core-library data)," (cons "  sorted, distinct, and non-empty, and no constant is plumbing in two rows or" (cons "  plumbing and declared;" (cons "- every type and call carries the toolchain source file that defines it and the" (cons "  SHA-256 of that file, and a source path that leaves the toolchain source or a" (cons "  digest that is not 64 lowercase hexadecimal digits is refused." definition{
  name: i0288,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Typing of a call.** A call's signature gives its typing and nothing else" (cons "does: the implicit binders that bind a type or a type constructor, in order," (cons "are the type arguments that *every* use states, each by the callee's own name" (cons "for it; the explicit binders are its arguments, trailing ones that carry a" (cons "default being optional; an instance binder, and an implicit binder of any" (cons "other kind (the instances a structure type carries as parameters, such as the" (cons "`BEq` and `Hashable` of `Std.HashMap`), is not stated, because Lean" (cons "synthesizes or infers it. A signature that applies a registered type to more" (cons "arguments than the type has parameters, as Lean's own types do with their" (cons "instances, is read at the type's parameters. A use that gives a wrong number of type arguments or" (cons "arguments, or an argument of another type, is refused with `LLT4015`. Types" (cons "are compared exactly, after the unfolding of abbreviations." (nil String)))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0289,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Terms.** A tool is written in a closed term language in which every binder is" (cons "typed and every type argument is explicit, so that nothing depends on" (cons "inference: a variable; a natural number, string, or character literal; a name" (cons "literal, which Lean resolves when it elaborates the generated text; a call; a" (cons "constructor of a registered type; the field of a value whose type has exactly" (cons "one constructor; a list or an array with its element type; `let`; a lambda;" (cons "the application of a function value; `if` on a `Bool`; and `match`. A match" (cons "is flat, one constructor per branch, and exhaustive at its scrutinee's index:" (cons "every constructor available at that index has a branch, a constructor that" (cons "exists only at another index has none, and a final default branch may stand" (cons "for the rest. A name is bound once along any path: a function parameter, a" (cons "`let`, a lambda parameter, or a branch binder that shadows a variable in scope" (cons "is refused with `LLT4015`, so that what a name stands for is read from its one" (cons "binding. Monadic sequencing, effects, and every other operation are calls" (cons "of registered constants (`Bind.bind`, `Pure.pure`); there is no `do` and no" (cons "mutable variable." (nil String)))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0290,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Recursion.** A function may call a registered call, a function defined before" (cons "it, itself when it is declared `partial`, and the functions of its mutual group:" (cons "a run of contiguous functions with one label, every member `partial`. Any other" (cons "forward or recursive call, and a mutual group that is not one run, is refused" (cons "with `LLT4015`. `partial` is Lean's own: such a function has no termination" (cons "argument and no proof, and a tooling module claims no theorem." (nil String)))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0291,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Complete traversal.** A function declared a *traversal* is `partial`, takes" (cons "the value it traverses as its first parameter, and is exactly a `match` on it" (cons "with no default branch. The recursive family of a type is the set of" (cons "registered types that reach it and are reached from it through constructor" (cons "fields (the strongly connected component of the type graph). Every branch" (cons "uses each field whose type mentions a type of the family, and recurses into" (cons "it: it calls, for each such type, a traversal of that type in its own mutual" (cons "group (or itself) *on a value derived from the field*, which is the field, a" (cons "field of a derived value, a name a `let` binds to a term that mentions one, a" (cons "branch binder of a `match` on one, or a parameter of a lambda handed to the" (cons "same call as one (a fold over `alts` hands the elements of `alts` to its" (cons "function). A handler applied to the subject or to a constant leaves the field" (cons "unvisited even when the branch mentions it, and is refused. Every other type" (cons "of the family has a traversal in that group. A traversal therefore cannot skip" (cons "a constructor (an unmatched one is a registry disagreement), a field (a" (cons "skipped one is refused), or a type (a family member without a handler is" (cons "refused), and a registry that gains a constructor or a field makes the tool" (cons "fail to link and not silently omit it, with `LLT4015`." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0292,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Commands.** A `command` registers the syntax kind `name`, a grammar of" (cons "atoms (a token, an identifier, a type written as a registered signature, the" (cons "first atom a token) and an elaborator: a public function with no parameters" (cons "whose type is `Lean.Elab.Command.CommandElab`, the abbreviation of" (cons "`Lean.Syntax → Lean.Elab.Command.CommandElabM Unit` that Lean's" (cons "`command_elab` attribute requires by name. An `invoke` applies a registered" (cons "command to one argument per non-token atom, an identifier or a signature tree," (cons "and an `evaluate` runs a function of the module that is a command action on" (cons "terms of the right types; both are checked against the command's grammar and" (cons "the function's typing. A host builds its invocations as semantic data and" (cons "never as text." (nil String))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0293,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Backends.** Both backends traverse the one linked module. The Lean backend" (cons "emits `module`, `public meta import Lean`, one `import all` per generated" (cons "project module of the module's imports, the fixed options" (cons "`set_option autoImplicit false` and `set_option linter.unusedVariables false`," (cons "a `universe` declaration of the universe variables the signatures name, and then" (cons "one rendering per declaration in order: a function as `[public ]meta" (cons "[partial ]def`, a mutual group inside one `mutual ... end`, a command as" (cons "`syntax (name := ...)` and `attribute [command_elab ...]`, an invocation as the" (cons "command's own syntax, an evaluation as `#eval`. A type, a call, and plumbing" (cons "have no Lean of their own: they are the registry that Lean checks when it" (cons "elaborates the uses (a signature or a constructor list that the tool is" (cons "consistent with and Lean is not is refused there, never by the compiler alone)," (cons "and the rows from which the extraction probes and registry check of §22.10 are" (cons "built. Every type argument is named by the callee's own" (cons "binder name, every constructor in a pattern is written after the dot, and a" (cons "reserved word is quoted, so that the text means exactly the node. The text" (cons "contains no comment. The LaTeX backend emits one entry per declaration with its" (cons "kind and the same types, signatures, and terms spelled by the same printer." (cons "Each declaration that has output has one mapped piece for its own object of the" (cons "source, the rest of the output belongs to the module, and output coverage is" (cons "complete (§19.6, §20.5). The pinned Lean elaborates the generated fixtures" (cons "without a message." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0294,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "**Fixtures.** `tests/tooling/core`, `tests/tooling/commands`, and" (cons "`tests/tooling/traversal` are committed tooling projects: the term language," (cons "recursion, and mutual groups over `Nat`, `List`, `Option`, `Prod`, and `Array`;" (cons "a command with its elaborator, invocation, and evaluation, calls with trailing" (cons "defaults and the empty structure instance, and the abbreviation `CommandElab`;" (cons "and a mutual traversal of the real `Lean.Compiler.LCNF.Code` family with its" (cons "`Purity` index. Their registry rows are the pinned Lean's own (constructor" (cons "lists, signatures, index requirements, and the toolchain source file and" (cons "digest that define each), and the constants Lean's elaboration uses beyond the" (cons "declared ones are their `plumbing`. A fixture commits its published build tree" (cons "under `expected/build/`, which `cargo xtask check-golden` compares and" (cons "`cargo xtask check-reproducibility` rebuilds in two directories (§28.3," (cons "§28.4); `check-golden --write` is the only writer. A project that holds a" (cons "tooling module is never built through the command line, so these gates render" (cons "it with tooling enabled." (nil String))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0299,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "Under language 1.2, the body of `\\begin{semanticmodule}` … `\\end{semanticmodule}`" (cons "is the notation: the verbatim text of its declarations, one after the other, to" (cons "the closing `\\end{semanticmodule}`. A `toolingmodule` (§17.21.7) and a" (cons "`coremodule` (§17.21.8) are written in the same way. The data form of §17.11," (cons "one `\\semanticdata{…}` (`\\toolingdata{…}`, `\\coredata{…}`) argument of canonical" (cons "JSON on one line, is the form of languages 1.0 and 1.1, which have it only; the" (cons "notation under language 1.1 is refused with `LLP2003`, and so is the data form" (cons "under language 1.2. The refusal is raised at link, where the project's" (cons "language is known and before the payload is read, at the position of the data" (cons "argument, and its message names the control and the authoring notation; the" (cons "module is not decoded, and `fmt` neither rewrites it nor reports anything but" (cons "the refusal. `LLP2003` carries it because it is the refusal the notation draws" (cons "under language 1.1, mirrored: a structural alternative the language does not" (cons "admit. The conversion between the forms is `lexlean::sdk::to_notation` and" (cons "`lexlean::sdk::to_data_form` (§24.8), an explicit conversion of a text and" (cons "not a source; the Atlas (language 1.0) is still written as `\\coredata`. Every" (cons "language-1.2 source of this repository that has a semantic, tooling or core" (cons "module is written in the notation (`AU-04`), and a language-1.2 source in the" (cons "data form is refused (`AU-13`). The text is the module's source" (cons "bytes between the two markers: it is not scanned for prose, glossary entries," (cons "or comments, every atom of it is covered as metadata owned by" (cons "`lexlean.core::semanticsource` (§20), and it writes a backslash only inside a" (cons "string, where it is the escape `\\\\` (so the first `\\end` control atom is the" (cons "environment's end). A numeral with a redundant leading zero outside a string" (cons "is `LLL1003` (§12.4); inside a string it is string data." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

The canonical source of a module is the" (cons "module with its body replaced by that text, and `fmt` writes it (§23.5); it is" (cons "a function of the module alone, so no layout of the source survives it and" (cons "`fmt` is idempotent. definition{
  name: i0322,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "- A malformed text is `LLP2001` at the position of the offending token, a" (cons "  notation under language 1.1 is `LLP2003`, and nothing in it panics: the" (cons "  notation is read with an explicit nesting bound and without recursion over" (cons "  its length." (cons "- **Round trip (`AU-01`).** For every value of the schema, and in particular for" (cons "  every alternative of the declaration, term, type, and proof unions read from" (cons "  the schema, with and without its optional members, and for every committed" (cons "  semantic module, `read(write(v)) = v` and `write(read(write(v))) = write(v)`." (cons "  The samples are read from the schema, so a variant added to it is checked" (cons "  the day it is added." (cons "- **The notation as source (`AU-02`).** A module in the notation builds, and its" (cons "  source maps relate each declaration to its text in the notation; `fmt` is the" (cons "  identity on the notation and restores its canonical layout from any other." (cons "  The data form of the same module is not a language-1.2 source (`AU-13`), so it" (cons "  is no second build to compare with: the SDK's two conversions (§24.8) are" (cons "  exact inverses over every committed module (`AU-08`, `AU-13`)." (cons "- **Failing closed (`AU-03`).** Every single-token deletion, duplication, and" (cons "  replacement of a committed module either fails with a registered code or links" (cons "  to a module that decodes." (cons "- **Tooling modules (`AU-05`).** The same laws for a tooling module: read and" (cons "  written back equal for every committed one, rendering with a source map that" (cons "  names the notation, `fmt` the identity on the notation, and every" (cons "  single-token deletion and duplication refused with a position or decoded." (cons "- **Core modules (`AU-09`..`AU-12`).** §17.21.8: the notation of a" (cons "  core module reads back to the module for every committed one and for a module" (cons "  that carries every kind of node, level, annotation, and declaration member;" (cons "  it builds, with a source map that names the notation, and `fmt` is the" (cons "  identity on the notation and restores its layout; and malformed text, with every single-token deletion and" (cons "  duplication of a committed module, is refused with a position or decoded;" (cons "  and a module written with names for its variables and its statements reads to" (cons "  the module its canonical notation is, down to a theorem added to the" (cons "  certification library by name." (cons "- **One identity (`AU-06`).** The compiler-semantics ID is the same for the" (cons "  embedded tree whether its modules are written in the notation or as data" (cons "  (§21.2), and changes with a module's meaning in either form." (cons "- **No other authoring path (`AU-07`).** The writers of the repository (the" (cons "  generators `cargo xtask check-calculus` compares and rewrites) emit no" (cons "  `.lex.tex` source of `compiler/`, `language/`, or `examples/` but the five" (cons "  named records: the fixture modules `TargetFixtures`, `ReasoningFixtures`," (cons "  `GnafFixtures`, and `GnafCertificates`, whose input is committed data (the" (cons "  fixtures, requests, and certificates, under `compiler/`), and the extraction" (cons "  adapter's module, whose input is the registry the pinned Lean answers" (cons "  (§22.10). Every other source is written and edited as source: the calculus" (cons "  and the machine (§17.14), the GNAF and certification models (§17.15, §17.18)," (cons "  the examples, and the libraries." (cons "- **The authoring API (`AU-08`).** §24.8." (cons "- **One authoring surface (`AU-04`).** Every committed source of the repository" (cons "  that has a semantic, tooling, or core module is read for its language and its" (cons "  form. A" (cons "  language-1.2 source of the product (the compiler, the language libraries, the" (cons "  examples, the positive fixtures) is written in the notation and is its own" (cons "  canonical source. The data form remains under language 1.0 and 1.1, which" (cons "  have it only, and in the refused fixtures of `tests/negative` whose payload" (cons "  the notation cannot spell, because they test the data form's own refusal; a" (cons "  refused fixture whose payload the notation can spell, written and read back" (cons "  equal, is a defect, so the data form cannot become a second way to write a" (cons "  module unnoticed. A language-1.2 source in the data form is refused" (cons "  (`AU-13`), so the one refused fixture of that kind is a fixture of that" (cons "  refusal." (cons "- **The data form refused (`AU-13`).** §17.21.1: under language 1.2 each of the" (nil String)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },

}

  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A tooling module (§17.20) is a typed registry and typed functions over it, so" (cons "its notation has no readable forms of its own: it is the generic notation" (cons "(§17.21.3), which is exact for every value, of the one object the module is," (cons "without its `spec` member, which the language implies (`lexlean/tooling-module/1`" (cons "under language 1.2; the notation under any other language is `LLT4013`, like" (cons "the module). The body of `\\begin{toolingmodule}` is that object laid out as in" (cons "§17.21.5 and ended by a line feed, or one `\\toolingdata{…}` argument. A" (cons "declaration is a `kind`-tagged object (`type{…}`, `call{…}`, `function{…}`," (cons "`command{…}`, `invoke{…}`, `evaluate{…}`, `plumbing{…}`), and what each member" (cons "means is decided by the decoder that reads the canonical JSON the notation is" (cons "read to, so a member the schema does not state is refused with `LLT4013` exactly" (cons "as in the data form. A malformed text is `LLP2001` at its position; the notation" (cons "is scanned as the semantic notation is (§17.21.1), and its metadata rows are" (cons "owned by `lexlean.core::toolingsource`. The source map of a module in the" (cons "notation relates each declaration to its own text, as the semantic map does." (cons "A language-1.2 tooling module written as `\\toolingdata` is refused (§17.21.1);" (cons "`lexlean::sdk::to_notation` is how its text leaves the data form." (nil String))))))))))))))))))

definition{
  name: i0329,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      "ebnf",
      (cons "module      = \"imports\" \"[\" [ name { \",\" name } ] \"]\" { declaration } { statement } ;" (cons "declaration = kind name [ \".{\" name { \",\" name } \"}\" ] [ \":\" ref [ \":=\" ref ] ] [ members ] ;" (cons "kind        = \"abbrev\" | \"definition\" | \"theorem\" | \"inductive\" | \"constructor\" | \"recursor\" ;" (cons "members     = object of the generic notation (§17.21.3) ;" (cons "statement   = ref \":=\" term ;" (cons "ref         = \"@\" ( numeral | name ) ;" (cons "" (cons "term     = \"fun\" binder \"=>\" term" (cons "         | ( \"let\" | \"have\" ) binder-name \":\" term \":=\" term \";\" term" (cons "         | binder \"->\" term" (cons "         | head [ \"->\" term ] ;" (cons "head     = \"Sort\" level | \"proj\" name name numeral atom" (cons "         | \"annot\" \"[\" [ entry { \",\" entry } ] \"]\" atom" (cons "         | atom { atom } ;" (cons "atom     = ref | \"#\" numeral | name [ \".{\" level { \",\" level } \"}\" ]" (cons "         | \"const\" string [ \".{\" level { \",\" level } \"}\" ]" (cons "         | numeral | \"nat\" string | string | \"proof\" \"(\" term \")\" | \"(\" term \")\" ;" (cons "binder   = \"(\" binder-name \":\" term \")\" | \"{\" binder-name \":\" term \"}\"" (cons "         | \"{{\" binder-name \":\" term \"}}\" | \"[\" binder-name \":\" term \"]\" ;" (cons "level    = ( numeral | name | \"max\" \"(\" level \",\" level \")\"" (cons "           | \"imax\" \"(\" level \",\" level \")\" | \"(\" level \")\" ) { \"+\" numeral } ;" (cons "entry    = name \":=\" ( \"bool\" ( \"true\" | \"false\" ) | \"nat\" digits | \"int\" digits" (cons "                     | \"str\" string | \"name\" name ) ;" (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],

definition{
  name: i0330,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A *name* is a word of letters, digits, `_ ' ! ? . @ #`, and any scalar outside" (cons "ASCII that is not white space, beginning with a letter, `_`, or a scalar" (cons "outside ASCII, not ending in `.`, not a keyword (`fun let have proof Sort proj" (cons "annot const max imax true false bool nat int str name`), and holding no" (cons "numeral with a redundant leading zero (§12.4); any other name is written as a" (cons "string, and a constant that is written as a string is written `const \"…\"`" (cons "because a string alone is a literal. A binder's empty name is `_`. White space" (cons "is the space and the line feed. Every declaration and every statement begins" (cons "at the start of a line, and no other line of the module does, so that a term" (cons "runs to the next line that begins at the margin." (nil String)))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0331,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "*The nodes.* A statement `@N := term` defines the node `N`: the nodes of a" (cons "module are numbered in the order they finish being read, a node written inside" (cons "a term is numbered before the node of the term that holds it, and the label" (cons "only states the number, which the reader checks. `@M` names the node `M`, which" (cons "is earlier. A node is written inside the node that uses it, and has no" (cons "statement, exactly when it is used once (a declaration uses the nodes it" (cons "states), as a child of the node that comes right after it and its other written-inside children (so that" (cons "the numbering of the text is the numbering of the module), and the nodes inside" (cons "it are fewer than 48 deep; every other node is a statement. Which nodes those" (cons "are is a function of the module, so every module has one notation. An" (cons "application `f a b` is the node of `a` applied to the node of `f a` applied to" (cons "`b`, the nodes of `f a` written inside it; the arrow `A -> B` is the dependent" (cons "function type with the empty binder name and explicit binder, and the others" (cons "write the binder; `let` and `have` are the two values of the node's `d`;" (cons "`proof(…)` states that the node it holds is one of the module's `proof_nodes`," (cons "which are therefore in increasing order. A universe level is `0`, a name, `max`" (cons "or `imax` of two levels, or one of them raised by `+n`, and a numeral `n` is the" (cons "level `n`. A natural-number literal that is not the canonical spelling of its" (cons "number, which the schema allows, is written `nat \"…\"`, and so is the value of" (cons "an annotation entry (`int \"…\"` for an integer); a string literal that holds a" (cons "value that is not a Unicode scalar has no notation." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0332,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "*Names.* A name in a term that is the name of a binder the term is under is" (cons "the variable of the nearest such binder (the body of an arrow is under a" (cons "binder that has no name, and `_` is no name), and any other name is a" (cons "constant. The printer writes a variable by the name of its binder exactly when" (cons "that is a bare word and no nearer binder of the statement has it, and" (cons "otherwise by its index `#i`; a constant a binder in scope is named like is" (cons "written `const \"…\"`; a variable free in a statement, or one whose binder is in" (cons "another statement because the body is a shared node, is written by its index." (cons "A statement may be written `@name := term` and named `@name` where its node is" (cons "used, and a declaration may name a statement that follows it; a name names one" (cons "statement, an unknown name, a repeated name, and a variable given universe" (cons "levels are errors, and `fmt` writes the numbers. A module written with names" (cons "and one written with numbers read to one module, and the canonical notation" (cons "keeps the names of the binders and writes the numbers of the statements, so" (cons "that a person adds a theorem to a library by writing a declaration and the" (cons "statements of its type and value, naming what they mean and counting nothing" (cons "(`AU-12`). The notation does not infer: every argument and every universe level" (cons "of a constant is written, as a kernel term has them (§17.10)." (nil String)))))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0333,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "*The declarations.* A declaration without `:` and a type is a derived row" (cons "(§17.10): it has no universe parameters, type, or value, and its members say" (cons "`derived: true`. The other members of a declaration (its transparency," (cons "policy, reducibility, class and generated flags, the members of version 2, and" (cons "every node it states other than its `type` and `value`) are the generic notation" (cons "of the object that has them, so that no member is unspellable and a member added" (cons "to the schema has a notation the day it is added. A declaration states its roots" (cons "by `@`, so that each is a statement." (nil String)))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: i0334,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The text is laid out at 100 columns: a statement is on one line when it fits," (cons "and when it does not an application puts each argument on a line of its own," (cons "indented, and a binder form puts its body on the next line. The canonical" (cons "source of a module is the module with its body replaced by that text, as" (cons "§17.21.5. A malformed text is `LLP2001` at its position; a notation read at a" (cons "depth beyond 256, a universe level raised by more than 1024 at once, a label" (cons "that is not the number of its node, a forward reference to a node, and a" (cons "statement that defines no node are errors, and nothing panics. The notation is" (cons "scanned as the semantic notation is (§17.21.1), and its metadata rows are owned" (cons "by `lexlean.core::coresource`; the source map of a module in the notation" (cons "relates the module's pieces to its text. A language-1.2 core module written as" (cons "`\\coredata` is refused (§17.21.1); `lexlean::sdk::to_notation` is how its text" (cons "leaves the data form." (nil String))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

It is" (cons "not an example: it passes the gates of §28.6 as the compiler project does, its" (cons "production export (§24.7) is committed under `tests/production-export/stdlib/`" (cons "and compared by `cargo xtask check-production-export`, and a member is" (cons "validated by a differential before it is a member. definition{
  name: i0340,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The second module, `Sha256`, is FIPS 180-4 SHA-256 (#126, stage 1), stated" (cons "over the closed primitive vocabulary of §17.12 and nothing else: addition" (cons "modulo 2^32 is `checked_add` of the two operands widened to `UInt64` by" (cons "`checked_convert`, narrowed by `bit_and` and `checked_convert`; a rotation is" (cons "two shifts and `bit_or`; the message is never padded as bytes, every word of" (cons "every block is read by position (`index` below the length, `0x80` at it, zero" (cons "beyond it, and the two length words from `quotient` and `remainder` of the" (cons "bit length); the schedule is a sixteen-word window advanced each round; the" (cons "sixty-four rounds are `iterate` over a record of the working variables, the" (cons "window, and the round number, whose constant is a balanced search by `<?`;" (cons "and the digest is the record `Digest` of eight `UInt32` or, for `sha256Hex`," (cons "its lowercase hexadecimal as `Bytes`, each nibble a balanced test of its bits" (cons "by `bit_and` and `equal`. Both lookups are balanced rather than linear because" (cons "a rendered package's program is canonical JSON read under a fixed nesting" (cons "bound (§17.16), and a linear chain of sixty-four is past it. Its roots and oracles:" (nil String))))))))))))))))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,

definition{
  name: i0342,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A disagreement with an oracle fails the case (`SL-02`) and is a defect; it is" (cons "never resolved by adopting the oracle's digest. **The compiler's digest (`SL-05`, #126 stage 2).** Every content identity of" (cons "§21 is computed by the rendered `rust-std` package of `sha256`, and no other" (cons "implementation of SHA-256 is part of the compiler: the shipped crate links the" (cons "committed export (`tests/production-export/stdlib/packages/rust-std/sha256/src/lib.rs`)" (cons "through the in-crate link `crates/lexlean/sha256_package.rs`, which `cargo" (cons "package` dereferences and `audit-shipped` requires to resolve to that file," (cons "and its manifest has no `sha2` dependency. The crate is a dependency of the" (cons "product's identities, not of their inputs, which is why it does not recurse on" (cons "itself: the file contains no digest of anything (the digests that describe the" (cons "package, its file hashes, program identity, and source identity, are in its" (cons "`provenance.json`, beside it), the compiler is built from the committed file" (cons "and not from a build of the project, and the package is not part of any" (cons "compiler-semantics ID (§21.2), exactly as the `sha2` crate was not. What holds" (cons "the function instead is the case of `SL-05` (the authorities, the oracle, and" (cons "the planted defects) and the byte-identity of every committed identity." (cons "Regenerating the export is two steps for that reason: the export is rewritten" (cons "by the compiler, the compiler is rebuilt from the rewritten file, and the" (cons "export is checked again. Of the compression function, the standard's" (cons "authorities (the rows `NIST-FIPS-180-4`, `NIST-SHA256-EXAMPLES`, `NIST-FIPS-180-2`," (cons "and `NIST-CAVP-SHA256-SHORTMSG` and `-LONGMSG` of `model/authorities.toml`, level" (cons "`some-true`) establish the digest and the Lean certificates of the package" (cons "establish its program; `sha2` falsifies and establishes nothing. The Rust" (cons "runtime text the package embeds (§17.16) is the declared Rust machine's, as it" (cons "is for every production package, so what remains outside LexLean source under" (cons "the digest is that text and the framing of §21.1, which is Rust until it is" (cons "authored in LexLean (#92). A message of 2^61 bytes or more is outside the" (cons "domain of FIPS 180-4 and is not allocatable on any supported host, so the" (cons "package's `Overflow` for it is a host impossibility and not a public failure." definition{
  name: i0353,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "`lex` reads the bytes of valid UTF-8 Lean source into a list of `Lexeme`" (cons "structures, each carrying its `kind` (`Ident` of a list of `Segment`s with" (cons "their unquoted `text` and whether each was written `«…»`, `Number`, `Literal`," (cons "`Command` of its `#`-prefixed word, or `Symbol` of the UTF-8 bytes of its" (cons "character), its zero-based `line`, whether it is `firstOnLine`, and its `start`" (cons "offset in Unicode scalars of the text: line and nested block comments are" (cons "dropped; string, raw string (`r\"…\"`, `r#\"…\"#`), and character literals are read" (cons "as `Literal`; numbers are read as the pinned Lean's `numberFnAux` reads them," (cons "with `0x`, `0b`, and `0o` radices, decimal fractions, exponents, `_` digit" (cons "separators, and tuple field projections after `.`, refusing ambiguous field" (cons "continuations (`p.1_0`, `p.1e5`) and bare dots before identifiers (`1.foo`);" (cons " identifiers are bounded by `isIdFirst` and `isIdRest` (`Lean.isLetterLike` and" (cons "subscripts included) and joined across `.` only when the next component begins" (cons "with `isIdFirst` or `«`; and an unclosed comment, string, raw string, character" (cons "literal, or quoted name, a quoted name whose body is empty or contains a" (cons "character outside `isIdRest` and `.`, an interpolated string (`s!\"…\"`), or a" (cons "control character (`U+0000`..`U+001F`, `U+007F`, `U+0080`..`U+009F`) is" (cons "refused with the oracle's exact message. `auditTokens` runs `lex` and checks" (cons "the token stream against the certificate environment: any `Command` is refused;" (cons "any identifier component equal to a forbidden constant (`ofReduceBool`," (cons "`ofReduceNat`, `sorryAx`), quoted or not, is refused with its dotted name; any" (cons "single-segment identifier equal to one of the 27 forbidden tokens is refused" (cons "unless it is quoted outside an `@[…]` or `attribute […]` bracket list; every" (cons "`set_option` must name one of the six allowed options as a dotted identifier;" (cons "and every `import` must name a dotted identifier present in `imports`." (nil String))))))))))))))))))))))))))
    ],

    type_arguments: []
  },

}

It refuses with" (cons "`LLL1004` a backslash that is followed by a non-ASCII scalar or by the end of the" (cons "text, with `LLL1001` an ASCII control that no class covers, and with `LLS8002`" (cons "the atom that exceeds `maxAtoms`. `rejectAtoms` transcribes" (cons "`reject_forbidden_atoms` and its payload tracking one to one: an always-forbidden" (cons "control (`LLL1002`), and a numeral with a redundant leading zero (`LLL1003`, with" (cons "its help) that does not continue identifier material and is not inside a payload" (cons "string, where the payload of a `\\semanticdata`, `\\toolingdata`, or `\\coredata`" (cons "argument and the body of a `semanticmodule`, `toolingmodule`, or `coremodule`" (cons "environment are tracked by their quotes, escapes, and braces. The record measures these inputs on" (cons "the host that wrote it. It is evidence at level `build` for the stacks the" (cons "suite gives, and it is not a stack bound for any program, input, or host: a" (cons "package states `recursion` and no more (§17.16), and the host that runs it" (cons "still gives the call a stack its depth fits." definition{
  name: i0363,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "The roots take a program as calculus data and not as its canonical JSON: reading a program (`Program::parse`, `canonical`, `load`) belongs to the front end (step 7 of the plan) and is not part of the row. `Ty`, `Value`, and `Expr` are three inductives that mirror `Ty`, `Value`, and `Expr` of `calculus/mod.rs`, constructor for constructor and in order, with `Shape`, `Prim`, `IntKind`, and the ordering value; a list of a recursive type is a *chain* of the type's own `Nil` and `Cons` constructors (the `Fn` parameters and the constructors, fields, and parameter types of a declaration are chains of `Ty`; a literal's items and fields chains of `Value`; operands, captures, and arms chains of `Expr`, whose `Arm` is a constructor of it). The chain is the list: `adts` is the chain of the declarations, each the chain of its constructors, each the chain of its field types; `types` the chain of the functions' parameter-type chains, `results` the chain of their result types, `bodies` the chain of their bodies, and `parameters` their parameter names, a `List (List Nat)`. A result is `Result.ok ()` or `Result.error` of the oracle's reason as UTF-8 bytes. The chains are not a style: certificate A (§17.17) cannot take as a root parameter, nor build in a definition, a `List` of a recursive type, and its commands cut a nested recursive group's rows in a large closure (issue 208); the case therefore decodes the arguments of every vector back to the program the oracle read (`list ↔ chain`, on the 175 committed programs and on every vector) and compares it with it. The oracle's reasons are reproduced to the byte, including the `{:?}` spelling of types, shapes, primitives, and literals, and the base-ten spelling of an index, a count, and an integer bound by decimal comparison; the one part they do not reproduce is Rust's `Debug` for a `str`, which escapes by the Unicode tables of `char::is_printable` and `Grapheme_Extend`: a string literal spelled in a reason is escaped as Rust escapes ASCII and `U+0080`..`U+009F` and copied for every other scalar, so a reason that spells a scalar beyond `U+009F` as `\\u{…}` (such as `U+00A0` or a combining mark) is outside the claim, and the corpora leave those runs out (the fixed point, step 7, replaces the oracle's escaping by a closed escaping on both sides)." (nil String))
    ],
    constructor: DocRows::Item.para,
    type_arguments: []
  },
  executable: true
}

On every host the rendered `rust-std` packages of both roots, compiled by the pinned rustc, run the 1,756 vectors of `checkProgram` (1,483 refused) and the 303 of `checkArguments` (129 refused): the 175 distinct programs committed in the repository, each read by the oracle's reader (the fixtures of `compiler/fixtures/`, the programs of the committed Rust packages, and the lowered programs a verification record preserves, among them the two roots' own), the refusals of `TC-02` as the edits that test makes, one program for each rule a planted defect removes, and the literal, primitive, and shape corpora at a fixed seed; the arguments are those of the committed fixtures, as stated and mutated. On the normative host the lowered programs of both roots under the calculus evaluator (§17.14) agree on the same vectors and on 4,722 seeded programs (3,933 refused) and 403 seeded argument runs (202 refused), mutations at the level of the data (a retyped operand, a dropped, duplicated, or reshaped arm, a changed binder, an undeclared function or declaration, a closure over every parameter, an operand dropped or added, each primitive at wrong operand types, a literal respelled or out of range, a specification changed), the rendered packages agree on 9,296 more under rustc, in harnesses of at most 1,000 runs, and twelve planted defects in the source are each detected by the same differential. Under the pinned Lean, the production export (§24.7) certifies both roots beside those of the other components under `tests/production-export/bootstrap/`." (nil String))

definition{
  name: i0367,
  parameters: [],
  result: DocRows::Item,
  body: constructor{
    arguments: [
      (cons "A `Crate` is the closed Rust AST (`calculus::rust::ast`) as first-order data" (cons "(§17.13 rule 1): whether the profile is `rust-core`, and the items as a chain," (cons "each sort carrying its lists as its own `Nil`/`Cons` chain. A literal is" (cons "read for its kind and width and a runtime function for its row of the table" (cons "the module transcribes from `runtime::Item` (path, failure, heap use, width)," (cons "so the payload of a literal is not data. `Result.error` carries the oracle's" (cons "message to the byte, including the `{:?}` of a `Type`, of an `Option<IntKind>`," (cons "and of a slice of strings. `validateCrate` checks, in the order of the oracle," (cons "that each enum, `apply`, and function is declared once, that every type a crate" (cons "names is declared and, in `rust-core`, has no heap component (and no heap" (cons "construct or runtime function appears), that every name is bound once per" (cons "function and in scope, that a value is moved at most once on every path" (cons "(including the components of a pair, and the alternatives of an `if` and a" (cons "`match`), that a fallible call propagates exactly where its function is" (cons "fallible, and that each `apply` arm states the failure of the function it calls."
